```ts
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { generateJwt } from "@coinbase/cdp-sdk/auth";
import { createCoinbaseClient, mapOrderStatus, mapPermissions, mapPreview, mapSubmit } from "@/lib/exchange/coinbase";
import { fetchCandles, fetchTicker, mapCandles, mapTicker } from "@/lib/exchange/coinbase-public";
import type { FetchLike } from "@/lib/exchange/json";

vi.mock("@coinbase/cdp-sdk/auth", () => ({ generateJwt: vi.fn(async () => "test.jwt.token") }));

type Call = { url: string; init: RequestInit | undefined };

function fakeFetch(body: unknown, status = 200): { fetchImpl: FetchLike; calls: Call[] } {
  const calls: Call[] = [];
  const fetchImpl: FetchLike = async (url, init) => {
    calls.push({ url, init });
    return new Response(typeof body === "string" ? body : JSON.stringify(body), { status });
  };
  return { fetchImpl, calls };
}

const candlesJson = {
  candles: [
    { start: "1760011200", low: "99", high: "101", open: "100", close: "100.5", volume: "12.5" },
    { start: "1760007600", low: "98", high: "100", open: "99", close: "100", volume: "10" }
  ]
};

const tickerJson = {
  trades: [
    { trade_id: "1", product_id: "BTC-USD", price: "100.25", size: "0.1", time: "2026-10-09T11:59:58.123Z", side: "BUY", bid: "", ask: "" }
  ],
  best_bid: "100.20",
  best_ask: "100.30"
};

describe("mapCandles", () => {
  it("parses strings and sorts oldest first", () => {
    expect(mapCandles(candlesJson)).toEqual([
      { start: 1760007600, open: 99, high: 100, low: 98, close: 100, volume: 10 },
      { start: 1760011200, open: 100, high: 101, low: 99, close: 100.5, volume: 12.5 }
    ]);
  });
  it("returns [] for a malformed body", () => {
    expect(mapCandles({ nope: true })).toEqual([]);
  });
});

describe("fetchCandles", () => {
  it("calls the public candles endpoint with a window sized to the limit", async () => {
    const { fetchImpl, calls } = fakeFetch(candlesJson);
    expect(await fetchCandles("BTC-USD", "ONE_HOUR", 120, fetchImpl)).toHaveLength(2);
    const url = new URL(calls[0]!.url);
    expect(`${url.origin}${url.pathname}`).toBe("https://api.coinbase.com/api/v3/brokerage/market/products/BTC-USD/candles");
    expect(url.searchParams.get("granularity")).toBe("ONE_HOUR");
    expect(url.searchParams.get("limit")).toBe("120");
    expect(Number(url.searchParams.get("end")) - Number(url.searchParams.get("start"))).toBe(120 * 3600);
  });
  it("throws with the status on a non-2xx", async () => {
    const { fetchImpl } = fakeFetch("rate limited", 429);
    await expect(fetchCandles("BTC-USD", "ONE_HOUR", 120, fetchImpl)).rejects.toThrow("Coinbase market data request failed (429)");
  });
});

describe("mapTicker / fetchTicker", () => {
  it("takes the last trade price and the best bid/ask", () => {
    expect(mapTicker("BTC-USD", tickerJson)).toEqual({
      productId: "BTC-USD",
      price: 100.25,
      bestBid: 100.2,
      bestAsk: 100.3,
      tradeTime: Date.parse("2026-10-09T11:59:58.123Z")
    });
  });
  it("throws when there is no trade", () => {
    expect(() => mapTicker("BTC-USD", { trades: [] })).toThrow(/no recent trade/);
  });
  it("requests limit=1", async () => {
    const { fetchImpl, calls } = fakeFetch(tickerJson);
    await fetchTicker("ETH-USD", fetchImpl);
    expect(calls[0]!.url).toBe("https://api.coinbase.com/api/v3/brokerage/market/products/ETH-USD/ticker?limit=1");
  });
});

describe("authenticated response mapping", () => {
  it("maps key permissions and fails closed when can_transfer is missing", () => {
    expect(mapPermissions({ can_view: true, can_trade: true, can_transfer: false, portfolio_uuid: "pf" })).toEqual({
      canView: true,
      canTrade: true,
      canTransfer: false,
      portfolioUuid: "pf"
    });
    expect(mapPermissions({ can_view: true, can_trade: true }).canTransfer).toBe(true);
  });
  it("maps a preview and keeps warnings", () => {
    expect(
      mapPreview({
        order_total: "101.00",
        commission_total: "1.00",
        errs: [],
        warning: ["BIG_ORDER"],
        best_bid: "99990.00",
        best_ask: "100010.00",
        preview_id: "pv-1"
      })
    ).toEqual({ previewId: "pv-1", orderTotal: 101, commissionTotal: 1, bestBid: 99990, bestAsk: 100010, warnings: ["BIG_ORDER"] });
  });
  it("throws when the preview carries errors", () => {
    expect(() => mapPreview({ errs: ["PREVIEW_INSUFFICIENT_FUND"] })).toThrow(/PREVIEW_INSUFFICIENT_FUND/);
  });
  it("maps submit success and failure", () => {
    expect(mapSubmit({ success: true, success_response: { order_id: "o1", client_order_id: "c1" } }, "c1")).toEqual({
      success: true,
      orderId: "o1",
      clientOrderId: "c1",
      detail: "Coinbase accepted order o1."
    });
    expect(
      mapSubmit(
        {
          success: false,
          error_response: {
            error: "INSUFFICIENT_FUND",
            message: "Insufficient balance in source account",
            preview_failure_reason: "PREVIEW_INSUFFICIENT_FUND"
          }
        },
        "c2"
      )
    ).toEqual({
      success: false,
      orderId: "",
      clientOrderId: "c2",
      detail: "Insufficient balance in source account (PREVIEW_INSUFFICIENT_FUND)"
    });
  });
  it("maps order statuses", () => {
    expect(
      mapOrderStatus({
        order: { order_id: "o1", status: "FILLED", filled_size: "0.001", average_filled_price: "100000", total_fees: "0.6" }
      })
    ).toEqual({ orderId: "o1", status: "FILLED", filledSize: 0.001, averagePrice: 100000, totalFees: 0.6, raw: "FILLED" });
    expect(mapOrderStatus({ order: { order_id: "o2", status: "QUEUED" } }).status).toBe("PENDING");
    expect(mapOrderStatus({ order: { order_id: "o3", status: "SOMETHING_NEW" } }).status).toBe("UNKNOWN");
  });
});

describe("createCoinbaseClient", () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    vi.mocked(generateJwt).mockClear();
  });

  it("signs each request for its method and path and previews a market IOC order", async () => {
    const { fetchImpl, calls } = fakeFetch({
      order_total: "100.60",
      commission_total: "0.60",
      errs: [],
      best_bid: "1",
      best_ask: "2",
      preview_id: "pv"
    });
    const preview = await createCoinbaseClient({ keyId: "kid", secret: "secret" }, fetchImpl).previewOrder({
      productId: "BTC-USD",
      side: "BUY",
      quoteUsd: 100
    });
    expect(preview.previewId).toBe("pv");
    expect(generateJwt).toHaveBeenCalledWith({
      apiKeyId: "kid",
      apiKeySecret: "secret",
      requestMethod: "POST",
      requestHost: "api.coinbase.com",
      requestPath: "/api/v3/brokerage/orders/preview"
    });
    expect(calls[0]!.url).toBe("https://api.coinbase.com/api/v3/brokerage/orders/preview");
    expect(calls[0]!.init?.method).toBe("POST");
    expect((calls[0]!.init?.headers as Record<string, string>).Authorization).toBe("Bearer test.jwt.token");
    expect(JSON.parse(String(calls[0]!.init?.body))).toEqual({
      product_id: "BTC-USD",
      side: "BUY",
      order_configuration: { market_market_ioc: { quote_size: "100.00" } }
    });
  });

  it("sends the client order id and preview id on create", async () => {
    const { fetchImpl, calls } = fakeFetch({ success: true, success_response: { order_id: "o1", client_order_id: "c1" } });
    const result = await createCoinbaseClient({ keyId: "kid", secret: "secret" }, fetchImpl).createOrder({
      productId: "ETH-USD",
      side: "SELL",
      quoteUsd: 12.5,
      clientOrderId: "c1",
      previewId: "pv"
    });
    expect(result.orderId).toBe("o1");
    expect(JSON.parse(String(calls[0]!.init?.body))).toEqual({
      client_order_id: "c1",
      product_id: "ETH-USD",
      side: "SELL",
      preview_id: "pv",
      order_configuration: { market_market_ioc: { quote_size: "12.50" } }
    });
  });

  it("reads an order by id", async () => {
    const { fetchImpl, calls } = fakeFetch({ order: { order_id: "o1", status: "OPEN" } });
    const status = await createCoinbaseClient({ keyId: "kid", secret: "secret" }, fetchImpl).getOrder("o1");
    expect(status.status).toBe("OPEN");
    expect(calls[0]!.url).toBe("https://api.coinbase.com/api/v3/brokerage/orders/historical/o1");
    expect(calls[0]!.init?.method).toBe("GET");
  });

  it("throws with status and body on a non-2xx", async () => {
    const { fetchImpl } = fakeFetch("nope", 401);
    await expect(createCoinbaseClient({ keyId: "kid", secret: "secret" }, fetchImpl).keyPermissions()).rejects.toThrow(
      "Coinbase request failed (401): nope"
    );
  });
});
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { createMockClient } from "@/lib/exchange/mock";

describe("createMockClient", () => {
  const client = createMockClient(async () => 200);

  it("is trade-only and never transfer-capable", async () => {
    expect(await client.keyPermissions()).toEqual({ canView: true, canTrade: true, canTransfer: false, portfolioUuid: null });
  });

  it("previews at the reference price with a 0.6% commission and a warning", async () => {
    const preview = await client.previewOrder({ productId: "BTC-USD", side: "BUY", quoteUsd: 100 });
    expect(preview).toMatchObject({ orderTotal: 100.6, commissionTotal: 0.6, warnings: ["Mock provider: no live order will be sent"] });
    expect(preview.bestBid).toBeLessThan(200);
    expect(preview.bestAsk).toBeGreaterThan(200);
  });

  it("accepts orders and reports them filled at the reference price", async () => {
    const submitted = await client.createOrder({ productId: "BTC-USD", side: "BUY", quoteUsd: 50, clientOrderId: "c-1" });
    expect(submitted).toMatchObject({ success: true, clientOrderId: "c-1" });
    expect(await client.getOrder(submitted.orderId)).toMatchObject({ status: "FILLED", filledSize: 0.25, averagePrice: 200, totalFees: 0.3 });
  });

  it("reports UNKNOWN for an order it never saw", async () => {
    expect((await client.getOrder("missing")).status).toBe("UNKNOWN");
  });
});
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { normalizeExchangeError } from "@/lib/exchange/errors";

describe("normalizeExchangeError", () => {
  it("classifies auth, rate limit, outage, funds and bad request", () => {
    expect(normalizeExchangeError(new Error("Coinbase request failed (401): unauthorized")).code).toBe("auth_error");
    expect(normalizeExchangeError(new Error("Coinbase request failed (429): slow down")).retriable).toBe(true);
    expect(normalizeExchangeError(new Error("Coinbase request failed (503): down")).code).toBe("provider_unavailable");
    expect(normalizeExchangeError(new Error("Coinbase rejected the preview: PREVIEW_INSUFFICIENT_FUND")).code).toBe("insufficient_funds");
    expect(normalizeExchangeError(new Error("Coinbase request failed (400): bad product")).code).toBe("invalid_request");
    expect(normalizeExchangeError("weird").code).toBe("provider_error");
  });
});
```

```ts
import { asArray, asRecord, toNumber, toText, type FetchLike } from "@/lib/exchange/json";
import type { Candle, Ticker } from "@/lib/types";

const MARKET_BASE = "https://api.coinbase.com/api/v3/brokerage/market/products";

export const GRANULARITY_SECONDS = {
  ONE_MINUTE: 60,
  FIVE_MINUTE: 300,
  FIFTEEN_MINUTE: 900,
  THIRTY_MINUTE: 1800,
  ONE_HOUR: 3600,
  TWO_HOUR: 7200,
  SIX_HOUR: 21600,
  ONE_DAY: 86400
} as const;

export type Granularity = keyof typeof GRANULARITY_SECONDS;

/** Coinbase returns newest first, as strings. Output is oldest first, numeric. */
export function mapCandles(json: unknown): Candle[] {
  return asArray(asRecord(json).candles)
    .map((raw) => {
      const candle = asRecord(raw);
      return {
        start: toNumber(candle.start),
        open: toNumber(candle.open),
        high: toNumber(candle.high),
        low: toNumber(candle.low),
        close: toNumber(candle.close),
        volume: toNumber(candle.volume)
      };
    })
    .filter((candle) => candle.start > 0 && candle.close > 0)
    .sort((a, b) => a.start - b.start);
}

export function mapTicker(productId: string, json: unknown): Ticker {
  const body = asRecord(json);
  const last = asRecord(asArray(body.trades)[0]);
  const price = toNumber(last.price);
  const tradeTime = Date.parse(toText(last.time));
  if (price <= 0 || Number.isNaN(tradeTime)) throw new Error(`Coinbase returned no recent trade for ${productId}.`);
  return {
    productId,
    price,
    bestBid: toNumber(body.best_bid) || toNumber(last.bid) || price,
    bestAsk: toNumber(body.best_ask) || toNumber(last.ask) || price,
    tradeTime
  };
}

async function getPublicJson(url: string, fetchImpl: FetchLike): Promise<unknown> {
  const response = await fetchImpl(url, { headers: { Accept: "application/json" }, cache: "no-store" });
  if (!response.ok) {
    const body = (await response.text()).slice(0, 300);
    throw new Error(`Coinbase market data request failed (${response.status}): ${body}`);
  }
  return (await response.json()) as unknown;
}

export async function fetchCandles(
  productId: string,
  granularity: Granularity,
  limit: number,
  fetchImpl: FetchLike = fetch
): Promise<Candle[]> {
  const end = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
  const start = end - limit * GRANULARITY_SECONDS[granularity];
  const params = new URLSearchParams({ start: String(start), end: String(end), granularity, limit: String(limit) });
  return mapCandles(await getPublicJson(`${MARKET_BASE}/${encodeURIComponent(productId)}/candles?${params}`, fetchImpl));
}

export async function fetchTicker(productId: string, fetchImpl: FetchLike = fetch): Promise<Ticker> {
  return mapTicker(productId, await getPublicJson(`${MARKET_BASE}/${encodeURIComponent(productId)}/ticker?limit=1`, fetchImpl));
}
```

```ts
import { generateJwt } from "@coinbase/cdp-sdk/auth";
import { asArray, asRecord, toNumber, toStringList, toText, type FetchLike } from "@/lib/exchange/json";
import type {
  Balance,
  ExchangeClient,
  KeyPermissions,
  OrderPreview,
  OrderStatus,
  OrderStatusCode,
  OrderSubmitResult
} from "@/lib/exchange/types";

const API_HOST = "api.coinbase.com";
const BASE_PATH = "/api/v3/brokerage";

const STATUS_MAP: Record<string, OrderStatusCode> = {
  OPEN: "OPEN",
  FILLED: "FILLED",
  CANCELLED: "CANCELLED",
  EXPIRED: "EXPIRED",
  FAILED: "FAILED",
  PENDING: "PENDING",
  QUEUED: "PENDING",
  CANCEL_QUEUED: "OPEN",
  UNKNOWN_ORDER_STATUS: "UNKNOWN"
};

/** A missing can_transfer is treated as transfer-capable: Kairis fails closed on key scope. */
export function mapPermissions(json: unknown): KeyPermissions {
  const body = asRecord(json);
  return {
    canView: body.can_view === true,
    canTrade: body.can_trade === true,
    canTransfer: body.can_transfer !== false,
    portfolioUuid: toText(body.portfolio_uuid) || null
  };
}

export function mapBalances(json: unknown): Balance[] {
  return asArray(asRecord(json).accounts)
    .map((raw) => {
      const account = asRecord(raw);
      return { currency: toText(account.currency), available: toNumber(asRecord(account.available_balance).value) };
    })
    .filter((balance) => balance.currency.length > 0);
}

export function mapPreview(json: unknown): OrderPreview {
  const body = asRecord(json);
  const errs = toStringList(body.errs);
  if (errs.length > 0) throw new Error(`Coinbase rejected the preview: ${errs.join(", ")}`);
  return {
    previewId: toText(body.preview_id),
    orderTotal: toNumber(body.order_total),
    commissionTotal: toNumber(body.commission_total),
    bestBid: toNumber(body.best_bid),
    bestAsk: toNumber(body.best_ask),
    warnings: toStringList(body.warning)
  };
}

export function mapSubmit(json: unknown, clientOrderId: string): OrderSubmitResult {
  const body = asRecord(json);
  const ok = asRecord(body.success_response);
  const orderId = toText(ok.order_id);
  if (body.success === true && orderId) {
    return { success: true, orderId, clientOrderId: toText(ok.client_order_id) || clientOrderId, detail: `Coinbase accepted order ${orderId}.` };
  }
  const err = asRecord(body.error_response);
  const reason =
    toText(err.preview_failure_reason) || toText(err.new_order_failure_reason) || toText(err.error) || toText(body.failure_reason);
  const message = toText(err.message) || toText(err.error_details) || "Coinbase rejected the order.";
  return { success: false, orderId: "", clientOrderId, detail: reason ? `${message} (${reason})` : message };
}

export function mapOrderStatus(json: unknown): OrderStatus {
  const order = asRecord(asRecord(json).order);
  const raw = toText(order.status) || "UNKNOWN_ORDER_STATUS";
  return {
    orderId: toText(order.order_id),
    status: STATUS_MAP[raw] ?? "UNKNOWN",
    filledSize: toNumber(order.filled_size),
    averagePrice: toNumber(order.average_filled_price),
    totalFees: toNumber(order.total_fees),
    raw
  };
}

function marketIoc(quoteUsd: number): { market_market_ioc: { quote_size: string } } {
  return { market_market_ioc: { quote_size: quoteUsd.toFixed(2) } };
}

/** One client per user, built from that user's decrypted key. Never cache it across users. */
export function createCoinbaseClient(creds: { keyId: string; secret: string }, fetchImpl: FetchLike = fetch): ExchangeClient {
  async function call(method: "GET" | "POST", requestPath: string, body?: unknown): Promise<unknown> {
    const token = await generateJwt({
      apiKeyId: creds.keyId,
      apiKeySecret: creds.secret,
      requestMethod: method,
      requestHost: API_HOST,
      requestPath
    });
    const response = await fetchImpl(`https://${API_HOST}${requestPath}`, {
      method,
      headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: body === undefined ? undefined : JSON.stringify(body),
      cache: "no-store"
    });
    if (!response.ok) {
      const text = (await response.text()).slice(0, 500);
      throw new Error(`Coinbase request failed (${response.status}): ${text}`);
    }
    return (await response.json()) as unknown;
  }

  return {
    provider: "coinbase",
    async keyPermissions() {
      return mapPermissions(await call("GET", `${BASE_PATH}/key_permissions`));
    },
    async balances() {
      return mapBalances(await call("GET", `${BASE_PATH}/accounts`));
    },
    async previewOrder(input) {
      return mapPreview(
        await call("POST", `${BASE_PATH}/orders/preview`, {
          product_id: input.productId,
          side: input.side,
          order_configuration: marketIoc(input.quoteUsd)
        })
      );
    },
    async createOrder(input) {
      const body = {
        client_order_id: input.clientOrderId,
        product_id: input.productId,
        side: input.side,
        ...(input.previewId ? { preview_id: input.previewId } : {}),
        order_configuration: marketIoc(input.quoteUsd)
      };
      return mapSubmit(await call("POST", `${BASE_PATH}/orders`, body), input.clientOrderId);
    },
    async getOrder(orderId) {
      return mapOrderStatus(await call("GET", `${BASE_PATH}/orders/historical/${encodeURIComponent(orderId)}`));
    }
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: lib/exchange/mock.ts**

```ts
import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
import type { ExchangeClient, OrderStatus } from "@/lib/exchange/types";
import type { Side } from "@/lib/types";

const COMMISSION_RATE = 0.006;
const MOCK_WARNING = "Mock provider: no live order will be sent";
const cents = (n: number) => Math.round(n * 100) / 100;

/** Process-wide so a later request can reconcile an order an earlier request created. Lost on restart. */
const mockOrders = new Map<string, { productId: string; side: Side; quoteUsd: number; price: number }>();

/** Exchange stand-in used whenever a user has no Coinbase connection. Nothing leaves the process. */
export function createMockClient(referencePrice: (productId: string) => Promise<number>): ExchangeClient {
  return {
    provider: "mock",
    async keyPermissions() {
      return { canView: true, canTrade: true, canTransfer: false, portfolioUuid: null };
    },
    async balances() {
      return [{ currency: "USD", available: 10000 }];
    },
    async previewOrder(input) {
      const price = await referencePrice(input.productId);
      const commissionTotal = cents(input.quoteUsd * COMMISSION_RATE);
      return {
        previewId: randomUUID(),
        orderTotal: cents(input.side === "BUY" ? input.quoteUsd + commissionTotal : input.quoteUsd - commissionTotal),
        commissionTotal,
        bestBid: price * 0.9995,
        bestAsk: price * 1.0005,
        warnings: [MOCK_WARNING]
      };
    },
    async createOrder(input) {
      const price = await referencePrice(input.productId);
      const orderId = randomUUID();
      mockOrders.set(orderId, { productId: input.productId, side: input.side, quoteUsd: input.quoteUsd, price });
      return {
        success: true,
        orderId,
        clientOrderId: input.clientOrderId,
        detail: "Mock provider accepted the order; nothing was sent to an exchange."
      };
    },
    async getOrder(orderId): Promise<OrderStatus> {
      const order = mockOrders.get(orderId);
      if (!order) return { orderId, status: "UNKNOWN", filledSize: 0, averagePrice: 0, totalFees: 0, raw: "UNKNOWN_ORDER_STATUS" };
      return {
        orderId,
        status: "FILLED",
        filledSize: order.quoteUsd / order.price,
        averagePrice: order.price,
        totalFees: cents(order.quoteUsd * COMMISSION_RATE),
        raw: "FILLED"
      };
    }
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 8: lib/exchange/errors.ts (rewrite, same exports)**

```ts
export type ExchangeError = {
  code: string;
  message: string;
  retriable: boolean;
  recommendation: string;
};

/** Pulls the HTTP status out of "Coinbase ... failed (401): ..." messages. */
function statusOf(message: string): number | null {
  const match = /\((\d{3})\)/.exec(message);
  return match ? Number(match[1]) : null;
}

/** Turns any exchange failure into a plain-language reason a user can act on. */
export function normalizeExchangeError(error: unknown): ExchangeError {
  const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Unknown exchange provider error.";
  const status = statusOf(message);

  if (/INSUFFICIENT_FUND/i.test(message)) {
    return {
      code: "insufficient_funds",
      message,
      retriable: false,
      recommendation: "The exchange account does not hold enough funds for this order. Lower the size or fund the account on Coinbase."
    };
  }
  if (status === 401 || status === 403) {
    return {
      code: "auth_error",
      message,
      retriable: false,
      recommendation: "Check that the API key still exists, has View and Trade permission, and that the private key was pasted in full."
    };
  }
  if (status === 429) {
    return {
      code: "rate_limited",
      message,
      retriable: true,
      recommendation: "Coinbase is rate limiting requests. Wait a minute and retry."
    };
  }
  if (status !== null && status >= 500) {
    return {
      code: "provider_unavailable",
      message,
      retriable: true,
      recommendation: "Coinbase is having trouble. Execution pauses until it recovers; retry later."
    };
  }
  if (status === 400 || status === 404) {
    return {
      code: "invalid_request",
      message,
      retriable: false,
      recommendation: "Coinbase rejected the request. Check the product id and the order size."
    };
  }
  return {
    code: "provider_error",
    message,
    retriable: false,
    recommendation: "Review the provider response before retrying."
  };
}
```

### Task 9: Identity (qavren-auth Keycloak realm via @qavren/auth-next)

**Files:**
- Create: `auth.ts` (repo root), `proxy.ts` (repo root), `lib/auth/paths.ts`, `app/api/auth/[...nextauth]/route.ts`
- Create: `app/sign-in/page.tsx`, `app/sign-in/actions.ts`, `app/app/sign-out/actions.ts`, `lib/server/session.ts`
- Create: `tests/unit/auth-paths.test.ts`
- Modify: `.env.local` (local only, gitignored)

How identity works here (mirrors fairsquare): the SDK builds an Auth.js v5 config for realm `env.realm` as a public PKCE client `<realm>-web`, issuer `${QAVREN_AUTH_URL}/realms/<realm>`, and maps realm roles onto `session.user.roles`. Kairis never calls another API with the access token, so there is no token refresh and no session redaction: the session only carries `user.id` (the Keycloak `sub`), `email`, `name` and `roles`. The SDK replaces the whole `callbacks` object when you pass one, so the config is built once with `buildAuthConfig` and its `jwt`/`session` callbacks are wrapped rather than replaced.

Env read by this task: `AUTH_SECRET`, `AUTH_URL`, `AUTH_TRUST_HOST=true`, `QAVREN_AUTH_URL` (defaults to `https://auth.qavrensolutions.com` inside the SDK), `QAVREN_REALM` (`kairis` in prod, `kairis-dev` locally).

- [ ] **Step 2: lib/auth/paths.ts** (copied from fairsquare, fallback `/app`)

```ts
/** Shared, dependency-free path helpers for the sign-in flow (safe in proxy, server, and client code). */

/** Only same-origin absolute paths are honoured as a post-sign-in destination. */
export function safeCallbackUrl(raw: string | undefined | null, fallback = "/app"): string {
  if (!raw) return fallback;
  if (!raw.startsWith("/") || raw.startsWith("//") || raw.startsWith("/\\")) return fallback;
  return raw;
}

export function signInPath(callbackUrl: string): string {
  const q = new URLSearchParams({ callbackUrl: safeCallbackUrl(callbackUrl) });
  return `/sign-in?${q}`;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: auth.ts (repo root)**

```ts
import { buildAuthConfig, createAuth } from "@qavren/auth-next";
import type { DefaultSession, Session } from "next-auth";
import { env } from "@/lib/env";

/**
 * Same shape the SDK already declares (roles on the user). Declared here too so the contract is
 * visible in this repo. `user.id` is Auth.js's own optional `User.id`: it is set from the Keycloak
 * `sub` in the session callback below and narrowed in `lib/server/session.ts`. Redeclaring `user`
 * with a required `id` would conflict with the SDK's declaration (TS2717), so do not.
 */
declare module "next-auth" {
  interface Session {
    user: DefaultSession["user"] & { roles: string[] };
  }
}

// The SDK spreads overrides last, so passing `callbacks` alone would drop its role mapping.
// Build the base config once and wrap its callbacks instead.
const base = buildAuthConfig({ realm: env.realm });
const baseJwt = base.callbacks?.jwt;
const baseSession = base.callbacks?.session;

export const { handlers, auth, signIn, signOut } = createAuth({
  realm: env.realm,
  pages: { signIn: "/sign-in" },
  callbacks: {
    async jwt(params) {
      return (baseJwt ? await baseJwt(params) : null) ?? params.token;
    },
    async session(params) {
      const session = (baseSession ? await baseSession(params) : params.session) as Session;
      if (session.user && typeof params.token.sub === "string") session.user.id = params.token.sub;
      return session;
    }
  }
});
```

- [ ] **Step 5: proxy.ts (repo root; the Next 16 middleware file)**

```ts
import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { auth } from "@/auth";
import { signInPath } from "@/lib/auth/paths";
import { env } from "@/lib/env";

/**
 * Everything under /app requires a session; anything else passes through. The redirect origin is
 * APP_BASE_URL, not the request: inside the container the request origin is the bind address, and
 * deriving it from the Host header would let a client steer where unauthenticated visitors go.
 */
export default auth((req) => {
  if (req.auth?.user) return NextResponse.next();
  const callbackUrl = `${req.nextUrl.pathname}${req.nextUrl.search}`;
  return NextResponse.redirect(new URL(signInPath(callbackUrl), env.appBaseUrl));
});

export const config = { matcher: ["/app/:path*"] };
```

```tsx
import type { Metadata } from "next";
import Link from "next/link";
import { redirect } from "next/navigation";
import { BrandMark } from "@/components/brand-mark";
import { safeCallbackUrl } from "@/lib/auth/paths";
import { currentUser } from "@/lib/server/session";
import { signInAction } from "./actions";

export const metadata: Metadata = { title: "Sign in" };
export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

export default async function SignInPage({
  searchParams
}: {
  searchParams: Promise<Record<string, string | string[] | undefined>>;
}) {
  const sp = await searchParams;
  const raw = sp.callbackUrl;
  const callbackUrl = safeCallbackUrl(typeof raw === "string" ? raw : null);
  if (await currentUser()) redirect(callbackUrl);

  return (
    <main className="page-shell narrow">
      <section className="panel sign-in">
        <BrandMark compact />
        <p className="eyebrow">Kairis</p>
        <h1>Sign in</h1>
        <p className="panel-copy">
          Kairis uses the Qavren account service. New here? Choose register on the next screen. Every account
          starts in paper mode; nothing reaches an exchange until you connect a trade-only key and confirm an
          order yourself.
        </p>
        <form action={signInAction}>
          <input type="hidden" name="callbackUrl" value={callbackUrl} />
          <button type="submit" className="cta-primary">
            Continue to sign in
          </button>
        </form>
        <Link className="inline-link" href="/">
          Back to the overview
        </Link>
      </section>
    </main>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 9: lib/server/session.ts**

```ts
import { notFound, redirect } from "next/navigation";
import { auth } from "@/auth";
import { signInPath } from "@/lib/auth/paths";
import { isOwner } from "@/lib/domain/owner";
import { env } from "@/lib/env";
import { getOnboarding } from "@/lib/server/repos/onboarding";

export type SessionUser = { id: string; email: string | null; name: string | null; roles: string[]; isOwner: boolean };

/** The signed-in user, or null. `id` is the Keycloak sub and keys every row Kairis stores. */
export async function currentUser(): Promise<SessionUser | null> {
  const session = await auth();
  const user = session?.user;
  if (!user?.id) return null;
  const roles = Array.isArray(user.roles) ? user.roles.filter((role): role is string => typeof role === "string") : [];
  const email = user.email ?? null;
  return { id: user.id, email, name: user.name ?? null, roles, isOwner: isOwner({ email, roles }, env.ownerEmails) };
}

/** Redirects to the sign-in page (coming back to `callbackUrl`) when nobody is signed in. */
export async function requireUser(callbackUrl = "/app"): Promise<SessionUser> {
  const user = await currentUser();
  if (!user) redirect(signInPath(callbackUrl));
  return user;
}

/**
 * Every /app page except onboarding calls this. The gate lives in the pages, not the /app layout,
 * because App Router layouts do not re-render on client-side navigation: a layout-only gate would
 * let a user who has not acknowledged the risks click straight past it.
 */
export async function requireOnboarded(callbackUrl: string): Promise<SessionUser> {
  const user = await requireUser(callbackUrl);
  const onboarding = await getOnboarding(user.id);
  if (!onboarding.riskAcknowledged) redirect("/app/onboarding");
  return user;
}

/** Owner-only pages: anyone else gets a 404, so the page's existence is not advertised. */
export async function requireOwner(): Promise<SessionUser> {
  const user = await requireUser("/app/operations");
  if (!user.isOwner) notFound();
  return user;
}
```

`.env.local` is gitignored and may still hold pre-v1 values. Make sure it contains these lines (add or replace; keep any others):

```
APP_BASE_URL=http://localhost:3000
AUTH_URL=http://localhost:3000
AUTH_TRUST_HOST=true
AUTH_SECRET=<output of: node -e "console.log(require('crypto').randomBytes(32).toString('base64'))">
QAVREN_REALM=kairis-dev
DATABASE_URL=postgres://kairis:kairis@localhost:55433/kairis
```

Leave `QAVREN_AUTH_URL` unset to use the hosted `https://auth.qavrensolutions.com` (the owner provisions the `kairis-dev` realm; see Task 16 `docs/deployment.md`), or set it to `http://localhost:8080` for the local Keycloak in `C:\Users\steve\projects\qavren-auth` (Task 17, Step 8).

### Task 10: Services

**Files:**
- Create under `lib/server/services/`: `market.ts`, `portfolio.ts`, `signals.ts`, `risk.ts`, `paper.ts`, `exchange-connection.ts`, `assisted.ts`, `exports.ts`, `auto.ts`
- Create: `lib/server/artifacts.ts`
- Rewrite: `lib/server/system-status.ts` (async, adds the database ping), `app/api/health/route.ts` (liveness only, no DB), `app/api/system/status/route.ts` (awaits the new status)
- Create: `tests/unit/services-paper.test.ts`, `tests/unit/services-assisted.test.ts`, `tests/unit/services-exchange.test.ts`

Services are the only layer that combines repos, market data, the exchange and the pure domain modules. Pages and server actions call services; services never import `@/auth` (they take a `userId`), which keeps them unit-testable with `vi.mock`.

Live positions note: Kairis only knows about fills it placed. Live positions and live realized P&L are rebuilt from Kairis-recorded fills (`status = 'filled'` assisted orders) by replaying them through `buildPositions`/`fillPaperOrder`, so selling coins bought outside Kairis is blocked by the risk engine's "no position" check. That is deliberate for v1 and is stated on the trade page.

- [ ] **Step 1: lib/server/services/market.ts**

```ts
import { STRATEGY } from "@/lib/domain/strategy";
import { fetchCandles, fetchTicker } from "@/lib/exchange/coinbase-public";
import type { Candle, Ticker } from "@/lib/types";

export type MarketSnapshot = { candles: Candle[]; ticker: Ticker; fetchedAt: number };

const TTL_MS = 30_000;
const cache = new Map<string, { snapshot: MarketSnapshot; expiresAt: number }>();
const inflight = new Map<string, Promise<MarketSnapshot>>();

/** Candles + ticker for one product, cached in process for 30 s; concurrent callers share one fetch. */
export async function getMarketSnapshot(productId: string): Promise<MarketSnapshot> {
  const hit = cache.get(productId);
  if (hit && hit.expiresAt > Date.now()) return hit.snapshot;
  const pending = inflight.get(productId);
  if (pending) return pending;
  const load = (async () => {
    const [candles, ticker] = await Promise.all([
      fetchCandles(productId, STRATEGY.granularity, STRATEGY.candleLimit),
      fetchTicker(productId)
    ]);
    const snapshot: MarketSnapshot = { candles, ticker, fetchedAt: Date.now() };
    cache.set(productId, { snapshot, expiresAt: Date.now() + TTL_MS });
    return snapshot;
  })();
  inflight.set(productId, load);
  try {
    return await load;
  } finally {
    inflight.delete(productId);
  }
}

export async function getReferencePrice(productId: string): Promise<number> {
  return (await getMarketSnapshot(productId)).ticker.price;
}

export function clearMarketCache(): void {
  cache.clear();
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: lib/server/services/portfolio.ts**

```ts
import { buildPositions, dayStats, fillPaperOrder } from "@/lib/domain/paper";
import { listAssistedFills, listPendingAssistedOrders } from "@/lib/server/repos/assisted";
import { listPaperFills } from "@/lib/server/repos/paper";
import { getReferencePrice } from "@/lib/server/services/market";
import type { AssistedOrder, DayStats, PaperTrade, PositionMap, TradeMode } from "@/lib/types";

export type Portfolio = {
  mode: TradeMode;
  positions: PositionMap;
  today: DayStats;
  marks: Record<string, number>;
  unrealizedPnlUsd: number;
};

/** Replays filled assisted orders through the paper fill model to get live realized P&L per sell. */
export function liveFills(orders: ReadonlyArray<AssistedOrder>): PaperTrade[] {
  const filled = orders
    .filter((order) => order.status === "filled" && (order.filledSize ?? 0) > 0 && (order.averagePrice ?? 0) > 0)
    .sort((a, b) => a.createdAt.localeCompare(b.createdAt));
  const out: PaperTrade[] = [];
  for (const order of filled) {
    const price = order.averagePrice ?? 0;
    const size = order.filledSize ?? 0;
    const fill = fillPaperOrder(
      buildPositions(out, {}),
      { productId: order.productId, side: order.side, quoteUsd: size * price, mode: "live" },
      price
    );
    out.push({
      id: order.id,
      userId: order.userId,
      productId: order.productId,
      side: order.side,
      baseSize: fill.baseSize,
      price,
      quoteUsd: order.quoteUsd,
      status: "filled",
      realizedPnlUsd: fill.realizedPnlUsd,
      note: "",
      signalId: order.signalId,
      riskDecision: null,
      createdAt: order.createdAt
    });
  }
  return out;
}

export function isSameUtcDay(isoTime: string, now: Date): boolean {
  return isoTime.slice(0, 10) === now.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
}

/**
 * Fills and today's stats for one mode. Live trade counts include submitted orders that are not
 * reconciled yet, so firing several orders before a reconcile cannot dodge the trades-per-day cap.
 */
export async function loadFills(userId: string, mode: TradeMode, now: Date): Promise<{ fills: PaperTrade[]; today: DayStats }> {
  if (mode === "paper") {
    const fills = await listPaperFills(userId);
    return { fills, today: dayStats(fills, now) };
  }
  const [filled, pending] = await Promise.all([listAssistedFills(userId), listPendingAssistedOrders(userId)]);
  const fills = liveFills(filled);
  const stats = dayStats(fills, now);
  const pendingToday = pending.filter((order) => isSameUtcDay(order.createdAt, now)).length;
  return { fills, today: { ...stats, tradesCount: stats.tradesCount + pendingToday } };
}

export async function getPortfolio(userId: string, mode: TradeMode, now = new Date()): Promise<Portfolio> {
  const { fills, today } = await loadFills(userId, mode, now);
  const unmarked = buildPositions(fills, {});
  const marks: Record<string, number> = {};
  await Promise.all(
    Object.keys(unmarked).map(async (productId) => {
      try {
        marks[productId] = await getReferencePrice(productId);
      } catch {
        // No mark available: notional falls back to average cost.
      }
    })
  );
  const positions = buildPositions(fills, marks);
  const unrealizedPnlUsd = Object.entries(positions).reduce(
    (sum, [productId, position]) => sum + ((marks[productId] ?? position.avgCost) - position.avgCost) * position.baseSize,
    0
  );
  return { mode, positions, today, marks, unrealizedPnlUsd };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: lib/server/services/signals.ts**

```ts
import { evaluateSignal, suggestedQuoteUsd } from "@/lib/domain/signals";
import { WATCHLIST } from "@/lib/domain/strategy";
import { appendAudit } from "@/lib/server/repos/audit";
import { insertSignal, latestSignals } from "@/lib/server/repos/signals";
import { getMarketSnapshot } from "@/lib/server/services/market";
import type { SignalEvaluation, SignalRecord, TradingLimits } from "@/lib/types";

export type SizedSignal = SignalRecord & { suggestedQuoteUsd: number };
type SizingLimits = Pick<TradingLimits, "dailyLossCapUsd" | "maxPositionUsd" | "perSymbolMaxUsd">;

function unavailable(productId: string, error: unknown): SignalEvaluation {
  const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : "unknown error";
  return {
    productId,
    action: "blocked",
    setup: "Market data unavailable",
    rationale: [`Coinbase market data could not be loaded (${message}); signals fail closed.`],
    strength: 0,
    referencePrice: 0,
    atrPct: null,
    rsi: null,
    spreadPct: null,
    dataAgeMs: 0,
    evaluatedAt: new Date().toISOString()
  };
}

/** Evaluates every watchlist product and stores one row per evaluation (PRD: log every signal evaluation). */
export async function refreshSignals(userId: string | null): Promise<SignalRecord[]> {
  const records: SignalRecord[] = [];
  for (const productId of WATCHLIST) {
    let evaluation: SignalEvaluation;
    try {
      const snapshot = await getMarketSnapshot(productId);
      evaluation = evaluateSignal(productId, snapshot.candles, snapshot.ticker, Date.now());
    } catch (error) {
      evaluation = unavailable(productId, error);
    }
    records.push(await insertSignal(evaluation));
  }
  await appendAudit(
    userId ?? "system",
    "signal",
    "signals.refreshed",
    records.map((record) => `${record.productId} ${record.action} (${record.setup})`).join("; ")
  );
  return records;
}

/** Suggested size for long setups only, capped by the product's per-symbol cap when one is set. */
export function sizeSignal(signal: SignalRecord, limits: SizingLimits): SizedSignal {
  const base = signal.action === "long" ? suggestedQuoteUsd(limits, signal.atrPct) : 0;
  const cap = limits.perSymbolMaxUsd[signal.productId];
  return { ...signal, suggestedQuoteUsd: cap !== undefined ? Math.min(base, cap) : base };
}

export async function latestSignalsWithSizing(limits: SizingLimits): Promise<SizedSignal[]> {
  const order = new Map<string, number>(WATCHLIST.map((productId, index): [string, number] => [productId, index]));
  return (await latestSignals())
    .map((signal) => sizeSignal(signal, limits))
    .sort((a, b) => (order.get(a.productId) ?? 99) - (order.get(b.productId) ?? 99));
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: lib/server/services/risk.ts**

```ts
import { buildPositions } from "@/lib/domain/paper";
import { evaluateRisk } from "@/lib/domain/risk";
import { STRATEGY } from "@/lib/domain/strategy";
import { appendAudit } from "@/lib/server/repos/audit";
import { getLimits } from "@/lib/server/repos/limits";
import { getMarketSnapshot } from "@/lib/server/services/market";
import { loadFills } from "@/lib/server/services/portfolio";
import type { OrderIntent, RiskContext, RiskDecision, TradeMode } from "@/lib/types";

export type RiskEvaluation = { decision: RiskDecision; context: RiskContext };

/** Limits + today's stats + positions for the mode + market freshness. A market failure marks the provider degraded. */
export async function buildRiskContext(userId: string, mode: TradeMode, productId: string): Promise<RiskContext> {
  const now = new Date();
  const [limits, { fills, today }] = await Promise.all([getLimits(userId), loadFills(userId, mode, now)]);
  let referencePrice = 0;
  let dataAgeMs = STRATEGY.maxTickerAgeMs + 1;
  let providerDegraded = false;
  try {
    const snapshot = await getMarketSnapshot(productId);
    referencePrice = snapshot.ticker.price;
    dataAgeMs = Math.max(0, now.getTime() - snapshot.ticker.tradeTime);
  } catch {
    providerDegraded = true;
  }
  return {
    limits,
    today,
    positions: buildPositions(fills, referencePrice > 0 ? { [productId]: referencePrice } : {}),
    referencePrice,
    dataAgeMs,
    maxDataAgeMs: STRATEGY.maxTickerAgeMs,
    providerDegraded,
    now
  };
}

/** "<mode> <side> <product> <size>: <reasons>". The journal's mode filter relies on the leading mode word. */
export function describeDecision(intent: OrderIntent, decision: RiskDecision): string {
  const size = Number.isFinite(intent.quoteUsd) ? `$${intent.quoteUsd.toFixed(2)}` : "invalid size";
  const why = decision.reasons.length > 0 ? decision.reasons.join(" ") : "All checks passed.";
  return `${intent.mode} ${intent.side} ${intent.productId} ${size}: ${why}`;
}

/** Runs the risk engine and writes an audit "risk" event with the outcome and reasons. */
export async function evaluateIntent(userId: string, intent: OrderIntent): Promise<RiskEvaluation> {
  const context = await buildRiskContext(userId, intent.mode, intent.productId);
  const decision = evaluateRisk(context, intent);
  await appendAudit(userId, "risk", `risk.${decision.outcome}`, describeDecision(intent, decision));
  return { decision, context };
}

export async function checkOrder(userId: string, intent: OrderIntent): Promise<RiskDecision> {
  return (await evaluateIntent(userId, intent)).decision;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: lib/server/services/paper.ts**

```ts
import { fillPaperOrder } from "@/lib/domain/paper";
import { appendAudit } from "@/lib/server/repos/audit";
import { insertPaperTrade } from "@/lib/server/repos/paper";
import { evaluateIntent } from "@/lib/server/services/risk";
import type { OrderIntent, PaperTrade } from "@/lib/types";

const cents = (n: number) => Math.round(n * 100) / 100;
/** quote_usd is numeric(12,2): clamp so a hostile size is recorded as blocked instead of overflowing. */
const storableQuote = (n: number) => (Number.isFinite(n) && n > 0 ? Math.min(cents(n), 9_999_999_999) : 0);

/**
 * Risk check, then either a blocked row carrying the decision or a simulated fill at the
 * reference price. Both outcomes are persisted and audited.
 */
export async function placePaperOrder(userId: string, intent: OrderIntent, note = ""): Promise<PaperTrade> {
  const paperIntent: OrderIntent = { ...intent, mode: "paper" };
  const { decision, context } = await evaluateIntent(userId, paperIntent);
  const signalId = paperIntent.signalId ?? null;

  if (decision.outcome !== "approved") {
    const trade = await insertPaperTrade({
      userId,
      productId: paperIntent.productId,
      side: paperIntent.side,
      baseSize: 0,
      price: context.referencePrice,
      quoteUsd: storableQuote(paperIntent.quoteUsd),
      status: "blocked",
      realizedPnlUsd: 0,
      note,
      signalId,
      riskDecision: decision
    });
    await appendAudit(userId, "paper-trade", "paper.blocked", `${paperIntent.side} ${paperIntent.productId} ${decision.outcome}: ${decision.reasons.join(" ")}`);
    return trade;
  }

  const fill = fillPaperOrder(context.positions, paperIntent, context.referencePrice);
  const trade = await insertPaperTrade({
    userId,
    productId: paperIntent.productId,
    side: paperIntent.side,
    baseSize: fill.baseSize,
    price: fill.price,
    quoteUsd: storableQuote(fill.baseSize * fill.price),
    status: "filled",
    realizedPnlUsd: cents(fill.realizedPnlUsd),
    note,
    signalId,
    riskDecision: decision
  });
  await appendAudit(
    userId,
    "paper-trade",
    "paper.filled",
    `${paperIntent.side} ${fill.baseSize.toFixed(8)} ${paperIntent.productId} at $${fill.price.toFixed(2)} (realized $${cents(fill.realizedPnlUsd).toFixed(2)}).`
  );
  return trade;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: lib/server/services/exchange-connection.ts**

```ts
import { openSecret, sealSecret } from "@/lib/domain/crypto";
import { env } from "@/lib/env";
import { createCoinbaseClient } from "@/lib/exchange/coinbase";
import { createMockClient } from "@/lib/exchange/mock";
import type { ExchangeClient, ExchangeProvider } from "@/lib/exchange/types";
import { appendAudit } from "@/lib/server/repos/audit";
import { deleteConnection, getConnection, saveConnection, type StoredConnection } from "@/lib/server/repos/exchange";
import { getOnboarding, saveOnboarding } from "@/lib/server/repos/onboarding";
import { getReferencePrice } from "@/lib/server/services/market";
import type { ExchangeConnection } from "@/lib/types";

export const SECRET_KEY_MISSING = "Exchange connections are disabled: KAIRIS_SECRET_KEY is not set";
export const TRANSFER_KEY_REJECTED =
  "This key can transfer funds. Kairis only accepts trade-only keys; create a new key without withdrawal/transfer permission.";

export type ConnectionStatus = { enabled: boolean; provider: ExchangeProvider; connection: ExchangeConnection | null };

export function maskKeyId(keyId: string): string {
  return keyId.length <= 8 ? keyId : `...${keyId.slice(-8)}`;
}

/** Coinbase key downloads often carry the PEM with literal "\n" escapes; turn them back into newlines. */
export function normalizeSecret(secret: string): string {
  return secret.trim().replace(/\\n/g, "\n");
}

function toPublic(stored: StoredConnection): ExchangeConnection {
  return {
    userId: stored.userId,
    provider: stored.provider,
    keyId: stored.keyId,
    canView: stored.canView,
    canTrade: stored.canTrade,
    canTransfer: stored.canTransfer,
    portfolioUuid: stored.portfolioUuid,
    validatedAt: stored.validatedAt,
    createdAt: stored.createdAt
  };
}

/** Validates the key against Coinbase, rejects transfer-capable keys, stores the secret AES-GCM sealed. */
export async function connectCoinbase(userId: string, keyId: string, secretPem: string): Promise<ExchangeConnection> {
  if (!env.secretKey) throw new Error(SECRET_KEY_MISSING);
  const trimmedKeyId = keyId.trim();
  const secret = normalizeSecret(secretPem);
  const permissions = await createCoinbaseClient({ keyId: trimmedKeyId, secret }).keyPermissions();
  if (permissions.canTransfer) {
    await appendAudit(userId, "exchange", "exchange.rejected", `Rejected Coinbase key ${maskKeyId(trimmedKeyId)}: it can transfer funds.`);
    throw new Error(TRANSFER_KEY_REJECTED);
  }
  const stored = await saveConnection({ userId, keyId: trimmedKeyId, sealed: sealSecret(secret, env.secretKey), ...permissions });
  const onboarding = await getOnboarding(userId);
  await saveOnboarding({ ...onboarding, exchangeConnected: true });
  await appendAudit(
    userId,
    "exchange",
    "exchange.connected",
    `Coinbase key ${maskKeyId(trimmedKeyId)} connected (view ${permissions.canView ? "yes" : "no"}, trade ${permissions.canTrade ? "yes" : "no"}, transfer no).`
  );
  return toPublic(stored);
}

export async function disconnect(userId: string): Promise<void> {
  const stored = await getConnection(userId);
  await deleteConnection(userId);
  const onboarding = await getOnboarding(userId);
  await saveOnboarding({ ...onboarding, exchangeConnected: false });
  await appendAudit(userId, "exchange", "exchange.disconnected", stored ? `Coinbase key ${maskKeyId(stored.keyId)} removed.` : "No key was stored.");
}

/** The user's Coinbase client when connected, else the mock client. Fails closed if a stored key cannot be opened. */
export async function getExchangeClient(userId: string): Promise<ExchangeClient> {
  const stored = await getConnection(userId);
  if (!stored) return createMockClient(getReferencePrice);
  if (!env.secretKey) throw new Error("Exchange connection unavailable: KAIRIS_SECRET_KEY is not set.");
  return createCoinbaseClient({ keyId: stored.keyId, secret: openSecret(stored.sealed, env.secretKey) });
}

/** The client that matches the provider an order was placed with (used by reconcile). */
export async function clientForProvider(userId: string, provider: ExchangeProvider): Promise<ExchangeClient> {
  if (provider === "mock") return createMockClient(getReferencePrice);
  const client = await getExchangeClient(userId);
  if (client.provider !== "coinbase") {
    throw new Error("The Coinbase connection used for this order was removed; reconnect it to reconcile.");
  }
  return client;
}

export async function providerFor(userId: string): Promise<ExchangeProvider> {
  return (await getConnection(userId)) ? "coinbase" : "mock";
}

/** Safe for the UI: never includes the sealed secret. */
export async function connectionStatus(userId: string): Promise<ConnectionStatus> {
  const stored = await getConnection(userId);
  return {
    enabled: env.secretKey.length > 0,
    provider: stored ? "coinbase" : "mock",
    connection: stored ? toPublic(stored) : null
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: lib/server/services/assisted.ts**

```ts
import { env } from "@/lib/env";
import { normalizeExchangeError } from "@/lib/exchange/errors";
import type { ExchangeClient, ExchangeProvider, OrderPreview } from "@/lib/exchange/types";
import {
  claimPreviewedOrder,
  getAssistedOrder,
  insertAssistedOrder,
  listPendingAssistedOrders,
  updateAssistedOrder
} from "@/lib/server/repos/assisted";
import { appendAudit } from "@/lib/server/repos/audit";
import { clientForProvider, getExchangeClient, providerFor } from "@/lib/server/services/exchange-connection";
import { evaluateIntent } from "@/lib/server/services/risk";
import type { AssistedOrder, OrderIntent, RiskDecision } from "@/lib/types";

export const PREVIEW_TTL_MS = 2 * 60 * 1000;
export const LIVE_DISABLED_DETAIL = "Live assisted trading is disabled by environment policy.";

export type AssistedResult = { decision: RiskDecision | null; order: AssistedOrder; preview: OrderPreview | null };
export type ReconcileSummary = { checked: number; filled: number; closed: number; open: number; errors: number };

const cents = (n: number) => Math.round(n * 100) / 100;
const storableQuote = (n: number) => (Number.isFinite(n) && n > 0 ? Math.min(cents(n), 9_999_999_999) : 0);
const label = (order: Pick<AssistedOrder, "side" | "productId" | "quoteUsd">) =>
  `${order.side} ${order.productId} $${order.quoteUsd.toFixed(2)}`;

/**
 * Step 1 of the assisted flow: risk check in live mode. Blocked or halted -> a blocked row with the
 * decision. Approved -> an exchange preview stored as a previewed row. Exchange errors -> a failed row.
 */
export async function previewAssisted(userId: string, intent: OrderIntent): Promise<AssistedResult> {
  const liveIntent: OrderIntent = { ...intent, mode: "live" };
  const { decision } = await evaluateIntent(userId, liveIntent);
  const base = {
    userId,
    productId: liveIntent.productId,
    side: liveIntent.side,
    quoteUsd: storableQuote(liveIntent.quoteUsd),
    signalId: liveIntent.signalId ?? null,
    riskDecision: decision
  };

  if (decision.outcome !== "approved") {
    const order = await insertAssistedOrder({
      ...base,
      status: "blocked",
      reconcileState: "reconciled",
      provider: await providerFor(userId),
      detail: decision.reasons.join(" ")
    });
    await appendAudit(userId, "assisted-order", "assisted.blocked", `${label(order)} ${decision.outcome}: ${order.detail}`);
    return { decision, order, preview: null };
  }

  let client: ExchangeClient | null = null;
  try {
    client = await getExchangeClient(userId);
    const preview = await client.previewOrder({ productId: base.productId, side: base.side, quoteUsd: base.quoteUsd });
    const order = await insertAssistedOrder({
      ...base,
      status: "previewed",
      reconcileState: "pending",
      provider: client.provider,
      previewId: preview.previewId || null,
      detail: `Preview: total $${preview.orderTotal.toFixed(2)}, commission $${preview.commissionTotal.toFixed(2)}.`
    });
    await appendAudit(userId, "assisted-order", "assisted.previewed", `${label(order)} via ${client.provider}; the preview expires in 2 minutes.`);
    return { decision, order, preview };
  } catch (error) {
    const normalized = normalizeExchangeError(error);
    const provider: ExchangeProvider = client ? client.provider : await providerFor(userId);
    const order = await insertAssistedOrder({
      ...base,
      status: "failed",
      reconcileState: "reconciled",
      provider,
      detail: `${normalized.message} ${normalized.recommendation}`
    });
    await appendAudit(userId, "assisted-order", "assisted.preview-failed", `${label(order)}: ${normalized.code}`);
    return { decision, order, preview: null };
  }
}

/**
 * Step 2: the user confirmed. Re-checks risk, enforces the environment gate for real Coinbase
 * orders, claims the row atomically, then submits with the row id as the client order id.
 */
export async function submitAssisted(userId: string, orderId: string): Promise<AssistedResult> {
  const order = await getAssistedOrder(orderId);
  if (!order || order.userId !== userId) throw new Error("Order not found.");
  if (order.status !== "previewed") throw new Error(`This order is ${order.status}; only a previewed order can be submitted.`);
  if (Date.now() - Date.parse(order.createdAt) > PREVIEW_TTL_MS) {
    await updateAssistedOrder(order.id, { status: "expired", reconcileState: "reconciled", detail: "Preview expired before confirmation." });
    await appendAudit(userId, "assisted-order", "assisted.expired", `${label(order)} preview expired.`);
    throw new Error("Preview expired, preview again.");
  }

  const { decision } = await evaluateIntent(userId, {
    productId: order.productId,
    side: order.side,
    quoteUsd: order.quoteUsd,
    mode: "live",
    signalId: order.signalId
  });
  if (decision.outcome !== "approved") {
    const blocked = await updateAssistedOrder(order.id, {
      status: "blocked",
      reconcileState: "reconciled",
      detail: decision.reasons.join(" "),
      riskDecision: decision
    });
    await appendAudit(userId, "assisted-order", "assisted.blocked", `${label(order)} ${decision.outcome} at submit: ${blocked.detail}`);
    return { decision, order: blocked, preview: null };
  }

  const client = await getExchangeClient(userId);
  if (client.provider !== order.provider) {
    const changed = await updateAssistedOrder(order.id, {
      status: "failed",
      reconcileState: "reconciled",
      detail: "The exchange connection changed after the preview. Preview again.",
      riskDecision: decision
    });
    await appendAudit(userId, "assisted-order", "assisted.rejected", `${label(order)}: exchange connection changed since the preview.`);
    return { decision, order: changed, preview: null };
  }
  if (!env.liveAssistedTradingEnabled && client.provider === "coinbase") {
    const blocked = await updateAssistedOrder(order.id, {
      status: "blocked",
      reconcileState: "reconciled",
      detail: LIVE_DISABLED_DETAIL,
      riskDecision: decision
    });
    await appendAudit(userId, "assisted-order", "assisted.blocked", `${label(order)}: ${LIVE_DISABLED_DETAIL}`);
    return { decision, order: blocked, preview: null };
  }

  const claimed = await claimPreviewedOrder(order.id);
  if (!claimed) throw new Error("This order was already submitted.");

  try {
    const result = await client.createOrder({
      productId: order.productId,
      side: order.side,
      quoteUsd: order.quoteUsd,
      clientOrderId: order.id,
      previewId: order.previewId
    });
    const updated = await updateAssistedOrder(
      order.id,
      result.success
        ? { orderId: result.orderId, detail: result.detail, riskDecision: decision }
        : { status: "failed", reconcileState: "reconciled", detail: result.detail, riskDecision: decision }
    );
    await appendAudit(
      userId,
      "assisted-order",
      result.success ? "assisted.submitted" : "assisted.rejected",
      `${label(order)} via ${client.provider}: ${result.detail} Client order id ${order.id}.`
    );
    return { decision, order: updated, preview: null };
  } catch (error) {
    const normalized = normalizeExchangeError(error);
    const failed = await updateAssistedOrder(order.id, {
      status: "failed",
      reconcileState: "error",
      detail: `${normalized.message} Check Coinbase for client order id ${order.id} before retrying.`,
      riskDecision: decision
    });
    await appendAudit(userId, "assisted-order", "assisted.submit-failed", `${label(order)}: ${normalized.code}`);
    return { decision, order: failed, preview: null };
  }
}

/** Step 3: compares every pending order with the exchange and records the outcome. */
export async function reconcileAssisted(userId: string): Promise<ReconcileSummary> {
  const pending = await listPendingAssistedOrders(userId);
  const summary: ReconcileSummary = { checked: 0, filled: 0, closed: 0, open: 0, errors: 0 };
  const clients = new Map<ExchangeProvider, ExchangeClient>();

  for (const order of pending) {
    summary.checked += 1;
    if (!order.orderId) {
      await updateAssistedOrder(order.id, {
        reconcileState: "error",
        detail: `No exchange order id was recorded. Check Coinbase for client order id ${order.clientOrderId ?? order.id}.`
      });
      summary.errors += 1;
      continue;
    }
    try {
      let client = clients.get(order.provider);
      if (!client) {
        client = await clientForProvider(userId, order.provider);
        clients.set(order.provider, client);
      }
      const status = await client.getOrder(order.orderId);
      const reconciledAt = new Date().toISOString();
      const fillFields = { filledSize: status.filledSize, averagePrice: status.averagePrice, totalFees: status.totalFees };
      if (status.status === "FILLED") {
        await updateAssistedOrder(order.id, { status: "filled", exchangeStatus: status.raw, ...fillFields, reconcileState: "reconciled", reconciledAt });
        summary.filled += 1;
      } else if (status.status === "CANCELLED" || status.status === "EXPIRED" || status.status === "FAILED") {
        if (status.filledSize > 0) {
          // A market IOC that partially filled before closing still moved coins: record it as a fill.
          await updateAssistedOrder(order.id, {
            status: "filled",
            exchangeStatus: status.raw,
            ...fillFields,
            reconcileState: "reconciled",
            reconciledAt,
            detail: `Partially filled before the exchange closed the order (${status.raw}).`
          });
          summary.filled += 1;
        } else {
          await updateAssistedOrder(order.id, {
            status: status.status === "CANCELLED" ? "cancelled" : status.status === "EXPIRED" ? "expired" : "failed",
            exchangeStatus: status.raw,
            reconcileState: "reconciled",
            reconciledAt
          });
          summary.closed += 1;
        }
      } else if (status.status === "OPEN" || status.status === "PENDING") {
        await updateAssistedOrder(order.id, { exchangeStatus: status.raw, reconcileState: "pending" });
        summary.open += 1;
      } else {
        await updateAssistedOrder(order.id, {
          exchangeStatus: status.raw,
          reconcileState: "error",
          detail: `The exchange reported an unknown status (${status.raw}).`
        });
        summary.errors += 1;
      }
    } catch (error) {
      await updateAssistedOrder(order.id, { reconcileState: "error", detail: `Reconcile failed: ${normalizeExchangeError(error).message}` });
      summary.errors += 1;
    }
  }

  await appendAudit(
    userId,
    "operations",
    "assisted.reconciled",
    `Checked ${summary.checked}: ${summary.filled} filled, ${summary.closed} closed, ${summary.open} still open, ${summary.errors} errors.`
  );
  return summary;
}
```

```ts
import { mkdir, writeFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import path from "node:path";
import { env } from "@/lib/env";

/** Local fallback for exports when R2 is not configured. The key never escapes LOCAL_DATA_DIR. */
export async function writeLocalArtifact(key: string, content: string): Promise<{ storage: "local"; location: string }> {
  const root = path.resolve(env.localDataDir);
  const target = path.resolve(root, key);
  if (!target.startsWith(`${root}${path.sep}`)) throw new Error("Artifact path escapes LOCAL_DATA_DIR.");
  await mkdir(path.dirname(target), { recursive: true });
  await writeFile(target, content, "utf8");
  return { storage: "local", location: path.relative(process.cwd(), target).split(path.sep).join("/") };
}
```

```ts
import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
import { toCsv, type CsvCell } from "@/lib/domain/csv";
import { env } from "@/lib/env";
import { writeLocalArtifact } from "@/lib/server/artifacts";
import { uploadArtifactToR2 } from "@/lib/server/r2";
import { listAssistedOrders } from "@/lib/server/repos/assisted";
import { appendAudit, listAudit } from "@/lib/server/repos/audit";
import { insertExport } from "@/lib/server/repos/exports";
import { listPaperTrades } from "@/lib/server/repos/paper";
import type { ExportArtifact, ExportType } from "@/lib/types";

const EXPORT_ROW_LIMIT = 10_000;

async function buildRows(userId: string, type: ExportType): Promise<{ header: string[]; rows: CsvCell[][] }> {
  if (type === "paper-journal") {
    const trades = await listPaperTrades(userId, EXPORT_ROW_LIMIT);
    return {
      header: ["created_at", "product_id", "side", "base_size", "price", "quote_usd", "realized_pnl_usd", "status", "note", "risk_outcome", "risk_reasons"],
      rows: trades.map((t) => [
        t.createdAt,
        t.productId,
        t.side,
        t.baseSize,
        t.price,
        t.quoteUsd,
        t.realizedPnlUsd,
        t.status,
        t.note,
        t.riskDecision?.outcome ?? "",
        t.riskDecision?.reasons.join(" ") ?? ""
      ])
    };
  }
  if (type === "assisted-orders") {
    const orders = await listAssistedOrders(userId, EXPORT_ROW_LIMIT);
    return {
      header: ["created_at", "updated_at", "product_id", "side", "quote_usd", "status", "reconcile_state", "provider", "order_id", "client_order_id", "filled_size", "average_price", "total_fees", "detail"],
      rows: orders.map((o) => [
        o.createdAt,
        o.updatedAt,
        o.productId,
        o.side,
        o.quoteUsd,
        o.status,
        o.reconcileState,
        o.provider,
        o.orderId,
        o.clientOrderId,
        o.filledSize,
        o.averagePrice,
        o.totalFees,
        o.detail
      ])
    };
  }
  const events = await listAudit(userId, { limit: EXPORT_ROW_LIMIT });
  return {
    header: ["created_at", "category", "action", "detail"],
    rows: events.map((e) => [e.createdAt, e.category, e.action, e.detail])
  };
}

/** Builds a CSV, stores it in R2 when configured (local file otherwise), records and audits it. */
export async function createExport(userId: string, type: ExportType): Promise<ExportArtifact> {
  const { header, rows } = await buildRows(userId, type);
  const csv = toCsv(header, rows);
  const stamp = new Date().toISOString().replace(/[:.]/g, "-");
  const key = `exports/${type}-${stamp}-${randomUUID().slice(0, 8)}.csv`;
  const uploaded = env.r2Configured ? await uploadArtifactToR2(key, csv, "text/csv") : null;
  const stored = uploaded ?? (await writeLocalArtifact(key, csv));
  const artifact = await insertExport({ userId, type, storage: stored.storage, location: stored.location });
  await appendAudit(userId, "export", "export.created", `${type}: ${rows.length} rows -> ${stored.storage} ${stored.location}`);
  return artifact;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 10: lib/server/services/auto.ts**

```ts
import { env } from "@/lib/env";
import { appendAudit } from "@/lib/server/repos/audit";
import { getLimits } from "@/lib/server/repos/limits";
import { previewAssisted, submitAssisted } from "@/lib/server/services/assisted";
import { refreshSignals, sizeSignal } from "@/lib/server/services/signals";

export type AutoCycleSummary = {
  ran: boolean;
  reason: string;
  signals: number;
  attempted: number;
  submitted: number;
  blocked: number;
  failed: number;
  details: string[];
};

const empty = (reason: string): AutoCycleSummary => ({ ran: false, reason, signals: 0, attempted: 0, submitted: 0, blocked: 0, failed: 0, details: [] });

/**
 * Owner-only auto cycle: refresh signals, then for each long setup preview and submit a BUY of the
 * suggested size. Never runs unless ENABLE_AUTO_MODE and ENABLE_LIVE_ASSISTED_TRADING are both true.
 * Every order still passes the full risk engine twice (preview and submit).
 */
export async function runAutoCycle(userId: string, caller: { isOwner: boolean }): Promise<AutoCycleSummary> {
  if (!caller.isOwner) throw new Error("Auto mode is owner-only.");
  const refuse = async (reason: string): Promise<AutoCycleSummary> => {
    await appendAudit(userId, "auto", "auto.refused", reason);
    return empty(reason);
  };
  if (!env.autoModeEnabled) return refuse("Auto mode is disabled: ENABLE_AUTO_MODE is not true.");
  if (!env.liveAssistedTradingEnabled) return refuse("Auto mode never runs while live assisted trading is disabled (ENABLE_LIVE_ASSISTED_TRADING).");
  const limits = await getLimits(userId);
  if (limits.tradingPaused) return refuse("Trading is paused by the global kill switch.");

  const signals = await refreshSignals(userId);
  const summary: AutoCycleSummary = { ...empty("Cycle completed."), ran: true, signals: signals.length };
  for (const signal of signals.map((s) => sizeSignal(s, limits))) {
    if (signal.action !== "long" || signal.suggestedQuoteUsd <= 0) continue;
    summary.attempted += 1;
    const preview = await previewAssisted(userId, {
      productId: signal.productId,
      side: "BUY",
      quoteUsd: signal.suggestedQuoteUsd,
      mode: "live",
      signalId: signal.id
    });
    if (preview.order.status !== "previewed") {
      if (preview.order.status === "blocked") summary.blocked += 1;
      else summary.failed += 1;
      summary.details.push(`${signal.productId}: ${preview.order.status} at preview (${preview.order.detail})`);
      continue;
    }
    try {
      const result = await submitAssisted(userId, preview.order.id);
      if (result.order.status === "submitted") summary.submitted += 1;
      else if (result.order.status === "blocked") summary.blocked += 1;
      else summary.failed += 1;
      summary.details.push(`${signal.productId}: ${result.order.status} (${result.order.detail})`);
    } catch (error) {
      summary.failed += 1;
      summary.details.push(`${signal.productId}: submit failed (${error instanceof Error ? error.message : "unknown error"})`);
    }
  }

  await appendAudit(
    userId,
    "auto",
    "auto.cycle",
    `Signals ${summary.signals}, attempted ${summary.attempted}, submitted ${summary.submitted}, blocked ${summary.blocked}, failed ${summary.failed}. ${summary.details.join(" | ")}`
  );
  return summary;
}
```

```ts
import { env } from "@/lib/env";
import { pingDatabase } from "@/lib/server/db";

export type SystemStatus = {
  app: string;
  appEnv: string;
  realm: string;
  liveAssistedTradingEnabled: boolean;
  autoModeEnabled: boolean;
  services: {
    database: "connected" | "error";
    r2: "configured" | "missing";
    secretKey: "configured" | "missing";
  };
  checkedAt: string;
};

/** Readiness report. Contains no secrets: safe to serve unauthenticated. */
export async function getSystemStatus(): Promise<SystemStatus> {
  const databaseOk = await pingDatabase(2000);
  return {
    app: env.appName,
    appEnv: env.appEnv,
    realm: env.realm,
    liveAssistedTradingEnabled: env.liveAssistedTradingEnabled,
    autoModeEnabled: env.autoModeEnabled,
    services: {
      database: databaseOk ? "connected" : "error",
      r2: env.r2Configured ? "configured" : "missing",
      secretKey: env.secretKey ? "configured" : "missing"
    },
    checkedAt: new Date().toISOString()
  };
}
```

```ts
  }))

}));

describe("placePaperOrder", () => {

    vi.clearAllMocks();
    h.limits = { ...baseLimits };
  });

  it("persists a blocked trade carrying the decision when trading is paused", async () => {

    const trade = await placePaperOrder("u1", { productId: "BTC-USD", side: "BUY", quoteUsd: 100, mode: "paper" }, "test");

    expect(trade.status).toBe("blocked");
    expect(trade.riskDecision?.reasons.join(" ")).toMatch(/max position/);
  });

  it("fills an approved buy at the reference price", async () => {
    const trade = await placePaperOrder("u1", { productId: "BTC-USD", side: "BUY", quoteUsd: 100, mode: "paper" });
    expect(trade).toMatchObject({ status: "filled", baseSize: 1, price: 100, quoteUsd: 100, realizedPnlUsd: 0 });
    expect(trade.riskDecision?.outcome).toBe("approved");
  });
});
```
