```ts
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import type { ExchangeClient, OrderStatus } from "@/lib/exchange/types";
import { claimPreviewedOrder, updateAssistedOrder } from "@/lib/server/repos/assisted";
import { reconcileAssisted, submitAssisted } from "@/lib/server/services/assisted";
import type { AssistedOrder, RiskDecision } from "@/lib/types";

const approved: RiskDecision = { outcome: "approved", checks: [], reasons: [], evaluatedAt: "2026-10-09T12:00:00.000Z" };

function makeOrder(over: Partial<AssistedOrder> = {}): AssistedOrder {
  return {
    id: "3f2b8c4e-1d2a-4b5c-9e8f-0a1b2c3d4e5f",
    userId: "u1",
    productId: "BTC-USD",
    side: "BUY",
    quoteUsd: 100,
    status: "previewed",
    reconcileState: "pending",
    reconciledAt: null,
    provider: "coinbase",
    detail: "preview",
    orderId: null,
    clientOrderId: null,
    previewId: "pv-1",
    exchangeStatus: null,
    filledSize: null,
    averagePrice: null,
    totalFees: null,
    signalId: null,
    riskDecision: approved,
    createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    ...over
  };
}

function makeClient(provider: "coinbase" | "mock", statuses: Record<string, OrderStatus> = {}): ExchangeClient {
  return {
    provider,
    keyPermissions: vi.fn(async () => ({ canView: true, canTrade: true, canTransfer: false, portfolioUuid: null })),
    balances: vi.fn(async () => []),
    previewOrder: vi.fn(async () => ({ previewId: "pv", orderTotal: 100.6, commissionTotal: 0.6, bestBid: 99, bestAsk: 101, warnings: [] })),
    createOrder: vi.fn(async (input: { clientOrderId: string }) => ({
      success: true,
      orderId: "ex-1",
      clientOrderId: input.clientOrderId,
      detail: "Accepted."
    })),
    getOrder: vi.fn(async (orderId: string) => statuses[orderId] ?? { orderId, status: "UNKNOWN" as const, filledSize: 0, averagePrice: 0, totalFees: 0, raw: "?" })
  };
}

const h = vi.hoisted(() => ({
  env: { liveAssistedTradingEnabled: false },
  order: null as AssistedOrder | null,
  pending: [] as AssistedOrder[],
  client: null as ExchangeClient | null
}));

vi.mock("@/lib/env", () => ({ env: h.env }));
vi.mock("@/lib/server/repos/audit", () => ({ appendAudit: vi.fn(async () => undefined) }));
vi.mock("@/lib/server/services/risk", () => ({
  evaluateIntent: vi.fn(async () => ({ decision: { outcome: "approved", checks: [], reasons: [], evaluatedAt: "" }, context: {} }))
}));
vi.mock("@/lib/server/services/exchange-connection", () => ({
  getExchangeClient: vi.fn(async () => h.client),
  clientForProvider: vi.fn(async () => h.client),
  providerFor: vi.fn(async () => "coinbase")
}));
vi.mock("@/lib/server/repos/assisted", () => ({
  getAssistedOrder: vi.fn(async () => h.order),
  insertAssistedOrder: vi.fn(),
  listPendingAssistedOrders: vi.fn(async () => h.pending),
  claimPreviewedOrder: vi.fn(async (id: string) => (h.order ? { ...h.order, id, status: "submitted", clientOrderId: id } : null)),
  updateAssistedOrder: vi.fn(async (id: string, patch: Partial<AssistedOrder>) => ({
    ...(h.order ?? h.pending.find((o) => o.id === id)),
    ...patch,
    id
  }))
}));

describe("submitAssisted", () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    vi.clearAllMocks();
    h.env.liveAssistedTradingEnabled = false;
    h.order = makeOrder();
    h.pending = [];
  });

  it("refuses a Coinbase submit when live trading is disabled by environment", async () => {
    h.client = makeClient("coinbase");
    const result = await submitAssisted("u1", h.order!.id);
    expect(h.client.createOrder).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(vi.mocked(claimPreviewedOrder)).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(vi.mocked(updateAssistedOrder)).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      h.order!.id,
      expect.objectContaining({ status: "blocked", detail: "Live assisted trading is disabled by environment policy." })
    );
    expect(result.order.status).toBe("blocked");
  });

  it("still lets the mock provider submit with the row id as the client order id", async () => {
    h.order = makeOrder({ provider: "mock" });
    h.client = makeClient("mock");
    const result = await submitAssisted("u1", h.order.id);
    expect(vi.mocked(claimPreviewedOrder)).toHaveBeenCalledWith(h.order.id);
    expect(h.client.createOrder).toHaveBeenCalledWith(expect.objectContaining({ clientOrderId: h.order.id, previewId: "pv-1" }));
    expect(result.order.orderId).toBe("ex-1");
  });

  it("submits to Coinbase when live trading is enabled", async () => {
    h.env.liveAssistedTradingEnabled = true;
    h.client = makeClient("coinbase");
    await submitAssisted("u1", h.order!.id);
    expect(h.client.createOrder).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it("expires a preview older than two minutes", async () => {
    h.order = makeOrder({ createdAt: new Date(Date.now() - 3 * 60 * 1000).toISOString() });
    h.client = makeClient("coinbase");
    await expect(submitAssisted("u1", h.order.id)).rejects.toThrow("Preview expired, preview again.");
    expect(vi.mocked(updateAssistedOrder)).toHaveBeenCalledWith(h.order.id, expect.objectContaining({ status: "expired" }));
  });

  it("rejects another user's order", async () => {
    h.client = makeClient("coinbase");
    await expect(submitAssisted("someone-else", h.order!.id)).rejects.toThrow("Order not found.");
  });
});

describe("reconcileAssisted", () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    vi.clearAllMocks();
    h.order = null;
  });

  it("maps exchange statuses onto orders", async () => {
    h.pending = [
      makeOrder({ id: "o-filled", status: "submitted", orderId: "a" }),
      makeOrder({ id: "o-cancelled", status: "submitted", orderId: "b" }),
      makeOrder({ id: "o-open", status: "submitted", orderId: "c" }),
      makeOrder({ id: "o-unknown", status: "submitted", orderId: "d" })
    ];
    h.client = makeClient("coinbase", {
      a: { orderId: "a", status: "FILLED", filledSize: 0.001, averagePrice: 100000, totalFees: 0.6, raw: "FILLED" },
      b: { orderId: "b", status: "CANCELLED", filledSize: 0, averagePrice: 0, totalFees: 0, raw: "CANCELLED" },
      c: { orderId: "c", status: "OPEN", filledSize: 0, averagePrice: 0, totalFees: 0, raw: "OPEN" },
      d: { orderId: "d", status: "UNKNOWN", filledSize: 0, averagePrice: 0, totalFees: 0, raw: "UNKNOWN_ORDER_STATUS" }
    });

    const summary = await reconcileAssisted("u1");

    expect(summary).toEqual({ checked: 4, filled: 1, closed: 1, open: 1, errors: 1 });
    const update = vi.mocked(updateAssistedOrder);
    expect(update).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      "o-filled",
      expect.objectContaining({ status: "filled", filledSize: 0.001, averagePrice: 100000, totalFees: 0.6, reconcileState: "reconciled" })
    );
    expect(update).toHaveBeenCalledWith("o-cancelled", expect.objectContaining({ status: "cancelled", reconcileState: "reconciled" }));
    expect(update).toHaveBeenCalledWith("o-open", { exchangeStatus: "OPEN", reconcileState: "pending" });
    expect(update).toHaveBeenCalledWith("o-unknown", expect.objectContaining({ reconcileState: "error" }));
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 15: tests/unit/services-exchange.test.ts**

```ts
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { createCoinbaseClient } from "@/lib/exchange/coinbase";
import { appendAudit } from "@/lib/server/repos/audit";
import { saveConnection } from "@/lib/server/repos/exchange";
import { saveOnboarding } from "@/lib/server/repos/onboarding";
import { connectCoinbase, getExchangeClient } from "@/lib/server/services/exchange-connection";

const h = vi.hoisted(() => ({
  env: { secretKey: Buffer.alloc(32, 7).toString("base64") },
  canTransfer: false
}));

vi.mock("@/lib/env", () => ({ env: h.env }));
vi.mock("@/lib/exchange/coinbase", () => ({
  createCoinbaseClient: vi.fn(() => ({
    provider: "coinbase",
    keyPermissions: vi.fn(async () => ({ canView: true, canTrade: true, canTransfer: h.canTransfer, portfolioUuid: "pf-1" }))
  }))
}));
vi.mock("@/lib/server/repos/audit", () => ({ appendAudit: vi.fn(async () => undefined) }));
vi.mock("@/lib/server/repos/exchange", () => ({
  getConnection: vi.fn(async () => null),
  deleteConnection: vi.fn(async () => undefined),
  saveConnection: vi.fn(async (input: { userId: string; keyId: string }) => ({
    ...input,
    provider: "coinbase",
    validatedAt: "2026-10-09T12:00:00.000Z",
    createdAt: "2026-10-09T12:00:00.000Z"
  }))
}));
vi.mock("@/lib/server/repos/onboarding", () => ({
  getOnboarding: vi.fn(async (userId: string) => ({
    userId,
    preferredMode: "paper",
    riskAcknowledged: true,
    exchangeConnected: false,
    completedAt: null,
    updatedAt: ""
  })),
  saveOnboarding: vi.fn(async () => undefined)
}));
vi.mock("@/lib/server/services/market", () => ({ getReferencePrice: vi.fn(async () => 100) }));

describe("connectCoinbase", () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    vi.clearAllMocks();
    h.env.secretKey = Buffer.alloc(32, 7).toString("base64");
    h.canTransfer = false;
  });

  it("rejects a transfer-capable key and stores nothing", async () => {
    h.canTransfer = true;
    await expect(connectCoinbase("u1", "organizations/o/apiKeys/k", "pem")).rejects.toThrow(
      "This key can transfer funds. Kairis only accepts trade-only keys; create a new key without withdrawal/transfer permission."
    );
    expect(vi.mocked(saveConnection)).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(vi.mocked(appendAudit)).toHaveBeenCalledWith("u1", "exchange", "exchange.rejected", expect.stringContaining("transfer"));
  });

  it("refuses to run without KAIRIS_SECRET_KEY", async () => {
    h.env.secretKey = "";
    await expect(connectCoinbase("u1", "k", "pem")).rejects.toThrow("Exchange connections are disabled: KAIRIS_SECRET_KEY is not set");
    expect(vi.mocked(createCoinbaseClient)).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("seals the secret, saves the connection and marks onboarding connected", async () => {
    const connection = await connectCoinbase("u1", " organizations/o/apiKeys/k ", "line1\\nline2");
    expect(vi.mocked(createCoinbaseClient)).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ keyId: "organizations/o/apiKeys/k", secret: "line1\nline2" });
    const saved = vi.mocked(saveConnection).mock.calls[0]![0];
    expect(saved.sealed.ciphertext).not.toContain("line1");
    expect(saved).toMatchObject({ keyId: "organizations/o/apiKeys/k", canTrade: true, canTransfer: false, portfolioUuid: "pf-1" });
    expect(vi.mocked(saveOnboarding)).toHaveBeenCalledWith(expect.objectContaining({ exchangeConnected: true }));
    expect(connection).not.toHaveProperty("sealed");
  });
});

describe("getExchangeClient", () => {
  it("falls back to the mock provider when no key is connected", async () => {
    expect((await getExchangeClient("u1")).provider).toBe("mock");
  });
});
```

### Task 11: App shell, landing, dashboard, onboarding

**Files:**
- Modify: `app/globals.css` (append only; nothing above the new block changes)
- Rewrite: `app/layout.tsx`, `app/page.tsx`
- Create: `lib/format.ts`, `lib/search-params.ts`, `tests/unit/format.test.ts`
- Create: `components/mode-badge.tsx`, `components/app-nav.tsx`, `components/app-shell.tsx`, `components/portfolio-panel.tsx`
- Create: `app/app/layout.tsx`, `app/app/page.tsx`, `app/app/onboarding/page.tsx`, `app/app/onboarding/actions.ts`
- Create: `app/app/controls/actions.ts` (kill switch only; Task 13 rewrites the file with the limits action added)

UI rules from the PRD/PDD that every screen in Tasks 11-14 honours:
- The mode is visible on every execution-capable screen: `ModeBadge` reads PAPER or LIVE in text, so it does not rely on colour alone.
- Paper and live are visually distinct: `data-mode="paper"` sets the accent to sage, `data-mode="live"` to brass. The shell root carries the user's default mode; each execution page wraps itself in `<section className="workspace" data-mode=...>`, and the nearest `data-mode` wins.
- The kill switch is on the dashboard and on Controls, and a TRADING PAUSED banner sits under the header on every page while it is on.
- Risk is shown before any confirmation, blocked actions are explained in plain language, and no copy implies returns.

Why the onboarding gate is not in `app/app/layout.tsx`: App Router layouts do not re-render on client-side navigation, so a layout redirect could be clicked past. Each page calls `requireOnboarded()` (Task 9) instead; the layout only authenticates, upserts the `users` row and renders the shell.

- [ ] **Step 2: lib/format.ts**

```ts
const usdFormat = new Intl.NumberFormat("en-US", {
  style: "currency",
  currency: "USD",
  minimumFractionDigits: 2,
  maximumFractionDigits: 2
});

const missing = (value: number | null | undefined): value is null | undefined =>
  value === null || value === undefined || !Number.isFinite(value);

export function usd(value: number | null | undefined): string {
  return missing(value) ? "n/a" : usdFormat.format(value);
}

export function signedUsd(value: number): string {
  if (missing(value)) return "n/a";
  const absolute = usdFormat.format(Math.abs(value));
  if (value > 0) return `+${absolute}`;
  return value < 0 ? `-${absolute}` : absolute;
}

export function amount(value: number | null | undefined, maxDigits = 8): string {
  return missing(value) ? "n/a" : value.toLocaleString("en-US", { maximumFractionDigits: maxDigits });
}

export function pct(value: number | null | undefined, digits = 2): string {
  return missing(value) ? "n/a" : `${value.toFixed(digits)}%`;
}

/** UTC, minute precision: the server and the browser render identical text. */
export function when(isoTime: string | null | undefined): string {
  if (!isoTime) return "n/a";
  const date = new Date(isoTime);
  return Number.isNaN(date.getTime()) ? "n/a" : `${date.toISOString().slice(0, 16).replace("T", " ")} UTC`;
}
```

```ts
export type SearchParams = Promise<Record<string, string | string[] | undefined>>;

/** Next 16 hands pages a Promise of search params; each value may be repeated. Take the first. */
export function firstParam(value: string | string[] | undefined): string | undefined {
  return Array.isArray(value) ? value[0] : value;
}
```

```css
/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
```

```tsx
import type { Metadata } from "next";
import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import "./globals.css";

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  title: { default: "Kairis", template: "%s | Kairis" },
  description: "Disciplined, non-custodial crypto trading workflows with visible risk controls."
};

export default function RootLayout({ children }: Readonly<{ children: ReactNode }>) {
  return (
    <html lang="en">
      <body>{children}</body>
    </html>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import type { TradeMode } from "@/lib/types";

type ModeBadgeProps = { mode: TradeMode; label?: string; large?: boolean };

/** PAPER / LIVE in words (not colour alone), styled by data-mode. */
export function ModeBadge({ mode, label, large = false }: ModeBadgeProps) {
  return (
    <span className={large ? "mode-badge mode-badge-large" : "mode-badge"} data-mode={mode}>
      {label ? <span className="mode-badge-label">{label}</span> : null}
      <span aria-hidden="true" className="mode-badge-dot" />
      {mode === "paper" ? "PAPER" : "LIVE"}
    </span>
  );
}
```

```tsx
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { usePathname } from "next/navigation";

export type NavItem = { href: string; label: string };

export function AppNav({ items }: { items: NavItem[] }) {
  const pathname = usePathname();
  return (
    <nav className="app-nav" aria-label="Primary">
      {items.map((item) => {
        const active = item.href === "/app" ? pathname === "/app" : pathname.startsWith(item.href);
        return (
          <Link key={item.href} href={item.href} aria-current={active ? "page" : undefined}>
            {item.label}
          </Link>
        );
      })}
    </nav>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import Link from "next/link";
import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import { signOutAction } from "@/app/app/sign-out/actions";
import { AppNav, type NavItem } from "@/components/app-nav";
import { BrandMark } from "@/components/brand-mark";
import { ModeBadge } from "@/components/mode-badge";
import type { TradeMode } from "@/lib/types";

const NAV: NavItem[] = [
  { href: "/app", label: "Dashboard" },
  { href: "/app/signals", label: "Signals" },
  { href: "/app/paper", label: "Paper" },
  { href: "/app/trade", label: "Trade" },
  { href: "/app/controls", label: "Controls" },
  { href: "/app/journal", label: "Journal" },
  { href: "/app/exchange", label: "Exchange" },
  { href: "/app/reports", label: "Reports" }
];

type AppShellProps = {
  user: { name: string | null; email: string | null; isOwner: boolean };
  mode: TradeMode;
  paused: boolean;
  children: ReactNode;
};

export function AppShell({ user, mode, paused, children }: AppShellProps) {
  const items = user.isOwner ? [...NAV, { href: "/app/operations", label: "Operations" }] : NAV;
  return (
    <div className="app-shell" data-mode={mode}>
      <header className="app-header">
        <Link className="app-brand" href="/app">
          <span className="app-brand-mark">
            <BrandMark compact />
          </span>
          <span>Kairis</span>
        </Link>
        <AppNav items={items} />
        <div className="app-account">
          <ModeBadge mode={mode} label="Default" />
          <span>{user.name ?? user.email ?? "Signed in"}</span>
          <form action={signOutAction}>
            <button type="submit" className="cta-secondary">
              Sign out
            </button>
          </form>
        </div>
      </header>
      {paused ? (
        <div className="banner-halt" role="alert">
          <strong>TRADING PAUSED</strong>
          <span>The global kill switch is on. New paper and live orders are halted until you resume.</span>
          <Link href="/app/controls">Open controls</Link>
        </div>
      ) : null}
      <main className="app-main">{children}</main>
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import Link from "next/link";
import { ModeBadge } from "@/components/mode-badge";
import { amount, signedUsd, usd } from "@/lib/format";
import type { Portfolio } from "@/lib/server/services/portfolio";

type PortfolioPanelProps = { portfolio: Portfolio; title: string; closeHref?: (productId: string, notionalUsd: number) => string };

/** Today's stats plus the open positions for one mode. Live positions only include fills Kairis placed. */
export function PortfolioPanel({ portfolio, title, closeHref }: PortfolioPanelProps) {
  const rows = Object.entries(portfolio.positions);
  return (
    <article className="panel" data-mode={portfolio.mode}>
      <div className="panel-heading">
        <h2>{title}</h2>
        <ModeBadge mode={portfolio.mode} />
      </div>
      <div className="status-stack">
        <div className="status-row">
          <span>Trades today (UTC)</span>
          <strong>{portfolio.today.tradesCount}</strong>
        </div>
        <div className="status-row">
          <span>Realized P&amp;L today</span>
          <strong>{signedUsd(portfolio.today.realizedPnlUsd)}</strong>
        </div>
        <div className="status-row">
          <span>Consecutive losses</span>
          <strong>{portfolio.today.consecutiveLosses}</strong>
        </div>
        <div className="status-row">
          <span>Unrealized P&amp;L</span>
          <strong>{signedUsd(portfolio.unrealizedPnlUsd)}</strong>
        </div>
      </div>
      {rows.length === 0 ? (
        <p className="table-empty">No open positions.</p>
      ) : (
        <div className="table-wrap">
          <table className="table">
            <thead>
              <tr>
                <th>Product</th>
                <th className="num">Size</th>
                <th className="num">Avg cost</th>
                <th className="num">Mark</th>
                <th className="num">Notional</th>
                {closeHref ? <th /> : null}
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {rows.map(([productId, position]) => (
                <tr key={productId}>
                  <td>{productId}</td>
                  <td className="num">{amount(position.baseSize)}</td>
                  <td className="num">{usd(position.avgCost)}</td>
                  <td className="num">{usd(portfolio.marks[productId] ?? null)}</td>
                  <td className="num">{usd(position.notionalUsd)}</td>
                  {closeHref ? (
                    <td>
                      <Link className="inline-link" href={closeHref(productId, position.notionalUsd)}>
                        Sell
                      </Link>
                    </td>
                  ) : null}
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      )}
    </article>
  );
}
```

The `closeHref` prop is a function, so `PortfolioPanel` must only be rendered from server components (it is: dashboard, paper and trade pages). The Sell link carries a query string the paper and trade tickets prefill from.

- [ ] **Step 11: app/app/controls/actions.ts** (kill switch; Task 13 replaces this file with a superset)

```ts
"use server";

import { revalidatePath } from "next/cache";
import { appendAudit } from "@/lib/server/repos/audit";
import { getLimits, saveLimits } from "@/lib/server/repos/limits";
import { requireOnboarded } from "@/lib/server/session";

/** Global kill switch: halts every new paper and live order until toggled back. */
export async function togglePauseAction(): Promise<void> {
  const user = await requireOnboarded("/app/controls");
  const limits = await getLimits(user.id);
  const paused = !limits.tradingPaused;
  await saveLimits({ ...limits, tradingPaused: paused });
  await appendAudit(
    user.id,
    "limits",
    paused ? "trading.paused" : "trading.resumed",
    paused ? "Global kill switch turned on: new orders are halted." : "Global kill switch turned off."
  );
  revalidatePath("/app", "layout");
}
```

```tsx
import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import { AppShell } from "@/components/app-shell";
import { appendAudit } from "@/lib/server/repos/audit";
import { getLimits } from "@/lib/server/repos/limits";
import { getOnboarding } from "@/lib/server/repos/onboarding";
import { upsertUser } from "@/lib/server/repos/users";
import { requireUser } from "@/lib/server/session";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

export default async function AppLayout({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const user = await requireUser("/app");
  const record = await upsertUser({ id: user.id, email: user.email, displayName: user.name, isOwner: user.isOwner });
  if (record.inserted) {
    await appendAudit(user.id, "auth", "user.created", `First sign-in for ${user.email ?? user.id}.`);
  }
  const [limits, onboarding] = await Promise.all([getLimits(user.id), getOnboarding(user.id)]);
  return (
    <AppShell user={user} mode={onboarding.preferredMode === "paper" ? "paper" : "live"} paused={limits.tradingPaused}>
      {children}
    </AppShell>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 13: app/app/onboarding/actions.ts**

```ts
"use server";

import { revalidatePath } from "next/cache";
import { redirect } from "next/navigation";
import { appendAudit } from "@/lib/server/repos/audit";
import { getOnboarding, saveOnboarding } from "@/lib/server/repos/onboarding";
import { requireUser } from "@/lib/server/session";
import type { ExecutionMode } from "@/lib/types";

export async function saveOnboardingAction(formData: FormData): Promise<void> {
  const user = await requireUser("/app/onboarding");
  if (formData.get("riskAcknowledged") !== "yes") redirect("/app/onboarding?error=acknowledge");
  const raw = formData.get("preferredMode");
  // Auto is owner-only and never offered here.
  const preferredMode: ExecutionMode = raw === "manual" || raw === "assisted" ? raw : "paper";
  const current = await getOnboarding(user.id);
  await saveOnboarding({
    userId: user.id,
    preferredMode,
    riskAcknowledged: true,
    exchangeConnected: current.exchangeConnected,
    completedAt: current.completedAt ?? new Date().toISOString()
  });
  await appendAudit(user.id, "onboarding", "onboarding.saved", `Risk acknowledged; preferred mode ${preferredMode}.`);
  revalidatePath("/app", "layout");
  redirect(preferredMode === "paper" ? "/app/paper" : "/app");
}
```

```tsx
import type { Metadata } from "next";
import { firstParam, type SearchParams } from "@/lib/search-params";
import { getOnboarding } from "@/lib/server/repos/onboarding";
import { requireUser } from "@/lib/server/session";
import { saveOnboardingAction } from "./actions";

export const metadata: Metadata = { title: "Get started" };

export default async function OnboardingPage({ searchParams }: { searchParams: SearchParams }) {
  const user = await requireUser("/app/onboarding");
  const [onboarding, sp] = await Promise.all([getOnboarding(user.id), searchParams]);
  const missingAck = firstParam(sp.error) === "acknowledge";
  const mode = onboarding.preferredMode;

  return (
    <section className="workspace" data-mode="paper">
      <header className="workspace-header">
        <div>
          <p className="eyebrow">Get started</p>
          <h1>Before you trade</h1>
        </div>
      </header>

      <div className="grid">
        <article className="panel">
          <h2>What Kairis does</h2>
          <ul>
            <li>Evaluates BTC-USD, ETH-USD and SOL-USD with a published rule set and explains why each signal fired or did not.</li>
            <li>Checks every order against your limits before it can go anywhere, and tells you in plain words when it blocks one.</li>
            <li>Simulates fills in paper mode. In assisted mode it prepares an order on your Coinbase account and waits for your confirmation.</li>
            <li>Logs every evaluation, decision and order so you can review and export them.</li>
          </ul>
        </article>
        <article className="panel">
          <h2>What it never does</h2>
          <ul>
            <li>Hold your funds. Your money stays in your Coinbase account.</li>
            <li>Accept an exchange key that can withdraw or transfer funds. Create a key with View and Trade only.</li>
            <li>Place a live order without your explicit confirmation.</li>
            <li>Promise returns. Signals are context, not advice, and trading can lose money.</li>
          </ul>
        </article>
      </div>

      <form action={saveOnboardingAction} className="panel form-grid">
        <h2>Your starting mode</h2>
        <fieldset className="field">
          <legend>Preferred mode</legend>
          <label className="check">
            <input type="radio" name="preferredMode" value="paper" defaultChecked={mode === "paper" || mode === "auto"} />
            <span>
              <strong>Paper.</strong> Simulated fills at the Coinbase price, no exchange involved. Recommended for everyone first.
            </span>
          </label>
          <label className="check">
            <input type="radio" name="preferredMode" value="manual" defaultChecked={mode === "manual"} />
            <span>
              <strong>Manual.</strong> Review signals and risk here, then place orders yourself on Coinbase.
            </span>
          </label>
          <label className="check">
            <input type="radio" name="preferredMode" value="assisted" defaultChecked={mode === "assisted"} />
            <span>
              <strong>Assisted.</strong> Kairis prepares and risk-checks orders on your Coinbase account; you confirm each one.
            </span>
          </label>
        </fieldset>
        <label className="check">
          <input type="checkbox" name="riskAcknowledged" value="yes" required defaultChecked={onboarding.riskAcknowledged} />
          <span>
            I understand that crypto trading can lose money, that Kairis signals are rule-based context and not advice, and
            that I am responsible for every order I confirm.
          </span>
        </label>
        {missingAck ? (
          <p className="error-copy" role="alert">
            Tick the acknowledgment to continue.
          </p>
        ) : null}
        <div className="button-row">
          <button type="submit" className="cta-primary">
            Save and continue
          </button>
        </div>
      </form>
    </section>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import type { Metadata } from "next";
import Link from "next/link";
import { togglePauseAction } from "@/app/app/controls/actions";
import { ModeBadge } from "@/components/mode-badge";
import { PortfolioPanel } from "@/components/portfolio-panel";
import { usd, when } from "@/lib/format";
import { getLimits } from "@/lib/server/repos/limits";
import { getOnboarding } from "@/lib/server/repos/onboarding";
import { connectionStatus } from "@/lib/server/services/exchange-connection";
import { getPortfolio } from "@/lib/server/services/portfolio";
import { latestSignalsWithSizing } from "@/lib/server/services/signals";
import { requireOnboarded } from "@/lib/server/session";

export const metadata: Metadata = { title: "Dashboard" };

const MODE_LABEL = { paper: "Paper", manual: "Manual", assisted: "Assisted", auto: "Auto (owner)" } as const;

export default async function DashboardPage() {
  const user = await requireOnboarded("/app");
  const [limits, onboarding, paper, live, exchange] = await Promise.all([
    getLimits(user.id),
    getOnboarding(user.id),
    getPortfolio(user.id, "paper"),
    getPortfolio(user.id, "live"),
    connectionStatus(user.id)
  ]);
  const signals = (await latestSignalsWithSizing(limits)).slice(0, 3);
  const caps = Object.entries(limits.perSymbolMaxUsd);

  return (
    <>
      <header className="workspace-header">
        <div>
          <p className="eyebrow">Dashboard</p>
          <h1>Hello{user.name ? `, ${user.name}` : ""}</h1>
        </div>
        <ModeBadge mode={onboarding.preferredMode === "paper" ? "paper" : "live"} label={MODE_LABEL[onboarding.preferredMode]} large />
      </header>

      <div className="workspace-grid">
        <article className="panel kill-switch" data-paused={limits.tradingPaused ? "true" : "false"}>
          <h2>Kill switch</h2>
          <p className="panel-copy">
            {limits.tradingPaused
              ? "Trading is paused. Every new paper and live order is halted until you resume."
              : "Trading is active. Pausing halts every new paper and live order at once. Orders already at the exchange are not cancelled."}
          </p>
          <form action={togglePauseAction} className="button-row">
            <button type="submit" className={limits.tradingPaused ? "cta-secondary" : "cta-danger"}>
              {limits.tradingPaused ? "Resume trading" : "Pause all trading"}
            </button>
          </form>
        </article>

        <article className="panel">
          <div className="panel-heading">
            <h2>Your limits</h2>
            <Link className="inline-link" href="/app/controls">
              Edit
            </Link>
          </div>
          <div className="status-stack">
            <div className="status-row">
              <span>Max position</span>
              <strong>{usd(limits.maxPositionUsd)}</strong>
            </div>
            <div className="status-row">
              <span>Daily loss cap</span>
              <strong>{usd(limits.dailyLossCapUsd)}</strong>
            </div>
            <div className="status-row">
              <span>Max trades per day</span>
              <strong>{limits.maxTradesPerDay}</strong>
            </div>
            <div className="status-row">
              <span>Cooldown after {limits.lossStreakTrigger} losses</span>
              <strong>{limits.cooldownMinutes} min</strong>
            </div>
            <div className="status-row">
              <span>Per-symbol caps</span>
              <strong>{caps.length === 0 ? "None" : caps.map(([productId, cap]) => `${productId} ${usd(cap)}`).join(", ")}</strong>
            </div>
          </div>
        </article>
      </div>

      <div className="workspace-grid">
        <PortfolioPanel portfolio={paper} title="Paper today" />
        <PortfolioPanel portfolio={live} title="Live today" />
      </div>

      <div className="workspace-grid">
        <article className="panel">
          <div className="panel-heading">
            <h2>Latest signals</h2>
            <Link className="inline-link" href="/app/signals">
              Review all
            </Link>
          </div>
          {signals.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="table-empty">No signals yet. Open Signals and refresh.</p>
          ) : (
            <div className="signal-list">
              {signals.map((signal) => (
                <div className="status-row" key={signal.id}>
                  <span>
                    {signal.productId}: {signal.setup} ({when(signal.evaluatedAt)})
                  </span>
                  <span className="signal-action" data-action={signal.action}>
                    {signal.action}
                  </span>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </article>

        <article className="panel">
          <div className="panel-heading">
            <h2>Exchange</h2>
            <Link className="inline-link" href="/app/exchange">
              Manage
            </Link>
          </div>
          <p className="panel-copy">
            {exchange.connection
              ? `Coinbase key connected (trade ${exchange.connection.canTrade ? "allowed" : "not allowed"}, transfer not allowed). Assisted orders go to Coinbase.`
              : "No exchange connected. Assisted orders use the mock provider and never reach an exchange."}
          </p>
        </article>
      </div>
    </>
  );
}
```

### Task 12: Signals and paper trading

**Files:**
- Create: `lib/domain/order-form.ts`, `tests/unit/order-form.test.ts`
- Create: `components/risk-decision.tsx`, `components/signal-card.tsx`, `components/paper-ticket.tsx`

Signal links carry `product`, `quote` (the suggested size) and `signal` (the signal id) as query parameters; the paper and trade pages prefill their tickets from them and the ticket posts the signal id back so the order row records which signal it acted on.

- [ ] **Step 1: Failing test** `tests/unit/order-form.test.ts`

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { formText, parseOrderForm } from "@/lib/domain/order-form";

function form(entries: Record<string, string>): FormData {
  const data = new FormData();
  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(entries)) data.set(key, value);
  return data;
}

describe("parseOrderForm", () => {
  it("normalizes a valid ticket", () => {
    expect(
      parseOrderForm(
        form({ productId: " eth-usd ", side: "sell", quoteUsd: "25.5", note: " take profit ", signalId: "3f2b8c4e-1d2a-4b5c-9e8f-0a1b2c3d4e5f" })
      )
    ).toEqual({
      ok: true,
      intent: { productId: "ETH-USD", side: "SELL", quoteUsd: 25.5, signalId: "3f2b8c4e-1d2a-4b5c-9e8f-0a1b2c3d4e5f" },
      note: "take profit"
    });
  });
  it("drops a malformed signal id instead of failing", () => {
    const result = parseOrderForm(form({ productId: "BTC-USD", side: "BUY", quoteUsd: "10", signalId: "nope" }));
    expect(result.ok && result.intent.signalId).toBeNull();
  });
  it("rejects bad input with a plain reason", () => {
    expect(parseOrderForm(form({ productId: "bitcoin", side: "BUY", quoteUsd: "10" }))).toEqual({
      ok: false,
      error: "Enter a Coinbase product id such as BTC-USD."
    });
    expect(parseOrderForm(form({ productId: "BTC-USD", side: "HOLD", quoteUsd: "10" }))).toEqual({ ok: false, error: "Choose buy or sell." });
    expect(parseOrderForm(form({ productId: "BTC-USD", side: "BUY", quoteUsd: "-1" })).ok).toBe(false);
    expect(parseOrderForm(form({ productId: "BTC-USD", side: "BUY", quoteUsd: "abc" })).ok).toBe(false);
    expect(parseOrderForm(form({ productId: "BTC-USD", side: "BUY", quoteUsd: "0.001" })).ok).toBe(false);
    expect(parseOrderForm(form({ productId: "BTC-USD", side: "BUY", quoteUsd: "2000000" }))).toEqual({
      ok: false,
      error: "Order size is capped at $1,000,000 per ticket."
    });
  });
});

describe("formText", () => {
  it("trims strings and ignores missing fields", () => {
    expect(formText(form({ a: "  x  " }), "a")).toBe("x");
    expect(formText(form({}), "a")).toBe("");
  });
});
```

```ts
import { isUuid, normalizeProductId } from "@/lib/domain/ids";
import type { Side } from "@/lib/types";

export const MAX_TICKET_USD = 1_000_000;

/** A form field as trimmed text; files and missing fields read as "". */
export function formText(form: FormData, name: string): string {
  const value = form.get(name);
  return typeof value === "string" ? value.trim() : "";
}

export type OrderFormResult =
  | { ok: true; intent: { productId: string; side: Side; quoteUsd: number; signalId: string | null }; note: string }
  | { ok: false; error: string };

/** Shared parser for the paper and assisted tickets. Rounds the size to cents. */
export function parseOrderForm(form: FormData): OrderFormResult {
  const productId = normalizeProductId(formText(form, "productId"));
  if (!productId) return { ok: false, error: "Enter a Coinbase product id such as BTC-USD." };
  const sideText = formText(form, "side").toUpperCase();
  const side: Side | null = sideText === "BUY" ? "BUY" : sideText === "SELL" ? "SELL" : null;
  if (!side) return { ok: false, error: "Choose buy or sell." };
  const raw = Number(formText(form, "quoteUsd"));
  const quoteUsd = Number.isFinite(raw) ? Math.round(raw * 100) / 100 : Number.NaN;
  if (!Number.isFinite(quoteUsd) || quoteUsd <= 0) return { ok: false, error: "Order size must be a positive dollar amount." };
  if (quoteUsd > MAX_TICKET_USD) return { ok: false, error: "Order size is capped at $1,000,000 per ticket." };
  const signalText = formText(form, "signalId");
  return {
    ok: true,
    intent: { productId, side, quoteUsd, signalId: isUuid(signalText) ? signalText : null },
    note: formText(form, "note").slice(0, 500)
  };
}
```

```tsx
import type { RiskDecision } from "@/lib/types";

const TITLES = {
  approved: "All risk checks passed",
  blocked: "Blocked by your limits",
  halted: "Trading halted"
} as const;

/** Every check with PASS/FAIL in words, plus the plain-language reasons for any block. */
export function RiskDecisionView({ decision }: { decision: RiskDecision }) {
  return (
    <section className="risk-decision" data-outcome={decision.outcome} aria-label="Risk checks">
      <h3>{TITLES[decision.outcome]}</h3>
      {decision.reasons.length > 0 ? (
        <ul className="risk-reasons">
          {decision.reasons.map((reason) => (
            <li key={reason}>{reason}</li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      ) : null}
      <ul className="risk-checks">
        {decision.checks.map((check) => (
          <li className="risk-check" data-passed={check.passed ? "true" : "false"} key={check.code}>
            <span className="risk-check-mark">{check.passed ? "PASS" : "FAIL"}</span>
            <span>{check.detail}</span>
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
    </section>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import Link from "next/link";
import { pct, usd, when } from "@/lib/format";
import type { SizedSignal } from "@/lib/server/services/signals";

const ACTION_LABEL = { long: "Long setup", observe: "Observe", blocked: "Blocked" } as const;

export function SignalCard({ signal }: { signal: SizedSignal }) {
  const params = new URLSearchParams({ product: signal.productId, signal: signal.id });
  if (signal.suggestedQuoteUsd > 0) params.set("quote", signal.suggestedQuoteUsd.toFixed(2));
  return (
    <article className="signal-card">
      <div className="signal-header">
        <div>
          <h3>{signal.productId}</h3>
          <p className="signal-meta">
            <span>{signal.setup}</span>
            <span>Evaluated {when(signal.evaluatedAt)}</span>
          </p>
        </div>
        <span className="signal-action" data-action={signal.action}>
          {ACTION_LABEL[signal.action]}
        </span>
      </div>
      <p className="signal-meta">
        <span>Price {usd(signal.referencePrice)}</span>
        <span>Strength {signal.strength.toFixed(2)}</span>
        <span>RSI {signal.rsi ?? "n/a"}</span>
        <span>ATR {pct(signal.atrPct)}</span>
        <span>Spread {pct(signal.spreadPct, 3)}</span>
      </p>
      <ul className="signal-rationale">
        {signal.rationale.map((line) => (
          <li key={line}>{line}</li>
        ))}
      </ul>
      <p className="panel-copy">
        {signal.suggestedQuoteUsd > 0
          ? `Suggested size for your limits: ${usd(signal.suggestedQuoteUsd)} (a quarter of your daily loss cap divided by ATR, capped at your max position and any per-symbol cap).`
          : "No size suggested: this is not a long setup."}
      </p>
      <div className="button-row">
        <Link className="cta-secondary" href={`/app/paper?${params}`}>
          Paper trade
        </Link>
        <Link className="cta-secondary" href={`/app/trade?${params}`}>
          Assisted trade
        </Link>
      </div>
    </article>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import type { Metadata } from "next";
import { SignalCard } from "@/components/signal-card";
import { STRATEGY } from "@/lib/domain/strategy";
import { getLimits } from "@/lib/server/repos/limits";
import { latestSignalsWithSizing } from "@/lib/server/services/signals";
import { requireOnboarded } from "@/lib/server/session";
import { refreshSignalsAction } from "./actions";

export const metadata: Metadata = { title: "Signals" };

export default async function SignalsPage() {
  const user = await requireOnboarded("/app/signals");
  const limits = await getLimits(user.id);
  const signals = await latestSignalsWithSizing(limits);

  return (
    <section className="workspace">
      <header className="workspace-header">
        <div>
          <p className="eyebrow">Signal review</p>
          <h1>Signals</h1>
        </div>
        <form action={refreshSignalsAction}>
          <button type="submit" className="cta-primary">
            Refresh signals
          </button>
        </form>
      </header>

      <article className="panel">
        <h2>How a signal fires</h2>
        <p className="panel-copy">
          Hourly Coinbase candles. A long setup needs EMA {STRATEGY.emaFast} to cross above EMA {STRATEGY.emaSlow} within the
          last {STRATEGY.crossLookback} candles with RSI {STRATEGY.rsiPeriod} between {STRATEGY.rsiMin} and {STRATEGY.rsiMax}.
          A spread above {STRATEGY.maxSpreadPct}%, ATR above {STRATEGY.maxAtrPct}% of price, or stale data blocks the setup.
          Signals are context for your own decision, not advice, and they do not predict returns. Every evaluation is logged.
        </p>
      </article>

      {signals.length === 0 ? (
        <article className="panel">
          <p className="panel-copy">No signals evaluated yet. Refresh to evaluate the watchlist now.</p>
        </article>
      ) : (
        <div className="signal-list">
          {signals.map((signal) => (
            <SignalCard key={signal.id} signal={signal} />
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: app/app/paper/actions.ts**

```ts
"use server";

import { revalidatePath } from "next/cache";
import { parseOrderForm } from "@/lib/domain/order-form";
import { placePaperOrder } from "@/lib/server/services/paper";
import { requireOnboarded } from "@/lib/server/session";
import type { PaperTrade } from "@/lib/types";

export type PaperActionState = { status: "idle" | "done" | "error"; message: string; trade: PaperTrade | null };

export async function placePaperOrderAction(_prev: PaperActionState, formData: FormData): Promise<PaperActionState> {
  const user = await requireOnboarded("/app/paper");
  const parsed = parseOrderForm(formData);
  if (!parsed.ok) return { status: "error", message: parsed.error, trade: null };
  try {
    const trade = await placePaperOrder(user.id, { ...parsed.intent, mode: "paper" }, parsed.note);
    revalidatePath("/app/paper");
    revalidatePath("/app");
    const message =
      trade.status === "filled"
        ? `Filled: ${trade.side} ${trade.baseSize.toFixed(8)} ${trade.productId} at $${trade.price.toFixed(2)}.`
        : "Blocked by your limits before it could fill. The reasons are below, and the attempt is in your journal.";
    return { status: "done", message, trade };
  } catch (error) {
    return { status: "error", message: error instanceof Error ? error.message : "The paper order failed.", trade: null };
  }
}
```

```tsx
"use client";

import { useActionState } from "react";
import { placePaperOrderAction, type PaperActionState } from "@/app/app/paper/actions";
import { RiskDecisionView } from "@/components/risk-decision";
import { WATCHLIST } from "@/lib/domain/strategy";
import type { Side } from "@/lib/types";

const initialState: PaperActionState = { status: "idle", message: "", trade: null };

type PaperTicketProps = { defaultProduct: string; defaultSide: Side; defaultQuote: string; signalId: string | null };

export function PaperTicket({ defaultProduct, defaultSide, defaultQuote, signalId }: PaperTicketProps) {
  const [state, formAction, pending] = useActionState(placePaperOrderAction, initialState);
  return (
    <div className="form-grid">
      <form action={formAction} className="form-grid">
        <div className="field-row">
          <label className="field">
            <span>Product</span>
            <input name="productId" list="paper-watchlist" defaultValue={defaultProduct} required autoComplete="off" spellCheck={false} />
            <datalist id="paper-watchlist">
              {WATCHLIST.map((productId) => (
                <option key={productId} value={productId} />
              ))}
            </datalist>
            <span className="field-hint">A watchlist product or any Coinbase spot pair, such as AVAX-USD.</span>
          </label>
          <label className="field">
            <span>Size (USD)</span>
            <input name="quoteUsd" type="number" min="0.01" step="0.01" inputMode="decimal" defaultValue={defaultQuote} required />
          </label>
        </div>
        <fieldset className="field">
          <legend>Side</legend>
          <div className="button-row">
            <label className="check">
              <input type="radio" name="side" value="BUY" defaultChecked={defaultSide === "BUY"} required />
              <span>Buy</span>
            </label>
            <label className="check">
              <input type="radio" name="side" value="SELL" defaultChecked={defaultSide === "SELL"} />
              <span>Sell</span>
            </label>
          </div>
        </fieldset>
        <label className="field">
          <span>Note (optional)</span>
          <textarea name="note" maxLength={500} />
        </label>
        <input type="hidden" name="signalId" value={signalId ?? ""} />
        <div className="button-row">
          <button type="submit" className="cta-primary" disabled={pending}>
            {pending ? "Checking limits..." : "Check limits and place paper order"}
          </button>
        </div>
      </form>
      {state.status === "error" ? (
        <p className="error-copy" role="alert">
          {state.message}
        </p>
      ) : null}
      {state.trade ? (
        <div className="form-grid" aria-live="polite">
          <p className={state.trade.status === "filled" ? "success-copy" : "error-copy"}>{state.message}</p>
          {state.trade.riskDecision ? <RiskDecisionView decision={state.trade.riskDecision} /> : null}
        </div>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import type { Metadata } from "next";
import { ModeBadge } from "@/components/mode-badge";
import { PaperTicket } from "@/components/paper-ticket";
import { PortfolioPanel } from "@/components/portfolio-panel";
import { isUuid, normalizeProductId } from "@/lib/domain/ids";
import { amount, signedUsd, usd, when } from "@/lib/format";
import { firstParam, type SearchParams } from "@/lib/search-params";
import { listPaperTrades } from "@/lib/server/repos/paper";
import { getPortfolio } from "@/lib/server/services/portfolio";
import { requireOnboarded } from "@/lib/server/session";
import type { Side } from "@/lib/types";

export const metadata: Metadata = { title: "Paper trading" };

const sellLink = (productId: string, notionalUsd: number) =>
  `/app/paper?${new URLSearchParams({ product: productId, side: "SELL", quote: notionalUsd.toFixed(2) })}`;

export default async function PaperPage({ searchParams }: { searchParams: SearchParams }) {
  const user = await requireOnboarded("/app/paper");
  const sp = await searchParams;
  const product = normalizeProductId(firstParam(sp.product)) ?? "BTC-USD";
  const quoteNumber = Number(firstParam(sp.quote));
  const quote = Number.isFinite(quoteNumber) && quoteNumber > 0 ? quoteNumber.toFixed(2) : "";
  const side: Side = firstParam(sp.side)?.toUpperCase() === "SELL" ? "SELL" : "BUY";
  const signalParam = firstParam(sp.signal);
  const signalId = isUuid(signalParam) ? signalParam : null;
  const [portfolio, trades] = await Promise.all([getPortfolio(user.id, "paper"), listPaperTrades(user.id, 200)]);

  return (
    <section className="workspace" data-mode="paper">
      <header className="workspace-header">
        <div>
          <p className="eyebrow">Paper mode</p>
          <h1>Paper trading</h1>
        </div>
        <ModeBadge mode="paper" large />
      </header>
      <p className="notice">
        Simulated fills at the current Coinbase price. Nothing is sent to an exchange. Your limits and the kill switch apply
        exactly as they do for live orders.
      </p>

      <div className="workspace-grid">
        <article className="panel">
          <h2>New paper order</h2>
          <PaperTicket
            key={`${product}-${side}-${quote}-${signalId ?? ""}`}
            defaultProduct={product}
            defaultSide={side}
            defaultQuote={quote}
            signalId={signalId}
          />
        </article>
        <PortfolioPanel portfolio={portfolio} title="Paper positions" closeHref={sellLink} />
      </div>

      <article className="panel">
        <h2>Paper journal</h2>
        {trades.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="table-empty">No paper orders yet.</p>
        ) : (
          <div className="table-wrap">
            <table className="table">
              <thead>
                <tr>
                  <th>Time</th>
                  <th>Product</th>
                  <th>Side</th>
                  <th className="num">Size</th>
                  <th className="num">Price</th>
                  <th className="num">Value</th>
                  <th className="num">Realized P&amp;L</th>
                  <th>Status</th>
                  <th>Note or reason</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {trades.map((trade) => (
                  <tr key={trade.id}>
                    <td>{when(trade.createdAt)}</td>
                    <td>{trade.productId}</td>
                    <td>{trade.side}</td>
                    <td className="num">{amount(trade.baseSize)}</td>
                    <td className="num">{usd(trade.price)}</td>
                    <td className="num">{usd(trade.quoteUsd)}</td>
                    <td className="num">{trade.status === "filled" ? signedUsd(trade.realizedPnlUsd) : "n/a"}</td>
                    <td>
                      <span className={trade.status === "filled" ? "pill-ok" : "pill-bad"}>{trade.status}</span>
                    </td>
                    <td>{trade.status === "blocked" ? (trade.riskDecision?.reasons.join(" ") ?? trade.note) : trade.note}</td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        )}
      </article>
    </section>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: lib/domain/limits-form.ts**

```ts
import { normalizeProductId } from "@/lib/domain/ids";
import { formText } from "@/lib/domain/order-form";
import type { TradingLimits } from "@/lib/types";

export type LimitsInput = Omit<TradingLimits, "userId" | "updatedAt">;
export type LimitsFormResult = { ok: true; limits: LimitsInput } | { ok: false; errors: string[] };

const MAX_USD = 10_000_000;

/** Server-side validation for the limits form: every number finite and > 0, counts whole, caps below max position. */
export function parseLimitsForm(form: FormData): LimitsFormResult {
  const errors: string[] = [];

  const money = (name: string, label: string): number => {
    const value = Number(formText(form, name));
    if (!Number.isFinite(value) || value <= 0) {
      errors.push(`${label} must be a number greater than 0.`);
      return 0;
    }
    if (value > MAX_USD) {
      errors.push(`${label} must be at most $10,000,000.`);
      return 0;
    }
    return Math.round(value * 100) / 100;
  };

  const whole = (name: string, label: string, max: number): number => {
    const value = Number(formText(form, name));
    if (!Number.isInteger(value) || value <= 0) {
      errors.push(`${label} must be a whole number greater than 0.`);
      return 0;
    }
    if (value > max) {
      errors.push(`${label} must be at most ${max}.`);
      return 0;
    }
    return value;
  };

  const maxPositionUsd = money("maxPositionUsd", "Max position");
  const dailyLossCapUsd = money("dailyLossCapUsd", "Daily loss cap");
  const maxTradesPerDay = whole("maxTradesPerDay", "Max trades per day", 1000);
  const cooldownMinutes = whole("cooldownMinutes", "Cooldown minutes", 10080);
  const lossStreakTrigger = whole("lossStreakTrigger", "Loss streak trigger", 100);

  const symbols = form.getAll("capSymbol");
  const amounts = form.getAll("capUsd");
  const perSymbolMaxUsd: Record<string, number> = {};
  symbols.forEach((rawSymbol, index) => {
    const symbolText = typeof rawSymbol === "string" ? rawSymbol.trim() : "";
    const rawAmount = amounts[index];
    const amountText = typeof rawAmount === "string" ? rawAmount.trim() : "";
    if (!symbolText && !amountText) return;
    const symbol = normalizeProductId(symbolText);
    if (!symbol) {
      errors.push(`"${symbolText}" is not a product id such as SOL-USD.`);
      return;
    }
    const cap = Number(amountText);
    if (!Number.isFinite(cap) || cap <= 0) {
      errors.push(`The ${symbol} cap must be a number greater than 0.`);
      return;
    }
    if (maxPositionUsd > 0 && cap >= maxPositionUsd) {
      errors.push(`The ${symbol} cap must be below the max position.`);
      return;
    }
    if (perSymbolMaxUsd[symbol] !== undefined) {
      errors.push(`${symbol} is listed twice.`);
      return;
    }
    perSymbolMaxUsd[symbol] = Math.round(cap * 100) / 100;
  });

  const tradingPaused = formText(form, "tradingPaused") === "on";
  if (errors.length > 0) return { ok: false, errors };
  return {
    ok: true,
    limits: { maxPositionUsd, dailyLossCapUsd, maxTradesPerDay, cooldownMinutes, lossStreakTrigger, perSymbolMaxUsd, tradingPaused }
  };
}
```

```ts
"use server";

import { revalidatePath } from "next/cache";
import { parseLimitsForm } from "@/lib/domain/limits-form";
import { appendAudit } from "@/lib/server/repos/audit";
import { getLimits, saveLimits } from "@/lib/server/repos/limits";
import { requireOnboarded } from "@/lib/server/session";

export type LimitsActionState = { status: "idle" | "saved" | "error"; message: string; errors: string[] };

export async function saveLimitsAction(_prev: LimitsActionState, formData: FormData): Promise<LimitsActionState> {
  const user = await requireOnboarded("/app/controls");
  const parsed = parseLimitsForm(formData);
  if (!parsed.ok) return { status: "error", message: "Limits were not saved.", errors: parsed.errors };
  const saved = await saveLimits({ userId: user.id, ...parsed.limits });
  const caps =
    Object.entries(saved.perSymbolMaxUsd)
      .map(([productId, cap]) => `${productId} $${cap.toFixed(2)}`)
      .join(", ") || "none";
  await appendAudit(
    user.id,
    "limits",
    "limits.saved",
    `Max position $${saved.maxPositionUsd.toFixed(2)}, daily loss cap $${saved.dailyLossCapUsd.toFixed(2)}, ${saved.maxTradesPerDay} trades/day, ${saved.cooldownMinutes} min cooldown after ${saved.lossStreakTrigger} losses, caps: ${caps}, paused: ${saved.tradingPaused ? "yes" : "no"}.`
  );
  revalidatePath("/app", "layout");
  return { status: "saved", message: "Limits saved. They apply to the next paper or live order.", errors: [] };
}

/** Global kill switch: halts every new paper and live order until toggled back. */
export async function togglePauseAction(): Promise<void> {
  const user = await requireOnboarded("/app/controls");
  const limits = await getLimits(user.id);
  const paused = !limits.tradingPaused;
  await saveLimits({ ...limits, tradingPaused: paused });
  await appendAudit(
    user.id,
    "limits",
    paused ? "trading.paused" : "trading.resumed",
    paused ? "Global kill switch turned on: new orders are halted." : "Global kill switch turned off."
  );
  revalidatePath("/app", "layout");
}
```

```tsx
"use client";

import { useActionState, useState, type ChangeEvent } from "react";
import { saveLimitsAction, type LimitsActionState } from "@/app/app/controls/actions";
import type { TradingLimits } from "@/lib/types";

type Field = "maxPositionUsd" | "dailyLossCapUsd" | "maxTradesPerDay" | "lossStreakTrigger" | "cooldownMinutes";
type CapRow = { key: number; symbol: string; usd: string };

const FIELDS: Array<{ name: Field; label: string; hint: string; step: string }> = [
  { name: "maxPositionUsd", label: "Max position (USD)", hint: "The most you can hold in one product, counting the new order.", step: "0.01" },
  { name: "dailyLossCapUsd", label: "Daily loss cap (USD)", hint: "When today's realized losses reach this, new orders wait until tomorrow (UTC).", step: "0.01" },
  { name: "maxTradesPerDay", label: "Max trades per day", hint: "Per mode, per UTC day.", step: "1" },
  { name: "lossStreakTrigger", label: "Loss streak trigger", hint: "Consecutive losing sells that start a cooldown.", step: "1" },
  { name: "cooldownMinutes", label: "Cooldown (minutes)", hint: "How long new orders wait after the loss streak.", step: "1" }
];

const initialState: LimitsActionState = { status: "idle", message: "", errors: [] };

export function LimitsForm({ limits }: { limits: TradingLimits }) {
  const [state, formAction, pending] = useActionState(saveLimitsAction, initialState);
  const [values, setValues] = useState<Record<Field, string>>({
    maxPositionUsd: String(limits.maxPositionUsd),
    dailyLossCapUsd: String(limits.dailyLossCapUsd),
    maxTradesPerDay: String(limits.maxTradesPerDay),
    lossStreakTrigger: String(limits.lossStreakTrigger),
    cooldownMinutes: String(limits.cooldownMinutes)
  });
  const [paused, setPaused] = useState(limits.tradingPaused);
  const [rows, setRows] = useState<CapRow[]>(() => {
    const existing = Object.entries(limits.perSymbolMaxUsd).map(([symbol, cap], index) => ({ key: index, symbol, usd: String(cap) }));
    return existing.length > 0 ? existing : [{ key: 0, symbol: "", usd: "" }];
  });
  const [nextKey, setNextKey] = useState(() => Object.keys(limits.perSymbolMaxUsd).length + 1);

  const setField = (field: Field) => (event: ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const value = event.target.value;
    setValues((current) => ({ ...current, [field]: value }));
  };
  const updateRow = (key: number, field: "symbol" | "usd", value: string) =>
    setRows((current) =>
      current.map((row) => {
        if (row.key !== key) return row;
        return field === "symbol" ? { ...row, symbol: value } : { ...row, usd: value };
      })
    );
  const addRow = () => {
    setRows((current) => [...current, { key: nextKey, symbol: "", usd: "" }]);
    setNextKey((key) => key + 1);
  };
  const removeRow = (key: number) => setRows((current) => current.filter((row) => row.key !== key));

  return (
    <form action={formAction} className="form-grid">
      <div className="field-row">
        {FIELDS.map((field) => (
          <label className="field" key={field.name}>
            <span>{field.label}</span>
            <input
              name={field.name}
              type="number"
              min={field.step}
              step={field.step}
              inputMode="decimal"
              required
              value={values[field.name]}
              onChange={setField(field.name)}
            />
            <span className="field-hint">{field.hint}</span>
          </label>
        ))}
      </div>

      <fieldset className="field">
        <legend>Per-symbol caps</legend>
        <span className="field-hint">Optional. A tighter max position for one product; it must be below the overall max position.</span>
        {rows.map((row) => (
          <div className="field-row" key={row.key}>
            <input
              name="capSymbol"
              aria-label="Product id"
              placeholder="SOL-USD"
              autoComplete="off"
              value={row.symbol}
              onChange={(event) => updateRow(row.key, "symbol", event.target.value)}
            />
            <input
              name="capUsd"
              aria-label="Cap in USD"
              type="number"
              min="0.01"
              step="0.01"
              placeholder="200"
              value={row.usd}
              onChange={(event) => updateRow(row.key, "usd", event.target.value)}
            />
            <button type="button" className="cta-secondary" onClick={() => removeRow(row.key)}>
              Remove
            </button>
          </div>
        ))}
        <div className="button-row">
          <button type="button" className="cta-secondary" onClick={addRow}>
            Add a cap
          </button>
        </div>
      </fieldset>

      <label className="check">
        <input type="checkbox" name="tradingPaused" checked={paused} onChange={(event) => setPaused(event.target.checked)} />
        <span>Pause all trading (the global kill switch)</span>
      </label>

      <div className="button-row">
        <button type="submit" className="cta-primary" disabled={pending}>
          {pending ? "Saving..." : "Save limits"}
        </button>
      </div>

      {state.status === "saved" ? (
        <p className="success-copy" role="status">
          {state.message}
        </p>
      ) : null}
      {state.status === "error" ? (
        <div role="alert">
          <p className="error-copy">{state.message}</p>
          <ul className="risk-reasons">
            {state.errors.map((error) => (
              <li key={error}>{error}</li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        </div>
      ) : null}
    </form>
  );
}
```

```tsx
        </div>
      </header>
      <p className="panel-copy">
        Limits are protection, not friction: they stop the trades you would not make with a clear head. They apply to paper
        and live orders alike, every order is checked against them first, and every block tells you which limit stopped it.
      </p>

      <div className="workspace-grid">
        <article className="panel kill-switch" data-paused={limits.tradingPaused ? "true" : "false"}>
          <h2>Kill switch</h2>

              ? "Trading is paused. Every new paper and live order is halted."

          </p>
          <form action={togglePauseAction} className="button-row">
            <button type="submit" className={limits.tradingPaused ? "cta-secondary" : "cta-danger"}>

            <li>An unreachable exchange or market feed halts new orders.</li>
            <li>A sell cannot exceed the position Kairis recorded for that product.</li>
            <li>A single ticket is capped at $1,000,000.</li>

      </div>

      <article className="panel">

      </article>

}
```

```ts
"use server";

import { revalidatePath } from "next/cache";
import { formText } from "@/lib/domain/order-form";
import { normalizeExchangeError } from "@/lib/exchange/errors";
import { connectCoinbase, disconnect, SECRET_KEY_MISSING, TRANSFER_KEY_REJECTED } from "@/lib/server/services/exchange-connection";
import { requireOnboarded } from "@/lib/server/session";

export type ExchangeActionState = { status: "idle" | "connected" | "error"; message: string };

function revalidateExchangeViews(): void {
  revalidatePath("/app/exchange");
  revalidatePath("/app/trade");
  revalidatePath("/app");
}

export async function connectExchangeAction(_prev: ExchangeActionState, formData: FormData): Promise<ExchangeActionState> {
  const user = await requireOnboarded("/app/exchange");
  const keyId = formText(formData, "keyId");
  const secret = formText(formData, "secret");
  if (!keyId || !secret) return { status: "error", message: "Both the API key name and the private key are required." };
  try {
    const connection = await connectCoinbase(user.id, keyId, secret);
    revalidateExchangeViews();
    return {
      status: "connected",
      message: `Connected. View ${connection.canView ? "allowed" : "not allowed"}, trade ${connection.canTrade ? "allowed" : "not allowed"}, transfer not allowed.`
    };
  } catch (error) {
    const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : "";
    if (message === TRANSFER_KEY_REJECTED || message === SECRET_KEY_MISSING) return { status: "error", message };
    const normalized = normalizeExchangeError(error);
    return { status: "error", message: `Coinbase did not accept this key: ${normalized.message} ${normalized.recommendation}` };
  }
}

export async function disconnectExchangeAction(): Promise<void> {
  const user = await requireOnboarded("/app/exchange");
  await disconnect(user.id);
  revalidateExchangeViews();
}
```

```tsx
import type { Metadata } from "next";

import { ModeBadge } from "@/components/mode-badge";

          <li>Your account, your funds. Kairis never holds money; assisted orders run on your own Coinbase account after you confirm them.</li>
          <li>Encrypted at rest. The private key is sealed with AES-256-GCM using a server-side key and is never displayed again.</li>
```

```ts
"use server";

import { revalidatePath } from "next/cache";
import { isUuid } from "@/lib/domain/ids";
import { formText, parseOrderForm } from "@/lib/domain/order-form";
import type { OrderPreview } from "@/lib/exchange/types";
import { previewAssisted, reconcileAssisted, submitAssisted } from "@/lib/server/services/assisted";
import { requireOnboarded } from "@/lib/server/session";
import type { AssistedOrder, RiskDecision } from "@/lib/types";

export type PreviewState = {
  status: "idle" | "previewed" | "blocked" | "failed" | "error";
  message: string;
  order: AssistedOrder | null;
  decision: RiskDecision | null;
  preview: OrderPreview | null;
};

export type SubmitState = {
  status: "idle" | "submitted" | "blocked" | "failed" | "error";
  message: string;
  orderId: string | null;
  order: AssistedOrder | null;
};

export async function previewAssistedAction(_prev: PreviewState, formData: FormData): Promise<PreviewState> {
  const user = await requireOnboarded("/app/trade");
  const parsed = parseOrderForm(formData);
  if (!parsed.ok) return { status: "error", message: parsed.error, order: null, decision: null, preview: null };
  try {
    const result = await previewAssisted(user.id, { ...parsed.intent, mode: "live" });
    revalidatePath("/app/trade");
    const status: PreviewState["status"] =
      result.order.status === "previewed" ? "previewed" : result.order.status === "blocked" ? "blocked" : "failed";
    const message =
      status === "previewed"
        ? "Risk checks passed. Review the exchange preview, then confirm."
        : status === "blocked"
          ? "Blocked before reaching the exchange. The reasons are listed below."
          : result.order.detail;
    return { status, message, order: result.order, decision: result.decision, preview: result.preview };
  } catch (error) {
    return { status: "error", message: error instanceof Error ? error.message : "The preview failed.", order: null, decision: null, preview: null };
  }
}

export async function submitAssistedAction(_prev: SubmitState, formData: FormData): Promise<SubmitState> {
  const user = await requireOnboarded("/app/trade");
  const orderId = formText(formData, "orderId");
  if (!isUuid(orderId)) return { status: "error", message: "No previewed order to submit. Preview again.", orderId: null, order: null };
  if (formText(formData, "confirm") !== "yes") {
    return { status: "error", message: "Confirm that you reviewed the risk checks and the preview.", orderId, order: null };
  }
  try {
    const result = await submitAssisted(user.id, orderId);
    revalidatePath("/app/trade");
    revalidatePath("/app");
    const orderStatus = result.order.status;
    const status: SubmitState["status"] =
      orderStatus === "submitted" || orderStatus === "filled" ? "submitted" : orderStatus === "blocked" ? "blocked" : "failed";
    return { status, message: result.order.detail, orderId, order: result.order };
  } catch (error) {
    revalidatePath("/app/trade");
    return { status: "error", message: error instanceof Error ? error.message : "The submit failed.", orderId, order: null };
  }
}

export async function reconcileAction(): Promise<void> {
  const user = await requireOnboarded("/app/trade");
  await reconcileAssisted(user.id);
  revalidatePath("/app/trade");
  revalidatePath("/app");
}
```

```tsx
"use client";

import { useActionState } from "react";
import { previewAssistedAction, submitAssistedAction, type PreviewState, type SubmitState } from "@/app/app/trade/actions";
import { RiskDecisionView } from "@/components/risk-decision";
import { WATCHLIST } from "@/lib/domain/strategy";
import { usd } from "@/lib/format";
import type { Side } from "@/lib/types";

const initialPreview: PreviewState = { status: "idle", message: "", order: null, decision: null, preview: null };
const initialSubmit: SubmitState = { status: "idle", message: "", orderId: null, order: null };

type AssistedTicketProps = {
  provider: "coinbase" | "mock";
  liveSubmitEnabled: boolean;
  defaultProduct: string;
  defaultSide: Side;
  defaultQuote: string;
  signalId: string | null;
};

export function AssistedTicket({ provider, liveSubmitEnabled, defaultProduct, defaultSide, defaultQuote, signalId }: AssistedTicketProps) {
  const [previewState, previewAction, previewing] = useActionState(previewAssistedAction, initialPreview);
  const [submitState, submitAction, submitting] = useActionState(submitAssistedAction, initialSubmit);

  const order = previewState.order;
  const aboutThisOrder = order !== null && submitState.orderId === order.id;
  const submitted = aboutThisOrder && submitState.status !== "idle" && submitState.status !== "error";
  const submitError = aboutThisOrder && submitState.status === "error" ? submitState.message : null;
  const step = submitted ? 3 : previewState.status === "previewed" ? 2 : 1;

  return (
    <div className="form-grid">
      <ol className="steps" aria-label="Assisted order steps">
        <li aria-current={step === 1 ? "step" : undefined}>1. Check and preview</li>
        <li aria-current={step === 2 ? "step" : undefined}>2. Confirm</li>
        <li aria-current={step === 3 ? "step" : undefined}>3. Result</li>
      </ol>
      <p className="field-hint">
        {provider === "coinbase"
          ? "Orders go to your Coinbase account, and only after you confirm."
          : "No exchange connected: the mock provider is used and nothing is sent anywhere."}
      </p>

      <form action={previewAction} className="form-grid">
        <div className="field-row">
          <label className="field">
            <span>Product</span>
            <input name="productId" list="trade-watchlist" defaultValue={defaultProduct} required autoComplete="off" spellCheck={false} />
            <datalist id="trade-watchlist">
              {WATCHLIST.map((productId) => (
                <option key={productId} value={productId} />
              ))}
            </datalist>
          </label>
          <label className="field">
            <span>Size (USD)</span>
            <input name="quoteUsd" type="number" min="0.01" step="0.01" inputMode="decimal" defaultValue={defaultQuote} required />
          </label>
        </div>
        <fieldset className="field">
          <legend>Side</legend>
          <div className="button-row">
            <label className="check">
              <input type="radio" name="side" value="BUY" defaultChecked={defaultSide === "BUY"} required />
              <span>Buy</span>
            </label>
            <label className="check">
              <input type="radio" name="side" value="SELL" defaultChecked={defaultSide === "SELL"} />
              <span>Sell</span>
            </label>
          </div>
        </fieldset>
        <input type="hidden" name="signalId" value={signalId ?? ""} />
        <div className="button-row">
          <button type="submit" className="cta-primary" disabled={previewing}>
            {previewing ? "Checking..." : "Check risk and preview"}
          </button>
        </div>
      </form>

      {previewState.status === "error" ? (
        <p className="error-copy" role="alert">
          {previewState.message}
        </p>
      ) : null}

      {order ? (
        <section className="form-grid" aria-live="polite">
          <p className={previewState.status === "previewed" ? "success-copy" : "error-copy"}>
            {order.side} {order.productId} {usd(order.quoteUsd)}: {previewState.message}
          </p>
          {previewState.decision ? <RiskDecisionView decision={previewState.decision} /> : null}

          {previewState.preview ? (
            <div className="status-stack">
              <div className="status-row">
                <span>Provider</span>
                <strong>{order.provider === "coinbase" ? "Coinbase (real order)" : "Mock (nothing is sent)"}</strong>
              </div>
              <div className="status-row">
                <span>Estimated total</span>
                <strong>{usd(previewState.preview.orderTotal)}</strong>
              </div>
              <div className="status-row">
                <span>Commission</span>
                <strong>{usd(previewState.preview.commissionTotal)}</strong>
              </div>
              <div className="status-row">
                <span>Best bid / ask</span>
                <strong>
                  {usd(previewState.preview.bestBid)} / {usd(previewState.preview.bestAsk)}
                </strong>
              </div>
              {previewState.preview.warnings.map((warning) => (
                <div className="status-row" key={warning}>
                  <span>Warning</span>
                  <strong>{warning}</strong>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          ) : null}

          {step === 2 ? (
            <form action={submitAction} className="form-grid">
              <input type="hidden" name="orderId" value={order.id} />
              {order.provider === "coinbase" && !liveSubmitEnabled ? (
                <p className="notice notice-danger">
                  Live submission is disabled by environment policy. Confirming records a blocked attempt and sends nothing.
                </p>
              ) : null}
              <label className="check">
                <input type="checkbox" name="confirm" value="yes" required />
                <span>I reviewed the risk checks and the preview</span>
              </label>
              <p className="field-hint">
                The preview expires two minutes after it was created. A market order fills at the prevailing price, which can
                differ from the preview.
              </p>
              <div className="button-row">
                <button type="submit" className="cta-primary" disabled={submitting}>
                  {submitting ? "Submitting..." : order.provider === "coinbase" ? "Submit order to Coinbase" : "Submit mock order"}
                </button>
              </div>
              {submitError ? (
                <p className="error-copy" role="alert">
                  {submitError}
                </p>
              ) : null}
            </form>
          ) : null}

          {step === 3 && submitState.order ? (
            <div className="form-grid">
              <p className={submitState.status === "submitted" ? "success-copy" : "error-copy"} role="status">
                Status: {submitState.order.status}. {submitState.message}
              </p>
              <p className="field-hint">Use &quot;Reconcile now&quot; under recent orders to pull the fill from the exchange.</p>
            </div>
          ) : null}
        </section>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { amount, usd, when } from "@/lib/format";
import type { AssistedOrder } from "@/lib/types";

function statusClass(status: AssistedOrder["status"]): string {
  if (status === "filled") return "pill-ok";
  return status === "previewed" || status === "submitted" ? "pill-warn" : "pill-bad";
}

function reconcileClass(state: AssistedOrder["reconcileState"]): string {
  if (state === "reconciled") return "pill-ok";
  return state === "pending" ? "pill-warn" : "pill-bad";
}

export function AssistedOrdersTable({ orders }: { orders: AssistedOrder[] }) {
  if (orders.length === 0) return <p className="table-empty">No assisted orders yet.</p>;
  return (
    <div className="table-wrap">
      <table className="table">
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th>Time</th>
            <th>Product</th>
            <th>Side</th>
            <th className="num">Size</th>
            <th>Status</th>
            <th>Reconcile</th>
            <th>Provider</th>
            <th className="num">Filled</th>
            <th className="num">Avg price</th>
            <th className="num">Fees</th>
            <th>Detail</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          {orders.map((order) => (
            <tr key={order.id}>
              <td>{when(order.createdAt)}</td>
              <td>{order.productId}</td>
              <td>{order.side}</td>
              <td className="num">{usd(order.quoteUsd)}</td>
              <td>
                <span className={statusClass(order.status)}>{order.status}</span>
              </td>
              <td>
                <span className={reconcileClass(order.reconcileState)}>{order.reconcileState}</span>
              </td>
              <td>{order.provider}</td>
              <td className="num">{amount(order.filledSize)}</td>
              <td className="num">{usd(order.averagePrice)}</td>
              <td className="num">{usd(order.totalFees)}</td>
              <td>{order.detail}</td>
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
  );
}
```
