```tsx
import type { Metadata } from "next";
import Link from "next/link";
import { AssistedOrdersTable } from "@/components/assisted-orders-table";
import { AssistedTicket } from "@/components/assisted-ticket";
import { ModeBadge } from "@/components/mode-badge";
import { PortfolioPanel } from "@/components/portfolio-panel";
import { isUuid, normalizeProductId } from "@/lib/domain/ids";
import { env } from "@/lib/env";
import { firstParam, type SearchParams } from "@/lib/search-params";
import { listAssistedOrders } from "@/lib/server/repos/assisted";
import { connectionStatus } from "@/lib/server/services/exchange-connection";
import { getPortfolio } from "@/lib/server/services/portfolio";
import { requireOnboarded } from "@/lib/server/session";
import type { Side } from "@/lib/types";
import { reconcileAction } from "./actions";

export const metadata: Metadata = { title: "Assisted trade" };

const sellLink = (productId: string, notionalUsd: number) =>
  `/app/trade?${new URLSearchParams({ product: productId, side: "SELL", quote: notionalUsd.toFixed(2) })}`;

export default async function TradePage({ searchParams }: { searchParams: SearchParams }) {
  const user = await requireOnboarded("/app/trade");
  const sp = await searchParams;
  const product = normalizeProductId(firstParam(sp.product)) ?? "BTC-USD";
  const quoteNumber = Number(firstParam(sp.quote));
  const quote = Number.isFinite(quoteNumber) && quoteNumber > 0 ? quoteNumber.toFixed(2) : "";
  const side: Side = firstParam(sp.side)?.toUpperCase() === "SELL" ? "SELL" : "BUY";
  const signalParam = firstParam(sp.signal);
  const signalId = isUuid(signalParam) ? signalParam : null;
  const [status, orders, portfolio] = await Promise.all([
    connectionStatus(user.id),
    listAssistedOrders(user.id, 50),
    getPortfolio(user.id, "live")
  ]);

  return (
    <section className="workspace" data-mode="live">
      <header className="workspace-header">
        <div>
          <p className="eyebrow">Live assisted mode</p>
          <h1>Assisted trade</h1>
        </div>
        <ModeBadge mode="live" large />
      </header>

      {!env.liveAssistedTradingEnabled ? (
        <p className="notice notice-danger" role="note">
          <strong>Live submission is disabled by environment policy.</strong> Risk checks and Coinbase previews work, but
          submitting a Coinbase order is blocked and recorded until the operator sets ENABLE_LIVE_ASSISTED_TRADING=true. Mock
          orders still go through.
        </p>
      ) : null}
      <p className="notice">
        {status.connection ? (
          "Orders go to your Coinbase account after you confirm. Kairis tracks only the positions it placed, so sells are limited to those."
        ) : (
          <>
            No exchange is connected, so previews and orders use the mock provider and nothing reaches an exchange.{" "}
            <Link className="inline-link" href="/app/exchange">
              Connect Coinbase
            </Link>
          </>
        )}
      </p>

      <div className="workspace-grid">
        <article className="panel">
          <h2>New assisted order</h2>
          <AssistedTicket
            key={`${product}-${side}-${quote}-${signalId ?? ""}`}
            provider={status.provider}
            liveSubmitEnabled={env.liveAssistedTradingEnabled}
            defaultProduct={product}
            defaultSide={side}
            defaultQuote={quote}
            signalId={signalId}
          />
        </article>
        <PortfolioPanel portfolio={portfolio} title="Live positions (Kairis fills)" closeHref={sellLink} />
      </div>

      <article className="panel">
        <div className="panel-heading">
          <h2>Recent assisted orders</h2>
          <form action={reconcileAction}>
            <button type="submit" className="cta-secondary">
              Reconcile now
            </button>
          </form>
        </div>
        <AssistedOrdersTable orders={orders} />
      </article>
    </section>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: lib/domain/journal.ts**

```ts
import type { AuditEvent, TradeMode } from "@/lib/types";

type Classifiable = Pick<AuditEvent, "category" | "detail">;

/** Paper or live for trade-related events; null for mode-neutral ones (limits, auth, exports...). */
export function auditMode(event: Classifiable): TradeMode | null {
  if (event.category === "paper-trade") return "paper";
  if (event.category === "assisted-order" || event.category === "exchange" || event.category === "auto") return "live";
  if (event.category === "risk") {
    // Risk details start with the intent's mode (see describeDecision in lib/server/services/risk.ts).
    if (event.detail.startsWith("paper ")) return "paper";
    if (event.detail.startsWith("live ")) return "live";
  }
  return null;
}

export function filterAuditByMode<T extends Classifiable>(events: ReadonlyArray<T>, mode: TradeMode | "all"): T[] {
  return mode === "all" ? [...events] : events.filter((event) => auditMode(event) === mode);
}
```

```tsx
import type { Metadata } from "next";
import Link from "next/link";
import { ModeBadge } from "@/components/mode-badge";
import { auditMode, filterAuditByMode } from "@/lib/domain/journal";
import { when } from "@/lib/format";
import { firstParam, type SearchParams } from "@/lib/search-params";
import { AUDIT_CATEGORIES, listAudit } from "@/lib/server/repos/audit";
import { requireOnboarded } from "@/lib/server/session";
import type { TradeMode } from "@/lib/types";

export const metadata: Metadata = { title: "Journal" };

export default async function JournalPage({ searchParams }: { searchParams: SearchParams }) {
  const user = await requireOnboarded("/app/journal");
  const sp = await searchParams;
  const categoryParam = firstParam(sp.category);
  const category = AUDIT_CATEGORIES.find((value) => value === categoryParam);
  const modeParam = firstParam(sp.mode);
  const mode: TradeMode | "all" = modeParam === "paper" || modeParam === "live" ? modeParam : "all";
  const events = filterAuditByMode(await listAudit(user.id, { category, limit: 500 }), mode);

  return (
    <section className="workspace">
      <header className="workspace-header">
        <div>
          <p className="eyebrow">Records</p>
          <h1>Journal</h1>
        </div>
      </header>
      <p className="panel-copy">
        Every signal refresh, risk decision, approval, order, fill, failure, limit change and export, newest first. The last
        500 events matching the filter are shown; Reports exports the full history.
      </p>

      <form method="get" className="panel field-row" aria-label="Filter the journal">
        <label className="field">
          <span>Category</span>
          <select name="category" defaultValue={category ?? ""}>
            <option value="">All categories</option>
            {AUDIT_CATEGORIES.map((value) => (
              <option key={value} value={value}>
                {value}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </label>
        <label className="field">
          <span>Mode</span>
          <select name="mode" defaultValue={mode}>
            <option value="all">Paper and live</option>
            <option value="paper">Paper only</option>
            <option value="live">Live only</option>
          </select>
        </label>
        <div className="button-row">
          <button type="submit" className="cta-secondary">
            Filter
          </button>
          <Link className="inline-link" href="/app/journal">
            Clear
          </Link>
        </div>
      </form>

      <article className="panel">
        {events.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="table-empty">No events match this filter.</p>
        ) : (
          <div className="table-wrap">
            <table className="table">
              <thead>
                <tr>
                  <th>Time</th>
                  <th>Mode</th>
                  <th>Category</th>
                  <th>Action</th>
                  <th>Detail</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {events.map((event) => {
                  const eventMode = auditMode(event);
                  return (
                    <tr key={event.id}>
                      <td>{when(event.createdAt)}</td>
                      <td>{eventMode ? <ModeBadge mode={eventMode} /> : "n/a"}</td>
                      <td>{event.category}</td>
                      <td>{event.action}</td>
                      <td>{event.detail}</td>
                    </tr>
                  );
                })}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        )}
      </article>
    </section>
  );
}
```

```ts
"use server";

import { revalidatePath } from "next/cache";
import { createExport } from "@/lib/server/services/exports";
import { requireOnboarded } from "@/lib/server/session";
import type { ExportType } from "@/lib/types";

export async function createExportAction(formData: FormData): Promise<void> {
  const user = await requireOnboarded("/app/reports");
  const raw = formData.get("type");
  const type: ExportType | null = raw === "paper-journal" || raw === "assisted-orders" || raw === "audit-log" ? raw : null;
  if (!type) return;
  await createExport(user.id, type);
  revalidatePath("/app/reports");
}
```

```tsx
  { type: "paper-journal", title: "Paper journal", body: "Every paper order, filled or blocked, with the risk outcome and reasons." },
  { type: "assisted-orders", title: "Assisted orders", body: "Live and mock assisted orders with status, exchange ids, fills and fees." },
  { type: "audit-log", title: "Audit log", body: "Every recorded event: signals, risk decisions, approvals, limits, exchange and exports." }
];

            <div className="button-row">

        ) : (
          <div className="table-wrap">
            <table className="table">
              <thead>
                <tr>
                  <th>Created</th>
                  <th>Type</th>
                  <th>Storage</th>
                  <th>Location</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {exports.map((artifact) => (
                  <tr key={artifact.id}>
                    <td>{when(artifact.createdAt)}</td>
                    <td>{artifact.type}</td>
                    <td>{artifact.storage}</td>
                    <td>
                      {artifact.storage === "r2" && artifact.location.startsWith("https://") ? (
                        <a className="inline-link" href={artifact.location} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">
                          Download CSV
                        </a>
                      ) : (
                        <code>{artifact.location}</code>
                      )}
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        )}
      </article>
    </section>
  );
}
```

The download link is an external R2 URL, so a plain `<a>` is right here. It exists only when `R2_PUBLIC_URL` is set (Task 10's `uploadArtifactToR2` builds an `https://` location from it); otherwise the row shows the `r2://` key or the local path.

```ts
"use server";

import { revalidatePath } from "next/cache";
import { reconcileAssisted } from "@/lib/server/services/assisted";
import { runAutoCycle } from "@/lib/server/services/auto";
import { requireUser, type SessionUser } from "@/lib/server/session";

async function requireOwnerForAction(): Promise<SessionUser> {
  const user = await requireUser("/app/operations");
  if (!user.isOwner) throw new Error("Operations are owner-only.");
  return user;
}

/** Reconciles the owner's own pending orders. Other users reconcile theirs from /app/trade. */
export async function reconcileAllAction(): Promise<void> {
  const owner = await requireOwnerForAction();
  await reconcileAssisted(owner.id);
  revalidatePath("/app/operations");
}

/** The summary lands in the audit log (category "auto"), which this page lists. */
export async function runAutoCycleAction(): Promise<void> {
  const owner = await requireOwnerForAction();
  await runAutoCycle(owner.id, { isOwner: owner.isOwner });
  revalidatePath("/app/operations");
}
```

```tsx
import type { Metadata } from "next";
import { when } from "@/lib/format";
import { env } from "@/lib/env";
import { listPendingAssistedOrders } from "@/lib/server/repos/assisted";
import { listAudit } from "@/lib/server/repos/audit";
import { countSignalsLastDay } from "@/lib/server/repos/signals";
import { countUsers } from "@/lib/server/repos/users";
import { requireOwner } from "@/lib/server/session";
import { getSystemStatus } from "@/lib/server/system-status";
import { reconcileAllAction, runAutoCycleAction } from "./actions";

export const metadata: Metadata = { title: "Operations" };

export default async function OperationsPage() {
  const owner = await requireOwner();
  const [status, users, signalsLastDay, pending, autoEvents, operationsEvents] = await Promise.all([
    getSystemStatus(),
    countUsers(),
    countSignalsLastDay(),
    listPendingAssistedOrders(owner.id),
    listAudit(owner.id, { category: "auto", limit: 5 }),
    listAudit(owner.id, { category: "operations", limit: 5 })
  ]);
  const autoReady = env.autoModeEnabled && env.liveAssistedTradingEnabled;
  const recent = [...autoEvents, ...operationsEvents].sort((a, b) => b.createdAt.localeCompare(a.createdAt)).slice(0, 8);

  return (
    <section className="workspace">
      <header className="workspace-header">
        <div>
          <p className="eyebrow">Owner only</p>
          <h1>Operations</h1>
        </div>
      </header>

      <div className="metric-grid">
        <article className="metric-card">
          <p className="metric-label">Database</p>
          <p className={status.services.database === "connected" ? "metric-value metric-sage" : "metric-value metric-brass"}>
            {status.services.database}
          </p>
        </article>
        <article className="metric-card">
          <p className="metric-label">Users</p>
          <p className="metric-value metric-light">{users}</p>
        </article>
        <article className="metric-card">
          <p className="metric-label">Signals, last 24 h</p>
          <p className="metric-value metric-light">{signalsLastDay}</p>
        </article>
        <article className="metric-card">
          <p className="metric-label">Your pending orders</p>
          <p className="metric-value metric-light">{pending.length}</p>
        </article>
      </div>

      <div className="workspace-grid">
        <article className="panel">
          <h2>System status</h2>
          <div className="status-stack">
            <div className="status-row">
              <span>Environment</span>
              <strong>{status.appEnv}</strong>
            </div>
            <div className="status-row">
              <span>Realm</span>
              <strong>{status.realm}</strong>
            </div>
            <div className="status-row">
              <span>Live assisted submission</span>
              <strong>{status.liveAssistedTradingEnabled ? "enabled" : "disabled"}</strong>
            </div>
            <div className="status-row">
              <span>Auto mode</span>
              <strong>{status.autoModeEnabled ? "enabled" : "disabled"}</strong>
            </div>
            <div className="status-row">
              <span>Exchange key encryption</span>
              <strong>{status.services.secretKey}</strong>
            </div>
            <div className="status-row">
              <span>R2 exports</span>
              <strong>{status.services.r2}</strong>
            </div>
            <div className="status-row">
              <span>Checked</span>
              <strong>{when(status.checkedAt)}</strong>
            </div>
          </div>
        </article>

        <article className="panel">
          <h2>Actions</h2>
          <form action={reconcileAllAction} className="form-grid">
            <p className="panel-copy">Compare your {pending.length} pending assisted order(s) with the exchange and record fills.</p>
            <div className="button-row">
              <button type="submit" className="cta-secondary">
                Reconcile all pending
              </button>
            </div>
          </form>
          <form action={runAutoCycleAction} className="form-grid">
            <p className="panel-copy">
              {autoReady
                ? "Refresh signals and, for each long setup, preview and submit a BUY of the suggested size. Every order passes the full risk engine twice and respects the kill switch."
                : "Disabled. The auto cycle only runs when both ENABLE_AUTO_MODE and ENABLE_LIVE_ASSISTED_TRADING are true in the server environment, and only for the owner."}
            </p>
            <div className="button-row">
              <button type="submit" className="cta-primary" disabled={!autoReady}>
                Run auto cycle
              </button>
            </div>
          </form>
        </article>
      </div>

      <article className="panel">
        <h2>Recent auto and operations events</h2>
        {recent.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="table-empty">Nothing yet.</p>
        ) : (
          <div className="table-wrap">
            <table className="table">
              <thead>
                <tr>
                  <th>Time</th>
                  <th>Action</th>
                  <th>Detail</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {recent.map((event) => (
                  <tr key={event.id}>
                    <td>{when(event.createdAt)}</td>
                    <td>{event.action}</td>
                    <td>{event.detail}</td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        )}
      </article>
    </section>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 8: Verify**

Run: `npx vitest run && npm run typecheck && npm run lint && npm run build` -> all pass.

Run `npm run dev`, then `curl -s http://localhost:3000/api/system/status` -> JSON with `"services":{"database":"connected",...}` while the compose DB runs; stop it with `npm run db:down` and repeat -> `"database":"error"` within about 2 s; `npm run db:up` again.

Manual (signed in): `/app/journal` lists events; filter category `risk` + mode `paper` shows only paper risk decisions; "Clear" resets. `/app/reports`: each "Create export" adds a row whose location is `.local-data/exports/<type>-<timestamp>-<id>.csv` (no R2 locally), and the file exists on disk. `/app/operations` with your email in `KAIRIS_OWNER_EMAILS` renders status, counts and actions, with "Run auto cycle" disabled; signed in as a non-owner (or with the variable empty) it returns 404 and the nav has no Operations link.

```bash
git add lib/domain/journal.ts tests/unit/journal.test.ts app/app/journal app/app/reports app/app/operations
git commit -m "feat(ui): filterable journal, CSV reports, owner operations with reconcile and gated auto cycle"
```

---

### Task 15: Production infra (Qavren hub, ECR, OIDC, Cloudflare tunnel)

**Files:**
- Create: `infra/main.tf`, `infra/variables.tf`, `infra/ecr.tf`, `infra/iam.tf`, `infra/cloudflare.tf`, `infra/outputs.tf`, `infra/terraform.tfvars.example`, `infra/README.md`
- Create: `compose.prod.yaml`, `.github/workflows/deploy-prod.yml`
- Rewrite: `Dockerfile`, `.dockerignore`, `.github/workflows/ci.yml`
- Unchanged: `.github/branch-protection.json`

Mirrors fairsquare (`C:\Users\steve\projects\fairsquare\infra`, `compose.prod.yaml`, `.github/workflows/deploy-prod.yml`) minus the EC2 instance and instance role (Kairis runs on the shared hub `Qavren-Web-Server`, never a box of its own), SES, email routing, the `www` record and the Stripe ingress rule. State is local and gitignored (`infra/.terraform/`, `infra/*.tfstate*`, `infra/*.tfplan`, `infra/terraform.tfvars` are already in `.gitignore` from Task 1). The agent writes these files and runs `terraform fmt`/`validate`; `terraform apply` is an owner step (Task 16 `docs/deployment.md`).

- [ ] **Step 1: infra/main.tf**

```hcl
terraform {
  required_version = ">= 1.6"

  required_providers {
    aws = {
      source  = "hashicorp/aws"
      version = "~> 5.0"
    }
    cloudflare = {
      source  = "cloudflare/cloudflare"
      version = "~> 4.0"
    }
    random = {
      source  = "hashicorp/random"
      version = "~> 3.0"
    }
  }

  # Local state, gitignored, matching the fleet pattern (fairsquare, undertow-engine).
  backend "local" {}
}

provider "aws" {
  region = var.aws_region
}

provider "cloudflare" {
  api_token = var.cloudflare_api_token
}

data "aws_caller_identity" "current" {}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: infra/variables.tf**

```hcl
variable "aws_region" {
  description = "AWS region"
  type        = string
  default     = "us-east-1"
}

variable "app" {
  description = "App slug; used for resource names and the app-<name> tag the deploy workflow targets"
  type        = string
  default     = "kairis"
}

variable "hostname" {
  description = "Public hostname served through the Cloudflare tunnel"
  type        = string
  default     = "kairis.qavrensolutions.com"
}

variable "dns_record_name" {
  description = "Record name inside the qavrensolutions.com zone (the hostname without the apex)"
  type        = string
  default     = "kairis"
}

variable "cloudflare_api_token" {
  description = "Cloudflare API token: Zone:DNS:Edit and Zone:Zone:Read on qavrensolutions.com, Account:Cloudflare Tunnel:Edit"
  type        = string
  sensitive   = true
}

variable "cloudflare_account_id" {
  description = "Cloudflare account ID"
  type        = string
}

variable "cloudflare_zone_id" {
  description = "Zone ID of qavrensolutions.com (zone overview page)"
  type        = string
}

variable "github_repo" {
  description = "GitHub repo in owner/name format; scopes the OIDC role"
  type        = string
  default     = "stevenfackley/Kairis"
}

variable "create_github_oidc_provider" {
  description = "false when the account already has the GitHub Actions OIDC provider (it does: created by another project)"
  type        = bool
  default     = false
}

# --- Shared host (2026-10 AWS consolidation) ---
# Kairis runs on the shared x86 host Qavren-Web-Server. That host is hand-built (no Terraform owns
# it), so this root only attaches what Kairis needs: the tag the deploy finds it by, and read access
# for the host's existing instance role.
variable "hub_instance_id" {
  description = "Shared x86 host that runs Kairis (Qavren-Web-Server)"
  type        = string
  default     = "i-03ba0ecb6f5697d64"
}

variable "hub_role_name" {
  description = "Instance role of the shared host. Not managed here; Kairis only adds an inline policy to it."
  type        = string
  default     = "QavrenRole"
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: infra/ecr.tf**

```hcl
resource "aws_ecr_repository" "web" {
  name                 = "${var.app}-web"
  image_tag_mutability = "MUTABLE"

  image_scanning_configuration {
    scan_on_push = true
  }

  tags = { App = var.app }
}

resource "aws_ecr_lifecycle_policy" "web" {
  repository = aws_ecr_repository.web.name

  policy = jsonencode({
    rules = [{
      rulePriority = 1
      description  = "Keep last 10 images"
      selection = {
        tagStatus   = "any"
        countType   = "imageCountMoreThan"
        countNumber = 10
      }
      action = { type = "expire" }
    }]
  })
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: infra/iam.tf**

```hcl
# --- GitHub Actions OIDC role: pushes the image, sends the SSM deploy command ---
resource "aws_iam_openid_connect_provider" "github" {
  count           = var.create_github_oidc_provider ? 1 : 0
  url             = "https://token.actions.githubusercontent.com"
  client_id_list  = ["sts.amazonaws.com"]
  thumbprint_list = ["6938fd4d98bab03faadb97b34396831e3780aea1", "1c58a3a8518e8759bf075b76b750d4f2df264fcd"]
}

locals {
  oidc_provider_arn = var.create_github_oidc_provider ? aws_iam_openid_connect_provider.github[0].arn : "arn:aws:iam::${data.aws_caller_identity.current.account_id}:oidc-provider/token.actions.githubusercontent.com"
  gh_owner          = split("/", var.github_repo)[0]
  gh_repo           = split("/", var.github_repo)[1]
}

resource "aws_iam_role" "github_actions" {
  name        = "${var.app}-github-actions"
  description = "Assumed by GitHub Actions for ${var.github_repo}"

  assume_role_policy = jsonencode({
    Version = "2012-10-17"
    Statement = [{
      Effect    = "Allow"
      Principal = { Federated = local.oidc_provider_arn }
      Action    = "sts:AssumeRoleWithWebIdentity"
      Condition = {
        # GitHub issues two subject formats: the classic "repo:owner/name:..." and, for newer
        # repositories, "repo:owner@OWNER_ID/name@REPO_ID:...". Accept both.
        StringLike   = { "token.actions.githubusercontent.com:sub" = ["repo:${var.github_repo}:*", "repo:${local.gh_owner}@*/${local.gh_repo}@*:*"] }
        StringEquals = { "token.actions.githubusercontent.com:aud" = "sts.amazonaws.com" }
      }
    }]
  })

  tags = { App = var.app }
}

resource "aws_iam_role_policy" "ecr_push" {
  name = "${var.app}-ecr-push"
  role = aws_iam_role.github_actions.id

  policy = jsonencode({
    Version = "2012-10-17"
    Statement = [
      { Sid = "ECRAuth", Effect = "Allow", Action = ["ecr:GetAuthorizationToken"], Resource = "*" },
      {
        Sid    = "ECRPush"
        Effect = "Allow"
        Action = [
          "ecr:BatchCheckLayerAvailability", "ecr:CompleteLayerUpload", "ecr:InitiateLayerUpload",
          "ecr:PutImage", "ecr:UploadLayerPart", "ecr:BatchGetImage", "ecr:GetDownloadUrlForLayer",
        ]
        Resource = [aws_ecr_repository.web.arn]
      },
    ]
  })
}

resource "aws_iam_role_policy" "ssm_deploy" {
  name = "${var.app}-ssm-deploy"
  role = aws_iam_role.github_actions.id

  policy = jsonencode({
    Version = "2012-10-17"
    Statement = [
      {
        # The shared host carries one app-<name> tag per app it runs.
        Sid       = "SendToSharedHost"
        Effect    = "Allow"
        Action    = ["ssm:SendCommand"]
        Resource  = "arn:aws:ec2:*:*:instance/*"
        Condition = { StringEquals = { "ssm:resourceTag/app-${var.app}" = "true" } }
      },
      {
        Sid      = "SendRunShellScript"
        Effect   = "Allow"
        Action   = ["ssm:SendCommand"]
        Resource = "arn:aws:ssm:*:*:document/AWS-RunShellScript"
      },
      {
        Sid      = "ReadBack"
        Effect   = "Allow"
        Action   = ["ssm:GetCommandInvocation", "ssm:DescribeInstanceInformation", "ssm:ListCommandInvocations"]
        Resource = "*"
      },
    ]
  })
}

# --- Shared host: tag it for the deploy lookup, let its role pull the image and read the token ---
resource "aws_ec2_tag" "hub_app" {
  resource_id = var.hub_instance_id
  key         = "app-${var.app}"
  value       = "true"
}

resource "aws_iam_role_policy" "hub_pull" {
  name = "${var.app}-hub-pull"
  role = var.hub_role_name

  policy = jsonencode({
    Version = "2012-10-17"
    Statement = [
      { Sid = "ECRAuth", Effect = "Allow", Action = ["ecr:GetAuthorizationToken"], Resource = "*" },
      {
        Sid      = "ECRPull"
        Effect   = "Allow"
        Action   = ["ecr:BatchGetImage", "ecr:GetDownloadUrlForLayer", "ecr:BatchCheckLayerAvailability"]
        Resource = [aws_ecr_repository.web.arn]
      },
      {
        Sid      = "TunnelToken"
        Effect   = "Allow"
        Action   = ["ssm:GetParameter"]
        Resource = "arn:aws:ssm:${var.aws_region}:${data.aws_caller_identity.current.account_id}:parameter/${var.app}/prod/*"
      },
    ]
  })
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: infra/cloudflare.tf**

```hcl
resource "random_id" "tunnel_secret" {
  byte_length = 32
}

resource "cloudflare_zero_trust_tunnel_cloudflared" "web" {
  account_id = var.cloudflare_account_id
  name       = "${var.app}-web"
  secret     = random_id.tunnel_secret.b64_std
}

resource "cloudflare_zero_trust_tunnel_cloudflared_config" "web" {
  account_id = var.cloudflare_account_id
  tunnel_id  = cloudflare_zero_trust_tunnel_cloudflared.web.id

  config {
    # cloudflared runs in the same compose project as web, so it reaches it by service name.
    ingress_rule {
      hostname = var.hostname
      service  = "http://web:3000"
    }
    ingress_rule {
      service = "http_status:404"
    }
  }
}

resource "cloudflare_record" "web" {
  zone_id = var.cloudflare_zone_id
  name    = var.dns_record_name
  content = "${cloudflare_zero_trust_tunnel_cloudflared.web.id}.cfargotunnel.com"
  type    = "CNAME"
  proxied = true
}

# The tunnel token reaches the host at deploy time via SSM Parameter Store, never via GitHub secrets.
resource "aws_ssm_parameter" "tunnel_token" {
  name  = "/${var.app}/prod/tunnel_token"
  type  = "SecureString"
  value = cloudflare_zero_trust_tunnel_cloudflared.web.tunnel_token
  tags  = { App = var.app }
}
```

```hcl
}

output "github_actions_role_arn" {
```

- [ ] **Step 8: infra/README.md**

````markdown
# infra

Terraform for Kairis on the shared Qavren hub (`Qavren-Web-Server`, `i-03ba0ecb6f5697d64`): one ECR
repository (`kairis-web`), a GitHub OIDC role (`kairis-github-actions`) scoped to
`stevenfackley/Kairis`, the `app-kairis = true` tag on the hub plus an inline `kairis-hub-pull`
policy on its `QavrenRole` (ECR pull, `ssm:GetParameter` on `/kairis/prod/*`), and a Cloudflare
tunnel (`kairis-web`) with one ingress, `kairis.qavrensolutions.com -> http://web:3000`, behind a
proxied `kairis` CNAME in the qavrensolutions.com zone. The tunnel token is stored in SSM as
`/kairis/prod/tunnel_token`. No EC2 instance is created here: the hub is hand-built and shared.

State is local and gitignored.

    cp terraform.tfvars.example terraform.tfvars   # fill in the Cloudflare values
    terraform init && terraform plan -out tfplan && terraform apply tfplan
    terraform output

Then in GitHub (`stevenfackley/Kairis`): repository variables `AWS_REGION` and `ECR_REGISTRY`
(`terraform output ecr_registry`); repository secret `AWS_OIDC_ROLE_ARN`
(`terraform output github_actions_role_arn`; the build job runs outside the environment);
environment `production` secrets `DOTENV_CONTENT`, `EXTRA_ENV_CONTENT` (optional) and
`QAVREN_DB_PROD_SESSION_URL`. The full sequence and the `.env` template are in
[`docs/deployment.md`](../docs/deployment.md).

## The host `.env`

`DOTENV_CONTENT` is the whole of `/opt/kairis/.env` except three lines the deploy job appends:
`ECR_REGISTRY` and `IMAGE_TAG` from the workflow and `TUNNEL_TOKEN` from SSM. `web` reads the file
through `env_file: [{ path: .env, required: false }]` (optional so `docker compose config` works
from a clean checkout). GitHub stores the secret as one write-only blob: to change one value,
rewrite the whole thing, or put the extra line in `EXTRA_ENV_CONTENT`, which the deploy appends
after it.

| Name | What it is |
|---|---|
| `APP_BASE_URL`, `AUTH_URL` | `https://kairis.qavrensolutions.com` |
| `AUTH_SECRET` | 32 random bytes, base64. Rotating it signs every session out. |
| `AUTH_TRUST_HOST` | `true`: the app sits behind the Cloudflare tunnel |
| `QAVREN_AUTH_URL`, `QAVREN_REALM` | `https://auth.qavrensolutions.com`, `kairis` |
| `KAIRIS_OWNER_EMAILS` | Comma list of owner emails (operations page, auto cycle) |
| `KAIRIS_SECRET_KEY` | 32 random bytes, base64: the AES-256-GCM key for stored exchange secrets. Losing it orphans every stored key (users reconnect). |
| `DATABASE_URL` | qavren-db transaction pooler, port 6543, user `kairis.<project-ref>`. No `sslmode` parameter. |
| `ENABLE_LIVE_ASSISTED_TRADING` | `false` until the owner decides otherwise |
| `ENABLE_AUTO_MODE` | `false` |
| `R2_*` | Optional; exports fall back to the container's `.local-data`, which a redeploy wipes |

Port `127.0.0.1:3040` on the hub is the loopback health-check port; nothing else on the host may
take it.
````

- [ ] **Step 9: compose.prod.yaml**

```yaml
name: kairis
services:
  web:
    image: ${ECR_REGISTRY}/kairis-web:${IMAGE_TAG:-latest}
    container_name: kairis-web
    env_file:
      - { path: .env, required: false }
    # Loopback only, for the deploy health check; the tunnel reaches web:3000 over the compose network.
    # 3040 because the hub's other apps hold 3000-3002, 3010 and 3021.
    ports: ["127.0.0.1:3040:3000"]
    restart: always
    healthcheck:
      test: ["CMD-SHELL", "wget -q -O /dev/null http://127.0.0.1:3000/api/health || exit 1"]
      interval: 15s
      timeout: 3s
      start_period: 15s
      retries: 5
    logging: { driver: json-file, options: { max-size: "10m", max-file: "3" } }
  cloudflared:
    image: cloudflare/cloudflared:latest
    container_name: kairis-tunnel
    command: tunnel --no-autoupdate run
    environment:
      TUNNEL_TOKEN: ${TUNNEL_TOKEN}
    depends_on:
      web: { condition: service_healthy }
    restart: always
    logging: { driver: json-file, options: { max-size: "10m", max-file: "3" } }
```

- [ ] **Step 10: Dockerfile (rewrite)**

```dockerfile
FROM node:26-alpine AS deps
WORKDIR /app
COPY package.json package-lock.json ./
RUN npm ci

FROM node:26-alpine AS builder
WORKDIR /app
ENV NEXT_TELEMETRY_DISABLED=1
# NEXT_PUBLIC_* values are inlined at build time, server bundles included: the host .env cannot change them later.
ENV NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_NAME=Kairis NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_ENV=production
COPY --from=deps /app/node_modules ./node_modules
COPY . .
RUN npm run build

FROM node:26-alpine AS runner
WORKDIR /app
ENV NODE_ENV=production NEXT_TELEMETRY_DISABLED=1 PORT=3000 HOSTNAME=0.0.0.0
RUN addgroup -g 1001 -S nodejs && adduser -S nextjs -u 1001
COPY --from=builder --chown=nextjs:nodejs /app/.next/standalone ./
COPY --from=builder --chown=nextjs:nodejs /app/.next/static ./.next/static
COPY --from=builder --chown=nextjs:nodejs /app/public ./public
RUN mkdir -p /app/.local-data && chown -R nextjs:nodejs /app/.local-data
USER nextjs
EXPOSE 3000
HEALTHCHECK --interval=15s --timeout=3s --start-period=15s --retries=5 CMD ["node", "-e", "fetch('http://127.0.0.1:3000/api/health').then(r=>process.exit(r.ok?0:1)).catch(()=>process.exit(1))"]
CMD ["node", "server.js"]
```

- [ ] **Step 12: .github/workflows/deploy-prod.yml**

```yaml
name: deploy-prod

on:
  push:
    tags: ["[0-9][0-9][0-9][0-9][0-9][0-9][0-9][0-9]_*_Release"]
  workflow_dispatch:
    inputs:
      ref:
        description: Tag or SHA to deploy
        required: true

concurrency:
  group: deploy-prod
  cancel-in-progress: false

permissions:
  contents: read
  id-token: write

env:
  AWS_REGION: ${{ vars.AWS_REGION }}
  ECR_REGISTRY: ${{ vars.ECR_REGISTRY }}

jobs:
  build:
    if: github.actor != 'dependabot[bot]'
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v7
        with: { ref: "${{ inputs.ref || github.ref }}" }
      - uses: aws-actions/configure-aws-credentials@v6
        with: { role-to-assume: "${{ secrets.AWS_OIDC_ROLE_ARN }}", aws-region: "${{ env.AWS_REGION }}" }
      - uses: aws-actions/amazon-ecr-login@v2
      - uses: docker/setup-buildx-action@v4
      - uses: docker/build-push-action@v7
        with:
          context: .
          file: Dockerfile
          push: true
          platforms: linux/amd64
          tags: |
            ${{ env.ECR_REGISTRY }}/kairis-web:prod-${{ github.sha }}
            ${{ env.ECR_REGISTRY }}/kairis-web:latest
          cache-from: type=gha,scope=kairis-web
          cache-to: type=gha,mode=max,scope=kairis-web

  deploy:
    needs: build
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    environment: production
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v7
        with: { ref: "${{ inputs.ref || github.ref }}" }
      - uses: actions/setup-node@v7
        with: { node-version-file: .nvmrc }
      # Migrations run from the runner, before the new image starts, through the qavren-db SESSION
      # pooler (:5432): DDL in a transaction needs session mode. Every migration is additive, so the
      # image still running keeps working against the new schema.
      - name: Apply database migrations
        env:
          DATABASE_URL: ${{ secrets.QAVREN_DB_PROD_SESSION_URL }}
        run: |
          npm ci --omit=dev --ignore-scripts
          node scripts/db-migrate.mjs
      - uses: aws-actions/configure-aws-credentials@v6
        with: { role-to-assume: "${{ secrets.AWS_OIDC_ROLE_ARN }}", aws-region: "${{ env.AWS_REGION }}" }
      - name: Deploy over SSM
        env:
          IMAGE_TAG: prod-${{ github.sha }}
          DOTENV_CONTENT: ${{ secrets.DOTENV_CONTENT }}
          # Appended after DOTENV_CONTENT. That blob is write-only, so adding one key would otherwise
          # mean retyping every other one from memory.
          EXTRA_ENV_CONTENT: ${{ secrets.EXTRA_ENV_CONTENT }}
        run: |
          set -euo pipefail
          INSTANCE_ID=$(aws ssm describe-instance-information --filters "Key=tag:app-kairis,Values=true" \
            --query "InstanceInformationList[?PingStatus=='Online'].InstanceId | [0]" --output text)
          if [ -z "$INSTANCE_ID" ] || [ "$INSTANCE_ID" = "None" ]; then echo "no online kairis host"; exit 1; fi
          echo "instance: $INSTANCE_ID"
          COMPOSE_B64=$(base64 -w0 compose.prod.yaml)
          ENV_B64=$(printf '%s\n%s\nECR_REGISTRY=%s\nIMAGE_TAG=%s\n' "$DOTENV_CONTENT" "${EXTRA_ENV_CONTENT:-}" "$ECR_REGISTRY" "$IMAGE_TAG" | base64 -w0)
          python3 - "$COMPOSE_B64" "$ENV_B64" "$AWS_REGION" "$ECR_REGISTRY" > /tmp/cmd.json <<'PY'
          import json, sys
          compose_b64, env_b64, region, registry = sys.argv[1:5]
          cmds = [
            "set -e",
            "mkdir -p /opt/kairis && cd /opt/kairis",
            f"echo {compose_b64} | base64 -d > compose.prod.yaml",
            f"umask 077 && echo {env_b64} | base64 -d > .env",
            f"TOKEN=$(aws ssm get-parameter --name /kairis/prod/tunnel_token --with-decryption --query Parameter.Value --output text --region {region})",
            "umask 077 && echo \"TUNNEL_TOKEN=$TOKEN\" >> .env",
            f"aws ecr get-login-password --region {region} | docker login --username AWS --password-stdin {registry}",
            "docker compose -f compose.prod.yaml pull",
            "docker compose -f compose.prod.yaml up -d --remove-orphans",
            "docker image prune -f",
            "for i in $(seq 1 40); do W=$(curl -fsS http://127.0.0.1:3040/api/health >/dev/null 2>&1 && echo ok || echo no); H=$(docker inspect --format '{{.State.Health.Status}}' kairis-web 2>/dev/null || echo missing); echo web=$W health=$H; if [ \"$W\" = ok ] && [ \"$H\" = healthy ]; then exit 0; fi; sleep 3; done",
            "docker compose -f compose.prod.yaml logs --tail 100; exit 1",
          ]
          json.dump({"commands": cmds}, sys.stdout)
          PY
          CMD_ID=$(aws ssm send-command --instance-ids "$INSTANCE_ID" --document-name AWS-RunShellScript \
            --parameters file:///tmp/cmd.json --timeout-seconds 900 --query Command.CommandId --output text)
          echo "command: $CMD_ID"
          DEADLINE=$(( $(date +%s) + 900 ))
          S=Pending
          while :; do
            S=$(aws ssm get-command-invocation --command-id "$CMD_ID" --instance-id "$INSTANCE_ID" --query Status --output text 2>/dev/null || echo Pending)
            case "$S" in Success|Failed|Cancelled|TimedOut) break;; esac
            if [ "$(date +%s)" -gt "$DEADLINE" ]; then S=TimedOut; break; fi
            sleep 10
          done
          aws ssm get-command-invocation --command-id "$CMD_ID" --instance-id "$INSTANCE_ID" --query 'StandardOutputContent' --output text || true
          aws ssm get-command-invocation --command-id "$CMD_ID" --instance-id "$INSTANCE_ID" --query 'StandardErrorContent' --output text || true
          echo "status: $S"
          [ "$S" = Success ]
      - name: Smoke the public URL
        run: |
          for i in $(seq 1 20); do curl -fsS https://kairis.qavrensolutions.com/api/health && exit 0; sleep 6; done
          exit 1
```

- [ ] **Step 1: README.md (rewrite)**

````markdown
# Kairis <sub>`>o)`</sub>

Kairis is a non-custodial crypto trading workspace built around disciplined execution: rule-based
signals that explain themselves, a risk engine that checks every order against the user's own limits,
paper trading, and assisted orders on the user's own Coinbase account that never go out without an
explicit confirmation.

## License

This repository is proprietary and is not open source. See [LICENSE](LICENSE) for the governing terms.

## What v1 does

- **Sign up and sign in** through the `kairis` realm on [qavren-auth](https://github.com/stevenfackley/qavren-auth)
  (Keycloak, public PKCE client `kairis-web`). Every row Kairis stores is keyed by the Keycloak `sub`.
- **Onboarding**: scope explanation, a required risk acknowledgment, a preferred mode (paper, manual,
  assisted). Everyone starts in paper.
- **Signals**: BTC-USD, ETH-USD and SOL-USD on hourly Coinbase candles. EMA 9/21 cross confirmed by
  RSI 14, blocked by wide spreads, ATR spikes or stale data, with plain-language rationale and a
  suggested size derived from the user's limits. Every evaluation is stored.
- **Risk engine**: max position, daily loss cap, trades per day, cooldown after a loss streak,
  per-symbol caps, a global kill switch, plus fail-closed checks for stale data and a degraded
  provider. Outcomes are `approved`, `blocked` or `halted`, each with the reasons.
- **Paper trading**: simulated fills at the Coinbase reference price, positions with average cost,
  realized P&L, and a journal that keeps blocked attempts with their reasons.
- **Coinbase connection**: per-user Coinbase Developer Platform keys. Kairis asks Coinbase what the
  key can do and rejects transfer-capable keys; the private key is stored AES-256-GCM encrypted.
- **Assisted trades**: check, preview, explicit confirm, submit, reconcile. The database row id is
  the exchange client order id, so a double submit cannot create a second order. Real submission is
  gated by `ENABLE_LIVE_ASSISTED_TRADING`; without a connected key a mock provider is used.
- **Records**: a filterable journal of every audit event and CSV exports (paper journal, assisted
  orders, audit log) to Cloudflare R2, or local files when R2 is not configured.
- **Owner operations**: system status, reconcile, and an auto cycle that only runs for the owner and
  only when both `ENABLE_AUTO_MODE` and `ENABLE_LIVE_ASSISTED_TRADING` are true.

## Current runtime state

- The code above is complete and tested (unit tests for every domain module and service,
  integration tests for every repo against Postgres).
- Production is `https://kairis.qavrensolutions.com` on the shared Qavren hub. It goes live when the
  owner runs the one-time steps in [docs/deployment.md](docs/deployment.md) (realms, database
  password, `terraform apply`, GitHub secrets, release tag).
- Live submission ships **disabled** (`ENABLE_LIVE_ASSISTED_TRADING=false`) and auto mode ships
  **disabled**. Previews against a real Coinbase key work; submits are blocked and recorded.
- Limits of v1: Coinbase only; spot only; market IOC orders only; live positions count only fills
  Kairis placed; exports stored in the container are lost on redeploy unless R2 is configured.

## Local setup

Prerequisites: Node 26 (`.nvmrc`), Docker Desktop.

```bash
npm ci
npm run db:up                      # Postgres 17 on 127.0.0.1:55433 (compose.dev.yaml)
cp .env.example .env.local
node -e "console.log(require('crypto').randomBytes(32).toString('base64'))"   # paste as AUTH_SECRET
node -e "console.log(require('crypto').randomBytes(32).toString('base64'))"   # paste as KAIRIS_SECRET_KEY
npm run db:migrate                 # applies db/migrations, tracked in schema_migrations
npm run dev                        # http://localhost:3000
```

Sign-in uses the `kairis-dev` realm (`QAVREN_REALM=kairis-dev`), which allows
`http://localhost:3000/*` as a redirect URI. Either use the hosted realm on
`https://auth.qavrensolutions.com` (leave `QAVREN_AUTH_URL` unset) or run qavren-auth locally and set
`QAVREN_AUTH_URL=http://localhost:8080`; see [docs/identity.md](docs/identity.md).

Gates: `npm run lint`, `npm run typecheck`, `npm run test`, `npm run build`,
`pwsh scripts/scan-secrets.ps1`. Integration tests run when `DATABASE_URL` points at localhost:
`DATABASE_URL=postgres://kairis:kairis@localhost:55433/kairis npm run test`.

## Deploy

One image (`kairis-web`) in ECR, deployed over SSM to the shared hub behind a Cloudflare tunnel. A
tag like `20261009_v1_Release` triggers `.github/workflows/deploy-prod.yml`: build and push, apply
migrations through the qavren-db session pooler, write `/opt/kairis/.env` and
`compose.prod.yaml` on the hub, restart, wait for `/api/health`, smoke the public URL. Details:
[docs/deployment.md](docs/deployment.md), [infra/README.md](infra/README.md),
[docs/database.md](docs/database.md).

## Source-of-truth decisions

- `Kairis` is the working product name; beginner retail spot traders are the first audience.
- Paper mode is the default starting experience; public value is control, not profit promises.
- Public unattended automation is not offered; the auto cycle is owner-only and env-gated.
- Identity is the `kairis` realm on qavren-auth (Keycloak); `kairis-dev` serves localhost.
- Data lives in the `kairis` schema on qavren-db (shared Supabase Postgres, schema-per-app). Postgres
  is required; there is no JSON fallback.
- Production runs on the shared Qavren hub (`Qavren-Web-Server`) behind a Cloudflare tunnel; no
  per-app EC2 box, no Proxmox.
- Cloudflare R2 holds exports when configured.
- GitHub Actions is the CI/CD system; AWS access is OIDC only.
- Exchange keys are per user, trade-only, and encrypted at rest.

The ADR log is [DECISIONS.md](DECISIONS.md).

## Document map

- Strategy: [Brand + Business Brief](docs/brand-business-brief.md), [Business Plan](docs/business-plan.md)
- Product and design: [PRD](docs/prd.md), [PDD](docs/pdd.md)
- System and data: [SDD](docs/sdd.md), [Data Flow Diagram](docs/diagrams/data-flow-diagram.md),
  [Database](docs/database.md), [Identity](docs/identity.md), [Deployment](docs/deployment.md)
- Risk and commercialization: [Risk and Compliance Memo](docs/risk-compliance-memo.md),
  [Pricing and Packaging](docs/pricing-packaging-brief.md), [Marketing Guide](docs/marketing-guide.md),
  [Go-to-Market Plan](docs/go-to-market-launch-plan.md)
- Brand assets: [Logo concepts](assets/logos/README.md), [Threshold K](assets/logos/kairis-threshold-k.svg)

## Working brand

Name `Kairis` (KAI-riss, from *kairos*, the right moment). Selected logo direction: Threshold K.
Palette: Obsidian `#111315`, Bone `#E9E4D8`, Sage Signal `#708B7A` (paper mode accent), Brass Index
`#C8B27A` (live mode accent).
````

- [ ] **Step 2: CLAUDE.md (rewrite)**

```markdown
# Kairis

> Disciplined, non-custodial crypto trading: signals with rationale, a risk engine, paper and assisted Coinbase trading.

## Stack
- **Runtime:** Next.js 16 App Router + React 19 + TypeScript 5.8 strict
- **Identity:** qavren-auth Keycloak realm `kairis` (`kairis-dev` locally) via `@qavren/auth-next` + `next-auth@5 beta`
- **Database:** qavren-db shared Postgres, schema/role `kairis`, `pg` through the Supavisor transaction pooler (:6543)
- **Exchange:** Coinbase Advanced Trade REST; `@coinbase/cdp-sdk/auth` for per-request JWTs only
- **Storage:** Cloudflare R2 via `@aws-sdk/client-s3` (exports), local file fallback
- **Hosting:** shared Qavren hub (`Qavren-Web-Server`) behind a Cloudflare tunnel; image in ECR; Terraform in `infra/`

## Layout
- `lib/domain/*`: pure, unit-tested logic (indicators, signals, risk, paper P&L, crypto, csv, form parsing)
- `lib/server/repos/*`: one module per table, explicit row types, `query<T>()` from `lib/server/db.ts`
- `lib/server/services/*`: the only layer that combines repos, market data and the exchange; takes a `userId`, never imports `@/auth`
- `app/app/<area>/{page.tsx,actions.ts}`: server pages, `"use server"` actions; `proxy.ts` gates `/app/*`
- `db/migrations/*.sql`: idempotent, applied in name order by `scripts/db-migrate.mjs`, tracked in `schema_migrations`

## Commands
- `npm run dev` / `npm run build` / `npm run start`
- `npm run lint` (eslint 10 + eslint-config-next), `npm run typecheck`, `npm run test` (vitest)
- `npm run db:up` / `npm run db:down` (local Postgres 17 on 55433), `npm run db:migrate`
- `DATABASE_URL=postgres://kairis:kairis@localhost:55433/kairis npm run test` runs the integration suite too
- `pwsh scripts/scan-secrets.ps1`

## Conventions
- **Package manager: npm** (`package-lock.json`)
- Server components by default. `"use client"` only for forms and interaction.
- Styling: global CSS tokens and classes in `app/globals.css` (paper accent sage, live accent brass, set by `data-mode`). No CSS-in-JS, no Tailwind.
- Every `/app` page except onboarding calls `requireOnboarded()`; owner pages call `requireOwner()`.
- Every order path goes through `evaluateIntent()` (risk + audit) before anything is stored or sent.
- Commits: Conventional Commits. Branches: `main` protected, squash-merge via PR.
- Releases: tag `YYYYMMDD_<name>_Release` triggers `deploy-prod.yml`.

## CI/CD
- `.github/workflows/ci.yml`: reusable `ci-node.yml@v1` with `run-postgres: true` (lint, typecheck, unit + integration tests, Docker build)
- `.github/workflows/deploy-prod.yml`: build -> ECR, migrations via `QAVREN_DB_PROD_SESSION_URL`, SSM deploy to the hub, public smoke test
- Dockerfile: `node:26-alpine` multi-stage, standalone output, healthcheck on `/api/health`

## Do not
- Bump the `axios` override below `^1.15.2` (currently `>=1.18.0`): multiple CVEs. The override is the only thing forcing transitive bumps past parent pins.
- Bump the `postcss` override below `^8.5.10` (currently `^8.5.18`): CVE.
- Treat a caret override as set-and-forget: it silently caps the dependency until bumped (lesson from PR #24).
- Add static AWS keys. Use OIDC.
- Reach for CSS-in-JS or Tailwind.
- Use `any`. Use `unknown` + narrowing.
- Add telemetry SDKs (ApplicationInsights/Sentry/Datadog/etc).
- Accept transfer-capable exchange keys, or log/return a decrypted exchange secret.
- Rely on Postgres session state (SET, named prepared statements, advisory locks): prod is a transaction pooler.
- Propose Proxmox, K3s, Supabase-per-app or a per-app EC2 box.
```

- [ ] **Step 3: DECISIONS.md (rewrite)**

```markdown
# Decisions

ADR log. Append-only.

## 2026-04-04 — Next.js 16 App Router stack

**Status:** accepted
**Context:** Greenfield web product that needs server rendering, server actions and a small container image.
**Decision:** Next.js App Router with React 19 and strict TypeScript, `output: "standalone"`, server components by default.
**Consequences:** `"use client"` only where interaction needs it. No telemetry SDKs (CI-enforced).

## 2026-07-12 — Shared Postgres on qavren-db

**Status:** accepted
**Context:** One Supabase project per app was costly and sprawling.
**Decision:** Kairis data lives in the `kairis` schema with its own login role on qavren-db. Runtime traffic uses the Supavisor transaction pooler (:6543); migrations use the session pooler (:5432).
**Consequences:** No reliance on session state. Isolation is Postgres grants, not RLS.

## 2026-10-09 — Identity on qavren-auth (Keycloak)

**Status:** accepted
**Context:** Users need accounts; building auth in-app duplicates platform work.
**Decision:** Realm `kairis` (and `kairis-dev` for localhost) on qavren-auth, public PKCE client `kairis-web`, wired through `@qavren/auth-next`. Rows are keyed by the Keycloak `sub`. Owners are `KAIRIS_OWNER_EMAILS` or the realm role `owner`.
**Consequences:** No passwords in Kairis. Sign-out clears the Kairis session only; the realm SSO session persists until it expires.

## 2026-10-09 — Hosting on the Qavren hub

**Status:** accepted (supersedes the Proxmox test lane and the per-app EC2 plan)
**Context:** One EC2 box per project drove the AWS bill; Proxmox is in storage.
**Decision:** One image in ECR, deployed over SSM to the shared `Qavren-Web-Server`, published through a Cloudflare tunnel at `kairis.qavrensolutions.com`. Terraform in `infra/` owns ECR, the OIDC role, the hub tag and pull policy, the tunnel and DNS.
**Consequences:** No test environment; CI plus local compose cover pre-prod. Releases are date-stamped tags.

## 2026-10-09 — Postgres required, JSON fallback removed

**Status:** accepted
**Context:** The scaffold silently fell back to local JSON files when no database was configured, which hid misconfiguration.
**Decision:** `DATABASE_URL` is required; the app errors without it. Migrations are tracked in `schema_migrations`.
**Consequences:** Local development needs `npm run db:up`. CI runs integration tests against a Postgres service container.

## 2026-10-09 — Rule-based signal engine on Coinbase public data

**Status:** accepted
**Context:** Signals must be explainable to beginners and must not imply predictions.
**Decision:** Hourly Coinbase candles and ticker (public endpoints, cached 30 s in process). EMA 9/21 cross within 3 candles, RSI 14 between 45 and 70; spread, ATR and staleness gates fail closed. Every evaluation is stored with its rationale.
**Consequences:** No ML, no third-party data vendor. Strategy constants live in `lib/domain/strategy.ts`.

## 2026-10-09 — Per-user encrypted exchange keys, transfer-capable keys rejected

**Status:** accepted
**Context:** Kairis must stay non-custodial and must never be able to move user funds.
**Decision:** Users connect their own Coinbase Developer Platform key. Kairis calls `key_permissions` and rejects any key with transfer permission (a missing flag counts as transfer-capable). The private key is sealed with AES-256-GCM under `KAIRIS_SECRET_KEY`; without that key, connections are disabled. Real submission is additionally gated by `ENABLE_LIVE_ASSISTED_TRADING`.
**Consequences:** Rotating `KAIRIS_SECRET_KEY` orphans stored keys; users reconnect.
```

````markdown
# Contributing

## Flow
1. Branch off `main` (`feat/...`, `fix/...`).
2. Conventional Commits.
3. PR -> CI green -> squash-merge. Never push to `main`.

## Gates (run all before pushing)

```bash
npm run lint
npm run typecheck
npm run db:up && DATABASE_URL=postgres://kairis:kairis@localhost:55433/kairis npm run test   # unit + integration
npm run build
pwsh scripts/scan-secrets.ps1
```

CI runs the same lint, typecheck and test steps (with a Postgres service container, so the
integration suite runs there too) plus a Docker build.

## Rules of thumb
- Pure logic goes in `lib/domain/*` with a unit test first.
- New tables or columns: a new idempotent file in `db/migrations/` (never edit an applied one), a repo
  in `lib/server/repos/`, and a case in `tests/integration/repos.test.ts`.
- Any new order path must call `evaluateIntent()` and write an audit event.

## Major changes
Add a dated ADR to `DECISIONS.md` for architectural changes.
````

- [ ] **Step 5: SECURITY.md (rewrite)**

```markdown
# Security

Report privately via GitHub Security Advisories. Acknowledged within 72 h.

Rules:
- No static AWS keys: GitHub Actions uses OIDC only.
- No telemetry SDKs (CI-enforced); secret-shape scan (`scripts/scan-secrets.ps1`) on every PR.
- The host `.env` is written with `umask 077` on deploy.
- Exchange secrets are stored AES-256-GCM encrypted with `KAIRIS_SECRET_KEY` (32 random bytes, base64) and are never returned to the browser or logged. Without the key, exchange connections are disabled.
- Transfer-capable exchange keys are rejected; Kairis checks `key_permissions` before storing anything, and a missing permission flag counts as transfer-capable.
- Real order submission is gated by the `ENABLE_LIVE_ASSISTED_TRADING` environment flag; the owner-only auto cycle also needs `ENABLE_AUTO_MODE`.
- Every order uses its database row id as the exchange client order id, so replays cannot duplicate an order.
```

- [ ] **Step 6: docs/deployment.md (new)**

````markdown
# Deployment

Kairis runs on the shared Qavren hub (`Qavren-Web-Server`, instance `i-03ba0ecb6f5697d64`) as one
container (`kairis-web`) plus its own `cloudflared` sidecar, published at
`https://kairis.qavrensolutions.com`. The image lives in ECR (`kairis-web`). Data is the `kairis`
schema on qavren-db; identity is the `kairis` realm on qavren-auth. There is no test environment:
CI and the local compose stack cover pre-prod.

## Moving parts

| Piece | Owner | Where |
|---|---|---|
| ECR repo, OIDC role, hub tag + pull policy, tunnel, DNS, SSM token | Terraform | `infra/` |
| Host `.env` | GitHub environment secrets | `DOTENV_CONTENT`, `EXTRA_ENV_CONTENT` |
| Schema migrations | deploy job, before the restart | `scripts/db-migrate.mjs` with `QAVREN_DB_PROD_SESSION_URL` |
| Restart + health wait | SSM command from the deploy job | `/opt/kairis` on the hub |

## One-time owner steps

The agent shell cannot run these (credentials and production mutations); run them in order.

1. **Keycloak realms** (qavren-auth). Generate both realm files (`-Apply` also loads them into the
   local qavren-auth Keycloak, if it is running):

       pwsh C:\Users\steve\projects\qavren-auth\tools\provision-app.ps1 -App kairis -DisplayName Kairis -RedirectUris 'https://kairis.qavrensolutions.com/*' -Apply
       pwsh C:\Users\steve\projects\qavren-auth\tools\provision-app.ps1 -App kairis-dev -DisplayName Kairis -RedirectUris 'http://localhost:3000/*' -Apply

   The fleet template ships `registrationAllowed: false`, which leaves only "Sign in with Google" as
   self-serve sign-up. For email sign-up, edit both `realms/apps/kairis.yaml` and
   `realms/apps/kairis-dev.yaml`: replace the `registrationAllowed: false` line with the block below
   (the pairing `realms/apps/fairsquare.yaml` uses: self-registration never ships without email
   verification, and a default action avoids the retroactive `verifyEmail` flag), and optionally add
   an `owner` entry under `roles.realm`:

   ```yaml
   registrationAllowed: true
   verifyEmail: false
   requiredActions:
     - alias: VERIFY_EMAIL
       name: Verify Email
       providerId: VERIFY_EMAIL
       enabled: true
       defaultAction: true
       priority: 50
       config: {}
   ```

   ```yaml
       - name: owner
         description: Kairis owner (operations page, auto cycle)
   ```

   Commit the two files in qavren-auth through a PR, then push them to the hosted Keycloak,
   in-process from a pwsh prompt (the script header explains why not `pwsh ./update-realms.ps1`):

       & C:\Users\steve\projects\qavren-auth\infra\update-realms.ps1 -Realm kairis
       & C:\Users\steve\projects\qavren-auth\infra\update-realms.ps1 -Realm kairis-dev

   Then add both broker endpoints to the shared Google OAuth client's Authorized redirect URIs
   (qavren-auth `docs/google-oauth-setup.md`), or the Google button fails with
   `redirect_uri_mismatch`: `https://auth.qavrensolutions.com/realms/kairis/broker/google/endpoint`
   and `https://auth.qavrensolutions.com/realms/kairis-dev/broker/google/endpoint`.
   If you added the `owner` role, assign it to yourself in the `kairis` realm; otherwise list your
   email in `KAIRIS_OWNER_EMAILS`.

2. **Database password** (qavren-db; the schema and role already exist):

       pwsh C:\Users\steve\projects\qavren-db\tools\provision-app.ps1 -App kairis -Env prod -Apply -RotatePassword

   It prints the password once, with `session_url=` (port 5432) and `pooler_url=` (port 6543). The
   pooler URL goes into `DATABASE_URL` in the `.env` below; the session URL becomes the
   `QAVREN_DB_PROD_SESSION_URL` secret. Neither may carry an `sslmode` parameter.

3. **Terraform**:

       cd C:\Users\steve\projects\Kairis\infra
       cp terraform.tfvars.example terraform.tfvars   # Cloudflare token, account id, qavrensolutions.com zone id
       terraform init
       terraform plan -out tfplan
       terraform apply tfplan
       terraform output

4. **GitHub variables and secrets** (`stevenfackley/Kairis`):

       gh variable set AWS_REGION --body us-east-1 --repo stevenfackley/Kairis
       gh variable set ECR_REGISTRY --body "<terraform output ecr_registry>" --repo stevenfackley/Kairis
       gh secret set AWS_OIDC_ROLE_ARN --body "<terraform output github_actions_role_arn>" --repo stevenfackley/Kairis
       gh api -X PUT repos/stevenfackley/Kairis/environments/production
       gh secret set DOTENV_CONTENT --env production --repo stevenfackley/Kairis < env.txt
       gh secret set QAVREN_DB_PROD_SESSION_URL --env production --repo stevenfackley/Kairis --body "<session_url from step 2>"

   `EXTRA_ENV_CONTENT` (environment `production`) is optional: lines in it are appended after
   `DOTENV_CONTENT`, which is the easy way to add or override one variable without retyping the
   whole blob. Delete `env.txt` after setting the secret.

5. **Release** (from an up-to-date `main` after the PR is merged):

       git tag 20261009_v1_Release && git push origin 20261009_v1_Release

   The `deploy-prod` workflow builds `prod-<sha>` and `latest`, applies migrations, deploys over SSM,
   waits for the container to report healthy on `127.0.0.1:3040`, and curls
   `https://kairis.qavrensolutions.com/api/health`. To redeploy a specific ref, run the workflow
   manually with `ref`.

## `env.txt` (the whole of `DOTENV_CONTENT`)

The deploy job appends `ECR_REGISTRY`, `IMAGE_TAG` and `TUNNEL_TOKEN` itself; do not include them.

```
NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_NAME=Kairis
NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_ENV=production
APP_BASE_URL=https://kairis.qavrensolutions.com
AUTH_URL=https://kairis.qavrensolutions.com
AUTH_SECRET=<32 random bytes, base64>
AUTH_TRUST_HOST=true
QAVREN_AUTH_URL=https://auth.qavrensolutions.com
QAVREN_REALM=kairis
KAIRIS_OWNER_EMAILS=<your email>
KAIRIS_SECRET_KEY=<32 random bytes, base64; keep a copy offline>
DATABASE_URL=postgres://kairis.<project-ref>:<password>@<pooler-host>:6543/postgres
ENABLE_LIVE_ASSISTED_TRADING=false
ENABLE_AUTO_MODE=false
R2_ACCOUNT_ID=
R2_ACCESS_KEY_ID=
R2_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY=
R2_BUCKET=
R2_PUBLIC_URL=
LOCAL_DATA_DIR=.local-data
```

Generate each random value with
`node -e "console.log(require('crypto').randomBytes(32).toString('base64'))"`.
`NEXT_PUBLIC_*` values are baked into the image at build time (the Dockerfile sets them); the lines
here only document them.

## Turning on live submission

Set `ENABLE_LIVE_ASSISTED_TRADING=true` in `EXTRA_ENV_CONTENT` (or rewrite `DOTENV_CONTENT`) and
re-run the deploy. Until then, Coinbase previews work and submits are blocked and recorded. The
owner-only auto cycle additionally needs `ENABLE_AUTO_MODE=true`.

## Rollback

Run `deploy-prod` manually with the previous release tag as `ref`. Migrations are additive, so an
older image runs against the newer schema.

## Checks after a deploy

- `curl -s https://kairis.qavrensolutions.com/api/health` -> `{"app":"Kairis","status":"ok","appEnv":"production"}`
- `curl -s https://kairis.qavrensolutions.com/api/system/status` -> `"database":"connected"`
- `/app` redirects to `/sign-in`; signing in returns to `/app/onboarding` for a new account.
````

- [ ] **Step 7: docs/database.md (new)**

````markdown
# Database

## Where it lives

- **Prod:** qavren-db (one Supabase Pro project for all Qavren apps). Kairis owns schema `kairis`
  and logs in as role `kairis`, which has no grants outside its schema. The role's `search_path` is
  `kairis, extensions`, so table names are unqualified.
- **Local:** `compose.dev.yaml`, Postgres 17 on `127.0.0.1:55433`, user/password/database `kairis`.
  Tables land in `public` there; the code does not care.
- **CI:** the reusable workflow's `postgres:16` service container
  (`postgresql://testuser:testpassword@localhost:5432/test_db`).

## Connection strings

| Use | Port | Mode | Variable |
|---|---|---|---|
| App runtime | 6543 | Supavisor transaction pooler | `DATABASE_URL` (host `.env`) |
| Migrations | 5432 | Supavisor session pooler | `QAVREN_DB_PROD_SESSION_URL` (GitHub environment secret) |

The username on both is `kairis.<project-ref>`. Do not add `sslmode` to either URL: `pg` lets the URL
override the client's `ssl` option, and both the app and the migrate script already use TLS without
certificate pinning for any non-localhost host.

Transaction pooling means each statement may run on a different server connection. The code never
uses `SET`, named prepared statements, `LISTEN`, or advisory locks across statements; keep it that way.

## Migrations

- Files: `db/migrations/*.sql`, applied in file-name order. Name new ones
  `YYYYMMDDHHMMSS_description.sql`. Never edit a file that has been applied; add a new one.
- Every migration is idempotent (`if not exists`, drop-and-add constraints) and additive, so the
  image that is still running keeps working while the deploy is in progress.
- `npm run db:migrate` (`scripts/db-migrate.mjs`) loads `.env` then `.env.local` without overriding the
  shell, creates `schema_migrations (name, applied_at)` if missing, skips names already recorded, and
  runs each new file in its own transaction together with its `schema_migrations` row.
- Migrations 1 and 2 were applied to qavren-db before tracking existed. The first tracked run re-applies
  them (safe: both are idempotent) and records all three.

## Tables (v1)

| Table | Key | Holds |
|---|---|---|
| `users` | `id` (Keycloak sub) | email, display name, owner flag, first/last seen |
| `onboarding_states` | `user_id` | preferred mode, risk acknowledgment, exchange connected |
| `trading_limits` | `user_id` | max position, daily loss cap, trades/day, cooldown, loss streak trigger, per-symbol caps (jsonb), pause flag |
| `exchange_connections` | `user_id` | Coinbase key name, sealed secret (ciphertext/iv/tag), permissions |
| `signals` | `id` | every evaluation with rationale (jsonb) and indicators |
| `paper_trades` | `id` | paper fills and blocked attempts, realized P&L, risk decision (jsonb) |
| `assisted_orders` | `id` (also the exchange client order id) | live/mock orders, exchange ids, fills, fees, reconcile state, risk decision |
| `audit_events` | `id` | every decision and action, by category |
| `export_artifacts` | `id` | CSV exports and where they are stored |
| `schema_migrations` | `name` | applied migration files |

## Local reset

    npm run db:down
    docker volume rm kairis-dev_kairis-dev-db
    npm run db:up && npm run db:migrate
````

- [ ] **Step 8: docs/identity.md (new)**

````markdown
# Identity

Kairis signs users in through qavren-auth (Keycloak). It stores no passwords.

## Realms

| Realm | Used by | Redirect URIs |
|---|---|---|
| `kairis` | production | `https://kairis.qavrensolutions.com/*` |
| `kairis-dev` | localhost | `http://localhost:3000/*` |

Each realm has a public PKCE client `<realm>-web` (no client secret) and the fleet's Google
identity provider. Generate both with `C:\Users\steve\projects\qavren-auth\tools\provision-app.ps1`
and push them to the hosted Keycloak with `infra/update-realms.ps1` (commands in
[deployment.md](deployment.md)).

Sign-up: Keycloak's first-broker-login creates an account for any new Google identity, whatever
`registrationAllowed` says. Email and password sign-up needs `registrationAllowed: true` plus the
`VERIFY_EMAIL` default required action in the realm file (the fairsquare pattern), so nobody can
register an address they do not own. The Kairis sign-in page hands off to the realm's login screen,
which shows "Register" when registration is on.

For a fully local loop, run Keycloak from qavren-auth:

    cd C:\Users\steve\projects\qavren-auth
    docker compose up -d --wait
    pwsh tools/provision-app.ps1 -App kairis-dev -RedirectUris 'http://localhost:3000/*' -Apply

and set `QAVREN_AUTH_URL=http://localhost:8080` in `.env.local`.

## Configuration

| Variable | Value |
|---|---|
| `QAVREN_REALM` | `kairis` (prod) / `kairis-dev` (local) |
| `QAVREN_AUTH_URL` | defaults to `https://auth.qavrensolutions.com` |
| `AUTH_URL` | the public origin (Auth.js callback base) |
| `AUTH_SECRET` | 32 random bytes, base64; rotating it signs everyone out |
| `AUTH_TRUST_HOST` | `true` (behind the tunnel) |

## Code

- `auth.ts`: `createAuth({ realm, pages: { signIn: "/sign-in" }, callbacks })`, wrapping the SDK's
  own `jwt`/`session` callbacks (passing `callbacks` alone would drop its role mapping).
- `proxy.ts`: every `/app/*` request without a session is redirected to
  `/sign-in?callbackUrl=<path>`, built on `APP_BASE_URL` rather than the request's Host header.
- `lib/auth/paths.ts`: only same-origin absolute paths are accepted as `callbackUrl`.
- `lib/server/session.ts`: `currentUser()`, `requireUser()`, `requireOnboarded()` (every `/app`
  page except onboarding), `requireOwner()` (404 for everyone else).

## Session shape

`session.user` carries `id` (the Keycloak `sub`, the key on every Kairis row), `email`, `name` and
`roles` (realm roles, read from the access token by the SDK). Kairis never calls another API with
the access token, so the session holds no tokens and needs no refresh logic.

## Owner gate

A user is an owner when their email is in `KAIRIS_OWNER_EMAILS` (comma list, case-insensitive) or
they hold the realm role `owner`. Owners see Operations and may run the auto cycle (which still
needs `ENABLE_AUTO_MODE` and `ENABLE_LIVE_ASSISTED_TRADING`). Payment tier never grants it.

## Sign-out

"Sign out" clears the Kairis session cookie and returns to `/`. The realm's SSO session survives
until it expires, so signing in again on the same browser may not prompt for a password. On a shared
computer, also sign out at `https://auth.qavrensolutions.com/realms/kairis/account`.
````

- [ ] **Step 9: docs/sdd.md (three replacements)**

Replace this block:

```
## Deployment Assumptions

The initial system design should reflect available infrastructure:

- `Supabase Postgres` is the default managed backend for relational data and operational records
- `Cloudflare R2` is the default object storage layer for exports, generated reports, and file-like artifacts
- local `Proxmox` is the intended host for test environments
- only `AWS EC2` is assumed to be available from AWS, and it is the intended production deployment target
- `GitHub Actions` is the default CI/CD mechanism for validation, packaging, and deployment
- the application runtime should be lightweight enough to run in local Proxmox-based test environments

Design consequence:

- prefer managed components for stateful infrastructure
- keep stateless application services portable
- avoid architecture that depends on heavy always-on self-managed infrastructure in the first release
```

with:

```
## Deployment Assumptions

The system runs on shared Qavren platform services:

- `qavren-db` (shared Supabase Postgres, schema-per-app) holds relational data and operational records in the `kairis` schema; runtime traffic uses the transaction pooler, migrations the session pooler
- `qavren-auth` (Keycloak) owns identity through the `kairis` realm (`kairis-dev` for local development)
- the application is one container image in ECR, deployed over SSM to the shared Qavren hub (`Qavren-Web-Server`) and published through a Cloudflare tunnel at `kairis.qavrensolutions.com`
- `Cloudflare R2` is the object storage layer for exports when configured
- `GitHub Actions` is the CI/CD mechanism; AWS access is OIDC only
- there is no separate test environment; CI (with a Postgres service container) and the local compose stack cover pre-production

Design consequence:

- stateful infrastructure is shared and managed; the app container is stateless apart from an optional local export fallback
- no per-app servers: new hosting goes on the hub
- the runtime must tolerate a transaction pooler (no session state)
```

Replace:

```
- user identity, entitlements, and account metadata should live in managed Postgres-backed data stores unless later requirements force separation
```

with:

```
- user identity lives in the qavren-auth `kairis` realm; Kairis stores account metadata, entitlement flags and the owner flag in qavren-db, keyed by the Keycloak `sub`
```

Replace:

```
- portability between local Proxmox test environments and AWS EC2 production hosting
```

with:

```
- one image runs identically on a developer machine (with the compose Postgres) and on the Qavren hub
```

- [ ] **Step 10: docs/business-plan.md (one replacement)**

Replace the body of `## Infrastructure Strategy` (everything between that heading and `## Revenue Model`) with:

```
Kairis uses the shared Qavren platform instead of per-product infrastructure.

Infrastructure assumptions:

- `qavren-db` (shared Supabase Postgres, schema-per-app) is the relational store
- `qavren-auth` (Keycloak, realm per app) provides sign-up, sign-in and roles
- the app runs as one container on the shared Qavren hub behind a Cloudflare tunnel; new hosting never adds a per-app EC2 box
- `Cloudflare R2` holds exports and other file artifacts
- `GitHub Actions` is the CI/CD layer, with AWS access through OIDC

Business implication:

- near-zero marginal hosting cost per product, because the database, identity and host are shared
- time goes to the product, not to platform work, until product fit is proven
- delivery stays simple and repo-native: a date-stamped release tag deploys production
```

```
- generated exports and file-like artifacts are assumed to live in `Cloudflare R2`
- application services should remain portable between local Proxmox test environments and AWS EC2 production hosting
```

with:

```
- identity is the `kairis` realm on `qavren-auth` (Keycloak); the client receives a session keyed by the Keycloak `sub`
- entitlements, operational records, signals, orders and the audit log live in the `kairis` schema on `qavren-db` (shared Postgres)
- exchange credentials are stored encrypted in `qavren-db`, never in object storage
- generated exports live in `Cloudflare R2` when configured
- the application runs as one container on the shared Qavren hub, reached through a Cloudflare tunnel
```

4. **Limits**: `/app/controls`, set a SOL-USD cap of 200; a $250 SOL-USD paper BUY is blocked by the cap. Pause, confirm the banner and a halted paper order; resume.
5. **Connect Coinbase**: `/app/exchange`. With a real trade-only CDP key the panel shows View yes, Trade yes, Transfer no. With a transfer-capable key the exact rejection message appears and nothing is stored. Without a key, a bogus one returns the plain "Coinbase did not accept this key" message.
6. **Assisted trade**: `/app/trade` -> preview a $20 BUY -> risk checks + preview -> tick the confirmation -> submit. With the mock provider: `submitted`, then "Reconcile now" -> `filled`. With a real key and `ENABLE_LIVE_ASSISTED_TRADING=false`: the row ends `blocked` with "Live assisted trading is disabled by environment policy."
7. `/app/journal` shows each step; `/app/reports` creates three CSVs; `/app/operations` (owner) shows status with "Run auto cycle" disabled.

Stop the dev server and, if you started it, `docker compose down` in qavren-auth.

```bash
## Summary

Takes Kairis from the phase-demo scaffold to the v1 finish line:

1. **Sign up / sign in**: Keycloak realm `kairis` on qavren-auth via `@qavren/auth-next`; `proxy.ts` gates `/app/*`; onboarding requires a risk acknowledgment and starts users in paper mode.
2. **Paper trading**: rule-based signals on Coinbase public data (EMA 9/21 + RSI 14, spread/ATR/staleness gates) with rationale and limit-based sizing; simulated fills, positions, realized P&L and a journal that keeps blocked attempts with their reasons.
3. **Connect Coinbase**: per-user CDP keys checked with `key_permissions`; transfer-capable keys rejected; secrets sealed AES-256-GCM under `KAIRIS_SECRET_KEY`.
4. **Assisted trades with enforced limits**: one risk engine for paper and live (max position, daily loss cap, trades/day, loss-streak cooldown, per-symbol caps, kill switch, stale-data and provider halts). Check -> preview -> explicit confirm -> submit -> reconcile; the row id is the client order id; live submission gated by `ENABLE_LIVE_ASSISTED_TRADING` (off by default).
5. **Prod host**: one image in ECR, SSM deploy to the shared Qavren hub behind a Cloudflare tunnel at `kairis.qavrensolutions.com`, Terraform in `infra/`, migrations tracked in `schema_migrations` and applied by the deploy job. The Proxmox lane, the Supabase CLI and the JSON fallback are gone.

Also: filterable journal, CSV exports (R2 or local), owner operations (status, reconcile, env-gated auto cycle), real lint/test gates, integration tests against Postgres in CI.
```
